}

/* โมดูลที่เปิดใช้ในงานนี้ ปิดแล้วแค่ซ่อนเมนู ไม่ลบข้อมูล เปิดกลับมาข้อมูลอยู่ครบ

 ["stage","Stage"],["exh","Exhibitor"],["tl","Timeline"]];
const FIXED=["dash","set"];
function modOn(id){
  if(FIXED.includes(id))return true;
  const m=EV.modules||{};
  return m[id]!==false;
}
function navTabs(n){
  if(!n.tabs)return null;
  return n.tabs.filter(t=>can(t[2])!=="none");
}
function navOK(n){
  if(!n.tabs)return !n.m||can(n.m)!=="none";
  return navTabs(n).length>0;
}
let tab={};
function curTab(n){
  const ts=navTabs(n); if(!ts)return null;
  if(!tab[n.id]||!ts.some(t=>t[0]===tab[n.id]))tab[n.id]=ts[0][0];
  return tab[n.id];
}

/* หน้าที่มีแท็บจะเขียนลง #tabbody ไม่ใช่ #page ทั้งก้อน
   ไม่งั้นเวลาฟังก์ชันหน้าเรียกตัวเองใหม่หลังผู้ใช้แก้อะไร แถบแท็บจะถูกเขียนทับหายไป */
const $=s=>{
  if(s==="#page"){
    if(PAGEHOST)return PAGEHOST;
    const tb=document.getElementById("tabbody"); if(tb)return tb;
  }
  return document.querySelector(s);
};

/* รูปแทนคนและโลโก้ สร้างจากชื่อ ใช้เป็นตัวยืนจนกว่าจะมีไฟล์จริง */
const LOGO={};                       // ชื่อบริษัท -> dataURI ที่อัปโหลด
const SITE={};                       // ชื่อบริษัท -> เว็บไซต์
function hue(s){let h=0;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++)h=(h*31+s.charCodeAt(i))%360;return h}
function initials(s){
  const t=String(s||"").replace(/^(คุณ|พี่|อ\.|เชฟ|ดร\.|น้า)/,"").trim();
  const w=t.split(/[\s·]+/).filter(Boolean);
  return ((w[0]||"?")[0]+((w[1]||"")[0]||"")).toUpperCase();
}
function avatar(name,px,ring){
  const h=hue(name||"?");
  return '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;'+
   'width:'+px+'px;height:'+px+'px;border-radius:99px;background:hsl('+h+' 42% 46%);color:#fff;'+
   'font-size:'+Math.round(px*.4)+'px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.02em;'+
   (ring?'box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(255,255,255,.55);':'')+'">'+esc(initials(name))+'</span>';
}
function norm(u){
  u=u.trim().replace(/^https?:\/\//i,"").replace(/\/$/,"");
  return "https://"+u;
}
/* ของจริงเซิร์ฟเวอร์จะไปอ่าน og:image หรือ favicon ของเว็บนั้นแล้วเก็บไฟล์ไว้เอง
   ตัวอย่างนี้เรียกข้ามเว็บไม่ได้ จึงวาดตัวยืนจากชื่อโดเมนแทน */
function fakeLogo(url){
  const d=url.replace(/^https?:\/\//,"").replace(/^www\./,"").split("/")[0];
  const nm=d.split(".")[0], h=hue(d);
  const svg='<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="96" height="96">'+
   '<rect width="96" height="96" rx="14" fill="hsl('+h+' 55% 42%)"/>'+
   '<text x="48" y="58" font-size="34" font-family="Helvetica,Arial" font-weight="700" '+
   'fill="#fff" text-anchor="middle">'+nm.slice(0,2).toUpperCase()+'</text></svg>';
  return "data:image/svg+xml;utf8,"+encodeURIComponent(svg);
}
function logoTile(co,px){
  if(LOGO[co])return '<img src="'+LOGO[co]+'" alt="" style="width:'+px+'px;height:'+px+
    'px;border-radius:8px;object-fit:contain;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--line)">';
  const h=hue(co||"?");
  return '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:'+px+
   'px;height:'+px+'px;border-radius:8px;background:hsl('+h+' 30% 92%);color:hsl('+h+' 45% 32%);'+
   'font-size:'+Math.round(px*.36)+'px;font-weight:700;border:1px solid var(--line)">'+esc(initials(co))+'</span>';
}

$("#lform").addEventListener("submit",e=>{

  showPick();
});
/* ───────── สร้างงานใหม่ ─────────
   กรอกข้อมูลตั้งต้นครั้งเดียว แล้วระบบวางโครงให้ทั้งงาน
   ไทม์ไลน์ตั้งช่วงเป็น 12 เดือนก่อนวันงาน ถึง 1 เดือนหลังวันงาน ทุกงานเหมือนกัน */
const TL_BEFORE=12, TL_AFTER=1;
function evRange(dateStr){
  const d=new Date(dateStr+"T00:00:00");
  const mo=k=>{const x=new Date(d.getFullYear(),d.getMonth()+k,1);
    return x.getFullYear()+"-"+String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")};
  return {pre:[mo(-TL_BEFORE),mo(0)], post:[mo(0),mo(TL_AFTER)]};
}
function slugId(name){
  const b=String(name||"").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,"-").replace(/^-|-$/g,"");
  let id=(b||"event").slice(0,24), n=1;
  while(EVENTS.some(e=>e.id===id))id=(b||"event").slice(0,20)+"-"+(++n);
  return id;
}
/* ผังบูธตั้งต้น เรียงเป็นตารางตามจำนวนบูธที่กรอก แบ่งโซนละ 24 บูธ */
function seedBooths(n,price){
  const out=[], perRow=12, Z="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOP";
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const z=Z[Math.floor(i/24)]||"Z", k=i%24+1;
    out.push({code:z+k,name:null,zone:z,x:i%perRow+1,y:Math.floor(i/perRow)+1,w:1,h:1,
      food:false,st:"free",co:null,sales:null,pkg:null,product:null,contact:null,
      phone:null,email:null,form:false,board:false,list:price});
  }
  return out;
}
function seedZoneNames(n){
  const o={}, Z="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOP";
  for(let i=0;i<Math.ceil(n/24);i++)o[Z[i]]="Zone "+Z[i];
  return o;
}
const cloneJ=o=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));

function newEventFrom(f){
  const id=slugId(f.short||f.name);
  const tpl=D.timelineTemplate||[];
  const ev={
    id, name:f.name, short:f.short||f.name, venue:f.venue,
    dates:fmtRange(f.d1,f.d2), event_date:f.d1, eventDate:f.d1, end_date:f.d2,
    status:"planning", brands:f.brands, logo:f.logo||null,
    owner:f.owner, client:f.owner==="client"?f.client:null,
    sqm:f.sqm||null, boothCount:f.n||0,
    booths:seedBooths(f.n||0,f.price||0), areas:[], deals:[], sessions:[],
    sales:[], target:0, target_note:"No target set yet",
    budget:cloneJ(D.template||[]), catTotals:{},
    tasks:cloneJ((EVENTS[0]&&EVENTS[0].tasks)||[]),
    packages:f.copyPrice?cloneJ(EVENTS[0].packages||{}):{},
    addons:f.copyPrice?cloneJ(EVENTS[0].addons||[]):[],
    zoneNames:seedZoneNames(f.n||0),
    manual:{name:"Exhibitor Manual "+(f.short||f.name)+".pdf",url:null},
    timeline:cloneJ(tpl),
    tlRange:evRange(f.d1), tlCols:[],
    buildDays:1, strikeDays:1,
  };
  /* งบตั้งต้นเอาโครงหมวดมาให้ครบ แต่ยังไม่ใส่ตัวเลข ทีมกรอกเอง */
  ev.budget.forEach(l=>{ l.fc=0; l.ac=0; l.st="planned"; l.sup=null; l.phone=null });
  (ev.budget||[]).forEach(l=>{ ev.catTotals[l.cat]=ev.catTotals[l.cat]||0 });
  return ev;
}

let nfLogo=null;

      ' months after the show</b> the same for every event</li>'+

      if(clone&&from){

          budget:(from.budget||[]).length+" budget lines",
          tasks:(from.tasks||[]).length+" checklist items",
          tl:(from.timeline||[]).length+" timeline tasks"};
        L.push("<b>Cloning from "+esc(from.short)+"</b>");
        parts.forEach(k=>L.push("Copy "+N[k]));
        if(!parts.length)L.push("Nothing selected to copy — the event will start empty");
        L.push("<i>Clients, deals, money figures and the stage schedule are not copied</i>");
      }else{
        L.push("<b>Starting from scratch</b>");
        L.push("Standard timeline of "+(D.timelineTemplate||[]).length+" tasks");
        L.push("Standard chart of accounts, "+(D.template||[]).length+" lines, all figures zero");
        L.push((+v("nf_n")||0)+" booths laid out as a grid");
      }
      L.push("Modules on: "+(mods.length?mods.map(k=>(MODULES.find(m=>m[0]===k)||[,k])[1]).join(", "):"none"));
      P.querySelector("#nf_sum").innerHTML='<b>Check before creating</b><ul>'+
        L.map(x=>'<li>'+x+'</li>').join("")+'</ul>';
      P.querySelector("#nf_sum").hidden=false;
      P.querySelector("#nf_go").textContent="Create event";
      confirmed=true;
      P.querySelector("#nf_sum").scrollIntoView({block:"nearest"});
      return;
    }

    const ev=newEventFrom({
      name:v("nf_name").trim(), short:v("nf_short").trim(), venue:v("nf_venue").trim(),
      brands:brands.length?brands:[v("nf_short").trim()||v("nf_name").trim()],
      d1, d2, owner:own, client:v("nf_client").trim(),
      n:+v("nf_n")||0, sqm:+v("nf_sqm")||null, price:+v("nf_price")||0,
      logo:nfLogo, copyPrice:false });
    if(clone&&from)cloneInto(ev,from,parts);
    ev.modules=Object.fromEntries(MODULES.map(([k])=>[k,mods.includes(k)]));
    EVENTS.push(ev);
    enter(ev.id); go(mods.includes("tl")?"tl":"dash");
  });
}
/* คัดลอกเฉพาะโครง ไม่เอาลูกค้า ดีล และตัวเลขเงิน */
function cloneInto(ev,from,parts){
  const J=o=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
  if(parts.includes("plan")){
    ev.booths=J(from.booths||[]).map(b=>Object.assign({},b,{
      name:null,st:"free",co:null,sales:null,product:null,
      contact:null,phone:null,email:null,form:false,board:false}));
    ev.zoneNames=J(from.zoneNames||{});
    ev.areas=J(from.areas||[]);
    ev.boothCount=ev.booths.length;
  }
  if(parts.includes("pkg")){ ev.packages=J(from.packages||{}); ev.addons=J(from.addons||[]) }
  if(parts.includes("budget")){
    ev.budget=J(from.budget||[]).map(l=>Object.assign({},l,{fc:0,ac:0,st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null}));
    ev.catTotals={};
  }
  if(parts.includes("tasks"))ev.tasks=J(from.tasks||[]);
  if(parts.includes("tl")){
    ev.timeline=J(from.timeline||[]).map(t=>{
      const c=Object.assign({},t,{st:"",note:t.note||""});
      delete c.aa; delete c.ab; delete c.at1; delete c.at2; return c});
  }
}
const fld=(l,ctl)=>'<label class="nff"><span>'+l+'</span>'+ctl+'</label>';
/* ย่อรูปก่อนเก็บ ไฟล์ใหญ่จะทำให้หน้าหนักเกินไป */
function shrinkImg(file,max,cb){
  const fr=new FileReader();
  fr.onload=()=>{ const im=new Image();
    im.onload=()=>{ const s=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(im.width,im.height));
      const c=document.createElement("canvas");
      c.width=Math.round(im.width*s); c.height=Math.round(im.height*s);
      c.getContext("2d").drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);
      try{ cb(c.toDataURL("image/png")) }catch(err){ cb(fr.result) } };
    im.onerror=()=>cb(fr.result);
    im.src=fr.result };
  fr.readAsDataURL(file);
}
/* เปลี่ยนโลโก้ของงานที่มีอยู่แล้ว จากการ์ดในหน้าเลือกงาน */
function pickLogo(id){
  const inp=document.createElement("input");
  inp.type="file"; inp.accept="image/*";
  inp.onchange=()=>{ const f=inp.files&&inp.files[0]; if(!f)return;
    shrinkImg(f,320,url=>{ const e=EVENTS.find(x=>x.id===id); if(e){e.logo=url; showPick()} }) };
  inp.click();
}

    b.addEventListener("click",e=>{e.stopPropagation();pickLogo(b.dataset.lg)}));

}
function enter(id){
  useEvent(id);
  $("#login").style.display="none";
  $("#pick").classList.remove("on");
  $("#app").classList.add("on");

    esc(e.short)+'</option>').join("");
  showEvLogo();
  UNDO.list.length=0; UNDO.at=-1;
  go("dash");
  watchPage(); snapPush();
}

  ["dealpanel","costpanel","exhpanel"].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.remove()});
  draw()}
/* ───────── ถอยหลัง เดินหน้า ─────────
   เก็บภาพนิ่งของงานที่เปิดอยู่หลังการวาดหน้าทุกครั้ง ถ้าต่างจากอันล่าสุดถึงจะเก็บ
   วิธีนี้ครอบคลุมทุกการแก้โดยไม่ต้องไปแตะโค้ดทุกจุดที่แก้ข้อมูล
   ไม่เก็บโลโก้กับไฟล์ผัง เพราะเป็น data URI ก้อนใหญ่และแทบไม่เปลี่ยน */
const HEAVY=["logo","planFile"];
const UNDO={ list:[], at:-1, busy:false, max:40, unlock:null };
function snapNow(){
  const o={}; for(const k in EV) if(!HEAVY.includes(k)) o[k]=EV[k];
  return JSON.stringify(o);
}
/* หน้าส่วนใหญ่แก้ข้อมูลแล้วเรียกฟังก์ชันของหน้าตัวเองใหม่ ไม่ได้เรียก draw()
   เลยต้องดักที่การเปลี่ยนแปลงของ DOM แทน จะได้ครอบคลุมทุกการแก้โดยไม่ต้องไล่แก้ทุกจุด */
let snapT=null;
function snapSoon(){ clearTimeout(snapT);
  snapT=setTimeout(()=>{ snapPush(); saveSoon() },260) }
let snapWatch=null;
function watchPage(){
  if(snapWatch)return;
  const el=document.getElementById("page"); if(!el)return;
  snapWatch=new MutationObserver(()=>{ if(!UNDO.busy)snapSoon() });
  snapWatch.observe(el,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});
}
function snapPush(){
  if(UNDO.busy||!EV)return;
  const js=snapNow();
  if(UNDO.at>=0&&UNDO.list[UNDO.at]&&UNDO.list[UNDO.at].js===js)return;
  UNDO.list.length=UNDO.at+1;               // แก้ใหม่หลังถอยแล้ว ตัดทางเดินหน้าทิ้ง
  UNDO.list.push({ev:EV.id,js});
  if(UNDO.list.length>UNDO.max)UNDO.list.shift();
  UNDO.at=UNDO.list.length-1;
  paintUndo();
}
function snapApply(n){
  const s=UNDO.list[n]; if(!s)return;
  if(s.ev!==EV.id)useEvent(s.ev);
  const o=JSON.parse(s.js);
  for(const k in o)EV[k]=o[k];
  useEvent(EV.id);                          // ผูก B AR DEALS SES เข้ากับชุดใหม่
  UNDO.at=n; UNDO.busy=true;
  sel=null; dealOpen=null; costOpen=null; exhOpen=null;
  ["dealpanel","costpanel","exhpanel"].forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.remove()});
  draw();
  /* ตัวดัก DOM ทำงานทีหลัง ถ้าปลดล็อกทันทีมันจะเก็บภาพของสถานะที่เพิ่งย้อนมา
     แล้วทางเดินหน้าจะหายไป จึงต้องกดไว้ให้นานกว่าจังหวะหน่วงของตัวดัก */
  clearTimeout(snapT);
  clearTimeout(UNDO.unlock);
  UNDO.unlock=setTimeout(()=>{ UNDO.busy=false; clearTimeout(snapT) },420);
  paintUndo();
}
function undoStep(){ if(UNDO.at>0){ snapApply(UNDO.at-1); toastTL("Undone") } }
function redoStep(){ if(UNDO.at<UNDO.list.length-1){ snapApply(UNDO.at+1); toastTL("Redone") } }
const isMac=/Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform||navigator.userAgent);
const KZ=isMac?"⌘Z":"Ctrl+Z", KY=isMac?"⇧⌘Z":"Ctrl+Shift+Z";
function paintUndo(){
  const b=document.getElementById("ubk"), f=document.getElementById("ufw");
  if(!b||!f)return;
  const back=Math.max(0,UNDO.at), fwd=Math.max(0,UNDO.list.length-1-UNDO.at);
  b.disabled=UNDO.at<=0;
  f.disabled=UNDO.at>=UNDO.list.length-1;
  b.title=b.disabled?"Nothing to undo":"Undo · "+KZ+" · "+back+" step"+(back===1?"":"s")+" back";
  f.title=f.disabled?"Nothing to redo":"Redo · "+KY+" · "+fwd+" step"+(fwd===1?"":"s")+" forward";
}
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
  if(e.key==="Escape"&&tlPick){ tlPick=null; if(tlRedraw)tlRedraw(); return }
  if(!(e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey))return;
  const a=document.activeElement;
  /* อยู่ในช่องพิมพ์ ปล่อยให้เบราว์เซอร์ถอยตัวหนังสือเองตามปกติ */
  if(a&&(a.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(a.tagName)))return;
  const k=e.key.toLowerCase();
  if(k==="z"&&!e.shiftKey){ e.preventDefault(); undoStep() }
  else if((k==="z"&&e.shiftKey)||k==="y"){ e.preventDefault(); redoStep() }
});
/* ---------- บันทึกอัตโนมัติ ----------
   เก็บลง localStorage ของเบราว์เซอร์ ทุกครั้งที่มีการแก้ ไม่ต้องกดบันทึก
   ทำให้รีเฟรชหน้า ปิดแท็บ หรืออัปหน้าเว็บเวอร์ชันใหม่ แล้วงานที่แก้ไว้ยังอยู่
   ไม่เก็บโลโก้กับไฟล์ผัง เป็น data URI ก้อนใหญ่และฝังกลับมาให้ตอน build อยู่แล้ว */
const SKEY="pxe.autosave.v2";
let saveT=null, savedJS="", saveOn=true, saveAt=0;
function saveBody(){
  return JSON.stringify({v:2,
    events:EVENTS.map(e=>{const o={}; for(const k in e)if(!HEAVY.includes(k))o[k]=e[k]; return o}),
    reps:REPS});
}
function saveNow(){
  if(!saveOn)return;
  let js; try{ js=saveBody() }catch(err){ return }
  if(js===savedJS)return;
  try{ localStorage.setItem(SKEY,js); savedJS=js; saveAt=Date.now(); paintSaved("ok") }
  catch(err){ saveOn=false; paintSaved("full") }
}
function saveSoon(){ clearTimeout(saveT); saveT=setTimeout(saveNow,600) }
function saveClear(){
  try{ localStorage.removeItem(SKEY) }catch(err){}
  savedJS=""; location.reload();
}
function saveWhen(){
  if(!saveAt)return "";
  const s=Math.round((Date.now()-saveAt)/1000);
  return s<8?"just now":s<60?s+"s ago":Math.round(s/60)+" min ago";
}
function paintSaved(kind){
  const el=document.getElementById("svst"); if(!el)return;
  if(kind==="full"){ el.textContent="Not saved — browser storage is full";
    el.className="svst bad"; el.title="Clear some space, or export a backup from Settings"; return }
  el.textContent="Saved "+saveWhen();
  el.className="svst on";
  el.title="Every change is kept in this browser. Reset it from Settings › Modules.";
  clearTimeout(paintSaved.t);
  paintSaved.t=setTimeout(()=>{ const e=document.getElementById("svst");
    if(e&&e.classList.contains("on"))e.textContent="Saved "+saveWhen() },20000);
}
/* คืนค่าที่บันทึกไว้ทับข้อมูลที่ฝังมากับหน้า ทำครั้งเดียวตอนโหลด */
function loadSaved(){
  let raw=null; try{ raw=localStorage.getItem(SKEY) }catch(err){ return }
  if(!raw)return;
  let o=null; try{ o=JSON.parse(raw) }catch(err){ return }
  if(!o||o.v!==2||!Array.isArray(o.events))return;
  o.events.forEach(sv=>{
    const e=EVENTS.find(x=>x.id===sv.id); if(!e)return;
    for(const k in sv)if(!HEAVY.includes(k))e[k]=sv[k];
  });
  if(Array.isArray(o.reps)){ REPS.length=0; o.reps.forEach(r=>REPS.push(r)) }
  /* ผูกตัวแปรลัดเข้ากับอาร์เรย์ชุดใหม่ ตอนนี้ยังเรียก useEvent ไม่ได้
     เพราะมันไปแตะ stg ที่ประกาศทีหลังในไฟล์ */
  B=EV.booths; AR=EV.areas; DEALS=EV.deals; SES=EV.sessions;
  savedJS=saveBody(); saveAt=Date.now();
}
loadSaved();

function draw(){
  $("#rl2").value=role; $("#wn").textContent=me; $("#wr").textContent=ROLE[role].n;
  const vis=NAV.filter(n=>navOK(n)&&modOn(n.id));
  if(!vis.some(n=>n.id===page))page=vis[0].id;
  $("#nav").innerHTML=vis.map(n=>{
    const c=n.id==="sales"?DEALS.filter(mine).length
      :n.id==="plan"?B.filter(x=>x.st==="free").length:"";
    return '<a class="'+(page===n.id?"on":"")+'" data-p="'+n.id+'">'+svg(n.id)+
      '<span class="lb">'+n.t+'</span>'+(c!==""?'<span class="cnt">'+c+'</span>':'')+'</a>';
  }).join("");
  $("#nav").querySelectorAll("a").forEach(a=>a.addEventListener("click",()=>go(a.dataset.p)));
  const n=NAV.find(x=>x.id===page);
  $("#ttl").textContent=n.t;
  const ts=navTabs(n), t=curTab(n);
  const host=document.getElementById("page");
  host.innerHTML = ts
    ? '<div class="tabs">'+ts.map(x=>'<button data-tb="'+x[0]+'" aria-pressed="'+
        (t===x[0])+'">'+x[1]+'</button>').join("")+'</div><div id="tabbody"></div>'
    : '<div id="tabbody"></div>';
  if(ts)host.querySelectorAll("[data-tb]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    tab[n.id]=b.dataset.tb; draw();
  }));
  // ให้ฟังก์ชันเดิมเขียนลง #tabbody โดยไม่ต้องแก้ทุกอัน
  if(page!=="tl"){ tlCtx=null; tlSel.clear() }
  const real=document.getElementById("tabbody");
  const proxy={set innerHTML(v){real.innerHTML=v},get innerHTML(){return real.innerHTML},
    querySelectorAll:(q)=>real.querySelectorAll(q),querySelector:(q)=>real.querySelector(q)};
  PAGEHOST=proxy;
  (PAGE[ts?page+":"+t:page]||pDash)();
  PAGEHOST=null;
  snapSoon();
}
let PAGEHOST=null;
function deny(t){return '<div class="deny"><h3>Role '+ROLE[role].n+' No access '+esc(t)+'</h3>'+
 '<p>This menu is hidden in the sidebar for this role</p></div>'}

/* ---------- ภาพรวม ---------- */
function money(){
  let list=0,con=0,got=0;
  B.forEach(b=>{list+=b.list||0;
    if(b.st!=="free"){con+=b.list||0;got+=(b.list||0)*(b.st==="paid"?1:b.st==="deposit"?dep:0)}});
  return {list,con,got,out:con-got};
}
/* ───────── สิ่งที่ต้องทำต่อ ─────────
   รวมสัญญาณที่กระจายอยู่ตามหน้าต่าง ๆ มาไว้ที่เดียว
   ทุกบรรทัดกดแล้วเด้งไปหน้าที่เกี่ยวข้องทันที เห็นเฉพาะโมดูลที่เปิดและมีสิทธิ์ดู */
function actionItems(){
  const out=[];
  const push=(o)=>{ if(o.n>0)out.push(o) };
  if(modOn("plan")&&can("floorplan")!=="none"){
    const ov=overdueBooths(), wn=warnBooths();
    push({n:ov.length,k:"risk",t:"Booths past the deposit deadline",
      s:ov.slice(0,5).map(b=>b.code).join(", ")+(ov.length>5?" and more":""),
      go:"plan",tab:"map",btn:"Open floor plan"});
    push({n:wn.length,k:"warn",t:"Booths due within 7 days",
      s:wn.slice(0,5).map(b=>b.code).join(", ")+(wn.length>5?" and more":""),
      go:"plan",tab:"map",btn:"Open floor plan"});
  }
  if(modOn("tl")&&can("timeline")!=="none"){
    const now=tlNowWeek();
    const late=(EV.timeline||[]).filter(r=>(r.ph||"pre")!=="on"&&r.st!=="done"&&pB(r)<now);
    const slip=(EV.timeline||[]).filter(r=>slip_(r)>0);
    push({n:late.length,k:"risk",t:"Timeline tasks past their due week",
      s:late.slice(0,3).map(r=>r.name).join(" · "),go:"tl",tab:"pre",btn:"Open timeline"});
    push({n:slip.length,k:"warn",t:"Timeline tasks running behind plan",
      s:slip.slice(0,3).map(r=>r.name).join(" · "),go:"tl",tab:"pre",btn:"Open timeline"});
  }
  if(modOn("exh")&&can("exhibitor")!=="none"){
    const req=(EV.tasks||[]).filter(t=>t.req&&t.by==="exhibitor");
    const behind=DEALS.filter(d=>d.stage!=="lost"&&req.some(t=>!(d.tasks||{})[t.code]));
    push({n:behind.length,k:"warn",t:"Exhibitors with required items still missing",
      s:behind.slice(0,3).map(d=>d.co).join(" · "),go:"exh",tab:"list",btn:"Open exhibitor list"});
  }
  if(modOn("sales")&&can("deal")!=="none"){
    const list=DEALS.filter(mine), open=list.filter(d=>!["paid","lost"].includes(d.stage));
    const od=open.filter(overdue), st=list.filter(isStale);
    push({n:od.length,k:"risk",t:"Deals past their agreed next step",
      s:od.slice(0,3).map(d=>d.co).join(" · "),go:"sales",tab:"mon",btn:"Open sales"});
    push({n:st.length,k:"warn",t:"Deals with no contact logged in the last 14 days",
      s:st.length===list.length
        ? "every deal — the old sheet never recorded contact history, so nothing has been logged yet"
        : st.slice(0,3).map(d=>d.co).join(" · "),
      go:"sales",tab:"mon",btn:"Open sales"});
  }
  return out.sort((a,b)=>(a.k==="risk"?0:1)-(b.k==="risk"?0:1)||b.n-a.n);
}
const slip_=(r)=>hasAct(r)?aB(r)-pB(r):null;

function actionCard(){
  const L=actionItems();
  if(!L.length)return '<div class="w actdone"><div class="actok">✓</div>'+
    '<div><b>Everything is up to date</b><span>No overdue booths, no late timeline tasks, '+
    'no missing exhibitor items and no deals waiting on us</span></div></div>';
  const risk=L.filter(x=>x.k==="risk").reduce((a,x)=>a+x.n,0);
  return '<div class="w actw"><div class="acth"><b>Needs attention</b>'+
    '<span>'+L.length+' thing'+(L.length===1?"":"s")+' to deal with'+
    (risk?' · '+f(risk)+' already overdue':'')+'</span></div>'+
    L.map((x,i)=>'<button class="actrow '+x.k+'" data-act="'+i+'">'+
      '<span class="actn">'+f(x.n)+'</span>'+
      '<span class="actt"><b>'+esc(x.t)+'</b>'+(x.s?'<small>'+esc(x.s)+'</small>':'')+'</span>'+
      '<span class="actgo">'+esc(x.btn)+' →</span></button>').join("")+'</div>';
}
function wireAction(P){
  const L=actionItems();
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-act]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const x=L[+b.dataset.act]; if(!x)return;
    if(x.tab)tab[x.go]=x.tab;
    go(x.go);
  }));
}
function pDash(){
  const seeMoney=can("budget")!=="none";
  const ACT=actionCard();
  const m=money(), held=B.filter(x=>x.st!=="free");
  const qn=Object.values(Q).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.length,0);
  const real=SES.filter(s=>["talk","keynote","panel","workshop"].includes(s.kind));
  const noSpk=real.filter(s=>!s.people.length).length;
  const noPhone=SES.reduce((n,s)=>n+s.people.filter(p=>!p.contact).length,0);
  const openTasks=DEALS.reduce((n,d)=>n+Object.values(d.tasks).filter(v=>!v).length,0);
  const goal=EV.target!=null?EV.target:Math.round(m.list*.64);

  // สถานะบูธ ใช้เป็นทั้งโดนัทและกรวย
  const SEG=[["paid","Paid in full","--s-paid"],["deposit","Deposit paid","--s-deposit"],
             ["booked","Booked","--s-booked"],["free","Free","--s-free"]];
  const segs=SEG.map(([k,l,c])=>({l,c:cv(c),n:B.filter(b=>b.st===k).length}));

  let h=ACT+'<div class="wg">';

  // โดนัทสถานะบูธ
  h+=w('Booths by status','w4',
    '<div class="donutwrap">'+donut(segs,116)+
    '<ul class="klist" style="flex:1;min-width:120px">'+segs.map(s=>
      '<li><span class="sw" style="background:'+s.c+'"></span>'+s.l+
      '<span class="n">'+s.n+'</span></li>').join("")+'</ul></div>');

  // ตัวเลขใหญ่
  h+=w(seeMoney?'Won':'Sold','w4',
    seeMoney
     ?'<div class="big">'+f(m.con)+'</div><p class="sub" style="margin:6px 0 0">THB at list price · '+
      held.length+' booths</p>'
     :'<div class="big">'+held.length+'</div><p class="sub" style="margin:6px 0 0">booths of '+
      B.length+' Field</p>');

  // เป้า
  if(seeMoney){
    const p1=Math.min(100,m.got/goal*100), p2=Math.min(100,m.con/goal*100);
    h+=w('Against revenue target','w4',
      '<div class="goalrow"><span class="big ok">'+f(m.con)+'</span>'+
      '<span class="of">Target '+f(goal)+'</span></div>'+
      '<div class="pbar"><i style="width:'+p1+'%;background:'+cv("--s-paid")+'"></i>'+
      '<i style="width:'+Math.max(0,p2-p1)+'%;background:'+cv("--s-deposit")+'"></i></div>'+
      '<ul class="klist" style="margin-top:11px">'+
      '<li><span class="sw" style="background:'+cv("--s-paid")+'"></span>Payment received'+
        '<span class="n">'+f(m.got)+'</span></li>'+
      '<li><span class="sw" style="background:'+cv("--s-deposit")+'"></span>Won but not fully paid'+
        '<span class="n">'+f(m.con-m.got)+'</span></li>'+
      '<li><span class="sw" style="background:'+cv("--line")+'"></span>Short by'+
        '<span class="n">'+f(Math.max(0,goal-m.con))+'</span></li></ul>');
  }else{
    h+=w('Booths needing deposit follow-up','w4',
      '<div class="big" style="color:'+cv("--risk")+'">'+
      (warnBooths().length+overdueBooths().length)+'</div>'+
      '<p class="sub" style="margin:6px 0 0">'+(overdueBooths().length
        ?overdueBooths().length+' Booths past due':'No booth is overdue')+'</p>');
  }

  // กรวยการขาย
  // แต่ละขั้นนับสะสม บูธที่จ่ายครบก็ผ่านขั้นมัดจำมาแล้ว ตัวเลขจึงลดหลั่นเสมอ
  const FUN=[["Whole plan",B.length],
             ["Held",held.length],
             ["Deposit paid",B.filter(b=>["deposit","paid"].includes(b.st)).length],
             ["Paid in full",B.filter(b=>b.st==="paid").length]];
  const conv=FUN[0][1]?Math.round(FUN[FUN.length-1][1]/FUN[0][1]*100):0;
  h+=w('Booth sales funnel','w6',funnel(FUN,conv));

  // บูธรายเซลล์ แท่งซ้อน
  const reps=[...new Set(DEALS.map(d=>d.sales||"—"))]
    .map(n=>({n,segs:["booked","deposit","paid"].map(k=>
      DEALS.filter(d=>(d.sales||"—")===n&&d.st===k).reduce((s,d)=>s+d.booths.length,0))}))
    .filter(r=>r.segs.some(x=>x))
    .sort((a,b)=>b.segs.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)-a.segs.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)).slice(0,7);
  h+=w('Booths sold, by rep','w6',
    stacked(reps,[cv("--s-booked"),cv("--s-deposit"),cv("--s-paid")])+
    '<ul class="klist" style="margin-top:12px;flex-direction:row;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">'+
    [["Booked","--s-booked"],["Deposit paid","--s-deposit"],["Paid in full","--s-paid"]].map(x=>
      '<li style="flex:0 0 auto"><span class="sw" style="background:'+cv(x[1])+'"></span>'+x[0]+'</li>').join("")+
    '</ul>');

  // ต้องตามงาน
  h+=w('Needs chasing','w12',
    '<div class="tw" style="border:0;box-shadow:none"><table style="min-width:520px"><tbody>'+
    row("Booths still free",B.filter(x=>x.st==="free").length,"less than a year of selling time left")+
    row("Booths needing deposit follow-up",warnBooths().length+overdueBooths().length,
      "due within 7 days, or already overdue")+
    (seeMoney?row("Outstanding (THB)",f(m.con-m.got),"Deposit received but not complete"):"")+
    row("Stage slots with no speaker",noSpk,"can we sell more stage time, or do we need speakers fast")+
    row("Speakers with no phone on file",noPhone,"if nobody shows up on the day, there is no number to call")+
    row("Open items with exhibitors",openTasks,"logos, documents and badges still outstanding")+
    '</tbody></table></div>');

  h+='</div>';
  if(!seeMoney)h+='<div class="note" style="margin-top:16px"><p>Role '+ROLE[role].n+
    ' cannot see money figures, so the revenue widget is replaced by a booth count</p></div>';
  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  wireAction($("#page"));
}
const w=(title,cls,body)=>'<section class="w '+cls+'"><h3>'+title+'</h3><div class="body">'+body+'</div></section>';

/* โดนัท วาดด้วย stroke-dasharray ไม่ต้องคำนวณ path arc */
function donut(segs,size){
  const tot=segs.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.n,0)||1, r=size/2-11, C=2*Math.PI*r;
  let off=0;
  const rings=segs.map(s=>{
    const len=s.n/tot*C;
    const el='<circle cx="'+size/2+'" cy="'+size/2+'" r="'+r+'" fill="none" stroke="'+s.c+
      '" stroke-width="20" stroke-dasharray="'+len+' '+(C-len)+'" stroke-dashoffset="'+(-off)+
      '" transform="rotate(-90 '+size/2+' '+size/2+')"></circle>';
    off+=len; return el;
  }).join("");
  return '<svg width="'+size+'" height="'+size+'" viewBox="0 0 '+size+' '+size+'" role="img">'+
   rings+'<text x="'+size/2+'" y="'+(size/2+6)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="19" font-weight="700" '+
   'fill="'+cv("--ink")+'">'+tot+'</text></svg>';
}
/* กรวย แท่งลดหลั่นพร้อมอัตราแปลง */
function funnel(rows,conv){
  const W=460,H=190,pad=26,max=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r[1]))||1;
  const bw=(W-pad*2)/rows.length;
  let bars="";
  rows.forEach((r,i)=>{
    const hgt=(r[1]/max)*(H-58);
    const x=pad+i*bw, y=H-38-hgt;
    bars+='<rect x="'+(x+7)+'" y="'+y+'" width="'+(bw-14)+'" height="'+hgt+'" rx="4" fill="'+cv("--accent")+'"/>'+
     '<text x="'+(x+bw/2)+'" y="'+(y-6)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="'+cv("--ink")+'">'+r[1]+'</text>'+
     '<text x="'+(x+bw/2)+'" y="'+(H-14)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" fill="'+cv("--ink2")+'">'+r[0]+'</text>';
  });
  return '<div style="display:flex;gap:16px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">'+
   '<svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="flex:1;min-width:280px;height:auto" role="img">'+bars+'</svg>'+
   '<div class="conv">'+conv+'%<small>Sold and paid in full</small></div></div>';
}
/* แท่งซ้อนรายเซลล์ */
function stacked(reps,colors){
  if(!reps.length)return '<p class="sub">No data</p>';
  const W=460,H=170,pad=20;
  const max=Math.max(...reps.map(r=>r.segs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)))||1;
  const bw=(W-pad*2)/reps.length;
  let bars="";
  reps.forEach((r,i)=>{
    let y=H-24;
    r.segs.forEach((v,j)=>{
      if(!v)return;
      const hgt=v/max*(H-46);
      y-=hgt;
      bars+='<rect x="'+(pad+i*bw+9)+'" y="'+y+'" width="'+(bw-18)+'" height="'+hgt+'" fill="'+colors[j]+'"/>';
    });
    const tot=r.segs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    bars+='<text x="'+(pad+i*bw+bw/2)+'" y="'+(y-6)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11.5" '+
      'font-weight="700" fill="'+cv("--ink")+'">'+tot+'</text>';
  });
  return '<svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" style="width:100%;height:auto" role="img">'+bars+'</svg>'+
   '<div class="repaxis">'+reps.map(r=>'<span>'+avatar(r.n,26)+esc(r.n.slice(0,9))+'</span>').join("")+'</div>';
}
const card=(v,l,s,c)=>{if(c===undefined){c=s;s=null}
  return '<div class="c" style="--t:'+cv(c)+'"><b>'+v+'</b><span>'+l+'</span>'+(s?'<small>'+s+'</small>':'')+'</div>'};
const row=(a,b,c)=>'<tr><td>'+a+'</td><td class="num"><b>'+b+'</b></td><td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+c+'</td></tr>';


/* ---------- กำหนดชำระมัดจำ ----------
   เซลล์เลือกได้ 30 / 45 / 60 วันตอนจอง
   เหลือ 7 วันขึ้นแดงเตือนให้ตาม เลยกำหนดแล้วปล่อยคิวถัดไปขึ้นมา */
const HOLD_OPTS=[30,45,60];
const WARN_DAYS=7;
function addDays(n){const d=new Date();d.setHours(0,0,0,0);d.setDate(d.getDate()+n);return ymd(d)}
function holdLeft(b){ return b.due?-daysAgo(b.due):null }   // บวก = เหลือกี่วัน
function holdState(b){
  if(b.st!=="booked")return null;                 // มัดจำแล้วไม่ต้องนับ
  if(!b.due)return {k:"none",t:"No deposit deadline set",c:"--warn"};
  const n=holdLeft(b);
  if(n<0)return {k:"over",t:"Deposit overdue "+(-n)+" day",c:"--risk"};
  if(n<=WARN_DAYS)return {k:"warn",t:"remaining "+n+" Deposit due",c:"--risk"};
  return {k:"ok",t:"remaining "+n+" day",c:"--ink2"};
}
function overdueBooths(){ return B.filter(b=>{const s=holdState(b);return s&&s.k==="over"}) }
function warnBooths(){ return B.filter(b=>{const s=holdState(b);return s&&(s.k==="warn"||s.k==="none")}) }
function releaseExpired(){
  let n=0;
  overdueBooths().forEach(b=>{
    const qs=Q[b.code]||[];
    if(qs.length){const x=qs.shift(); b.co=x.co; b.sales=x.sales; b.due=addDays(30); b.hold=30;
      if(!qs.length)delete Q[b.code];}
    else {b.st="free"; b.co=null; b.sales=null; b.due=null; b.hold=null;}
    n++;
  });
  return n;
}


/* เงื่อนไขแพ็กเกจ ให้เซลล์ตอบลูกค้าได้ทันทีโดยไม่ต้องเปิดชีต */
const KIND={included:["Already included","--s-paid"],limit:["Quota","--s-deposit"],
 restriction:["Not allowed","--risk"],optional:["Add more","--warn"]};
function canPrice(){ const l=ROLE[role].p.price; return l==="write"||l==="approve" }
function pkgHTML(name,pk){
  return '<div style="margin-top:14px;padding-top:12px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)">'+
   '<b style="font-size:13px">Package terms '+esc(name)+'</b>'+
   '<div style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink2);margin:4px 0 9px">'+
     esc(pk.size)+' · '+esc(pk.build)+' · list price '+f(pk.price)+'</div>'+
   '<ul class="ql" style="border:0;padding:0;margin:0">'+pk.b.map(x=>
     '<li style="align-items:flex-start"><span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(KIND[x[0]][1])+
     ';font-size:11px;flex:0 0 auto">'+KIND[x[0]][0]+'</span>'+
     '<span style="font-size:13px">'+esc(x[1])+'</span></li>').join("")+'</ul></div>';
}

/* ---------- ผังบูธ ---------- */
function shown(){
  const s=q.trim().toLowerCase();
  return B.filter(b=>(!s||b.code.toLowerCase().includes(s)||(b.co||"").toLowerCase().includes(s))
    &&(!fZone||b.zone===fZone)&&(!fPkg||b.pkg===fPkg));
}
/* ───────── ไฟล์ผังพื้นที่ ─────────
   รับได้ทั้งไฟล์ภาพ PDF และไฟล์จาก CAD
   ไฟล์ภาพเอามาวางเป็นพื้นหลังใต้ผังบูธได้เลย จะได้วางบูธทับของจริง
   PDF กับ CAD เก็บไว้ให้ทีมโหลดต่อ พร้อมบอกวิธีทำให้ใช้เป็นพื้นหลังได้ */
const PLAN_MAX=12*1024*1024;
const IMG_EXT=/\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|svg)$/i;
const isPlanImg=f=>f&&(IMG_EXT.test(f.name||"")||/^image\//.test(f.type||""));
function planFile(){ return EV.planFile||null }
function fsize(n){ return n>=1048576?(n/1048576).toFixed(1)+" MB":Math.round(n/1024)+" KB" }
function planBar(wr){
  const f=planFile();
  if(!f)return wr?'<div class="pfbar"><span class="pfl">Floor plan file</span>'+
    '<span class="pfs">Nothing uploaded yet — accepts images, PDF and CAD files (DWG DXF)</span>'+
    '<label class="pfup">Upload floor plan file<input type="file" id="pfin" '+
    'accept=".pdf,.dwg,.dxf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.svg,image/*,application/pdf"></label></div>':'';
  const img=f.kind==="img";
  return '<div class="pfbar has">'+
   '<span class="pfic '+esc(f.kind)+'">'+(img?"▦":f.kind==="pdf"?"PDF":"CAD")+'</span>'+
   '<span class="pfn"><b>'+esc(f.name)+'</b><small>'+fsize(f.size)+
     (img?' · can be used as a floor plan backdrop':f.kind==="pdf"
       ?' · PDF is kept for the team to download — to use it as a backdrop, export a PNG and upload that'
       :' · CAD file kept for the team to download — to use it as a backdrop, export a PDF or PNG')+
     '</small></span>'+
   (img?'<label class="pfsw"><input type="checkbox" id="pfshow"'+(f.show!==false?" checked":"")+
       '> Show as backdrop</label>'+
      '<label class="pfrg">Opacity <input type="range" id="pfop" min="5" max="100" value="'+
       (f.op==null?45:f.op)+'"></label>'+
      '<label class="pfrg">Size <input type="range" id="pfsc" min="30" max="300" value="'+
       (f.sc==null?100:f.sc)+'"></label>':'')+
   '<button class="pfdl" id="pfget">Open file</button>'+
   (wr?'<label class="pfup sm">Change file<input type="file" id="pfin" '+
     'accept=".pdf,.dwg,.dxf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.webp,.svg,image/*,application/pdf"></label>'+
     '<button class="pfx" id="pfdel" title="Remove file">×</button>':'')+
   '</div>';
}
function wirePlanBar(P,redraw){
  const inp=P.querySelector("#pfin");
  if(inp)inp.addEventListener("change",e=>{
    const f=e.target.files&&e.target.files[0]; if(!f)return;
    if(f.size>PLAN_MAX){ alert("File is larger than "+fsize(PLAN_MAX)+" Try a lower resolution, or export only the pages you need"); return }
    const ext=(f.name.match(/\.(\w+)$/)||[,""])[1].toLowerCase();
    const kind=isPlanImg(f)?"img":(ext==="pdf"||/pdf/.test(f.type))?"pdf":"cad";
    const done=url=>{ EV.planFile={name:f.name,size:f.size,kind,url,show:true,op:45,sc:100};
      redraw(); toastTL("Upload "+f.name+" then") };
    if(kind==="img")shrinkImg(f,2400,done);
    else { const fr=new FileReader(); fr.onload=()=>done(fr.result); fr.readAsDataURL(f) }
  });
  const g=P.querySelector("#pfget");
  if(g)g.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const f=planFile(); if(!f)return;
    const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=f.url; a.download=f.name;
    a.target="_blank"; a.rel="noopener"; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
  });
  const d=P.querySelector("#pfdel");
  if(d)d.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(!confirm("Remove the floor plan file from this event?"))return;
    EV.planFile=null; redraw() });
  const sh=P.querySelector("#pfshow");
  if(sh)sh.addEventListener("change",()=>{ planFile().show=sh.checked; redraw() });
  const op=P.querySelector("#pfop");
  if(op)op.addEventListener("input",()=>{ planFile().op=+op.value; applyPlanBg() });
  const sc=P.querySelector("#pfsc");
  if(sc)sc.addEventListener("input",()=>{ planFile().sc=+sc.value; applyPlanBg() });
}
/* ปรับพื้นหลังโดยไม่วาดผังใหม่ทั้งอัน เลื่อนแถบแล้วเห็นผลทันที */
function applyPlanBg(){
  const el=document.getElementById("planbg"); const f=planFile();
  if(!el)return;
  if(!f||f.kind!=="img"||f.show===false){ el.style.display="none"; return }
  el.style.display="block";
  el.style.opacity=(f.op==null?45:f.op)/100;
  el.style.backgroundImage='url("'+f.url+'")';
  el.style.backgroundSize=(f.sc==null?100:f.sc)+"%";
}
function pPlan(){
  const ovr=overdueBooths(), wrn=warnBooths();
  $("#page").innerHTML=
   (ovr.length||wrn.length?'<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px;border-left-color:'+
     cv(ovr.length?"--risk":"--warn")+'"><p>'+
     (ovr.length?'<b style="color:var(--risk)">Deposit overdue '+ovr.length+' booths</b> — '+
       esc(ovr.slice(0,6).map(b=>b.code).join(", "))+(ovr.length>6?" and others":"")+
       ' &nbsp;<button class="act" id="rel" style="display:inline-block;padding:5px 11px;font-size:13px">'+
       'Release and move the queue up</button>':'')+
     (ovr.length&&wrn.length?'<br>':'')+
     (wrn.length?'<b style="color:var(--warn)">Due soon '+wrn.length+' booths</b> — '+
       esc(wrn.slice(0,6).map(b=>b.code).join(", "))+(wrn.length>6?" and others":""):'')+
     '</p></div>':'')+
   '<div class="bar2">'+
   '<input type="search" id="q" placeholder="Search booth code or company" value="'+esc(q)+'">'+
   '<select id="fz"><option value="">All zones</option>'+
     Object.keys(EV.zoneNames||{}).map(z=>'<option value="'+z+'"'+(fZone===z?" selected":"")+'>'+
       esc(EV.zoneNames[z])+' ('+z+')</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   '<select id="fp"><option value="">All packages</option>'+
     Object.keys(EV.packages||{}).map(k=>'<option value="'+esc(k)+'"'+(fPkg===k?" selected":"")+'>'+
       esc(k)+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   ((fZone||fPkg||q)?'<button class="act" id="fclear" style="padding:9px 14px">Clear filters</button>':'')+
   '<div class="seg">'+["free","booked","deposit","paid"].map(k=>
     '<span style="padding:9px 13px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)"><i class="dot" style="background:'+
     cv(ST[k].c)+'"></i>'+ST[k].l+' '+shown().filter(x=>x.st===k).length+'</span>').join("")+'</div></div>'+
   ((fZone||fPkg)?'<p class="rdonly" style="margin:0 0 10px">Showing '+shown().length+' booths of '+
     B.length+' · '+[fZone?(EV.zoneNames||{})[fZone]:"",fPkg].filter(Boolean).map(esc).join(" · ")+
     '</p>':'')+
   planBar(can("floorplan")==="write")+
   '<div class="planhost"><div class="pw"><div id="planbg"></div><svg id="plan"></svg></div>'+
   '<aside class="side2" id="sd" hidden></aside></div>';
  wirePlanBar($("#page"),pPlan);
  $("#q").addEventListener("input",e=>{q=e.target.value;paint()});
  $("#fz").addEventListener("change",e=>{fZone=e.target.value;pPlan()});
  $("#fp").addEventListener("change",e=>{fPkg=e.target.value;pPlan()});
  const fc=$("#fclear");
  if(fc)fc.addEventListener("click",()=>{fZone="";fPkg="";q="";pPlan()});
  const rel=$("#rel");
  if(rel)rel.addEventListener("click",()=>{const n=releaseExpired();pPlan();
    alert("Release "+n+" booths released — the next in queue has moved up");});
  const xs=B.concat(AR);
  const X0=Math.min(...xs.map(o=>o.x))-1,Y0=Math.min(...xs.map(o=>o.y))-1;
  const X1=Math.max(...xs.map(o=>o.x+o.w))+1,Y1=Math.max(...xs.map(o=>o.y+o.h))+1;
  $("#plan").setAttribute("viewBox",X0+" "+Y0+" "+(X1-X0)+" "+(Y1-Y0));
  applyPlanBg();
  paint(); sidebar();
}
/* ตัดรหัสบูธเป็นบรรทัดให้พอดีความกว้าง แล้วคืนขนาดตัวอักษรที่ใช้ได้ */
/* จัดป้ายโซนให้อยู่ในกรอบ ตัดตามช่องว่างก่อน ถ้ายังไม่พอค่อยย่อขนาด */
function wrapLabel(txt,w,h){
  const W=.52, MAX=.8, MIN=.15;
  const words=String(txt).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const pack=(per)=>{                       // จัดคำลงบรรทัด ไม่หักกลางคำ
    const out=[]; let cur="";
    for(const wd of words){
      const t=cur?cur+" "+wd:wd;
      if(t.length<=per)cur=t; else{ if(cur)out.push(cur); cur=wd }
    }
    if(cur)out.push(cur);
    return out;
  };
  // รอบแรก ย่อขนาดลงเรื่อย ๆ จนคำยาวสุดพอดีบรรทัด โดยไม่หักคำ
  for(let fs=MAX; fs>=MIN; fs-=.03){
    const per=Math.max(1,Math.floor(w/(fs*W)));
    if(Math.max(...words.map(x=>x.length))>per)continue;
    const lines=pack(per);
    if(lines.length*fs*1.12<=h)return {lines,n:lines.length,fs};
  }
  // ยังไม่พอ ค่อยยอมหักคำที่ขนาดเล็กสุด
  const per=Math.max(2,Math.floor(w/(MIN*W)));
  const out=[];
  for(const wd of words){
    if(wd.length<=per)out.push(wd);
    else for(let i=0;i<wd.length;i+=per)out.push(wd.slice(i,i+per));
  }
  const max=Math.max(1,Math.floor(h/(MIN*1.12)));
  if(out.length>max){out.length=max; out[max-1]=out[max-1].slice(0,per-1)+"…"}
  return {lines:out,n:out.length,fs:MIN};
}
function fitLines(code,w){
  const W=.53;                       // ความกว้างตัวอักษรโดยประมาณ เทียบกับ font-size
  const size=t=>Math.min(.5,w/(t.length*W));
  let parts=[code];
  if(size(code)<.3){                 // แคบไป ลองตัดเป็นสองบรรทัด
    const cut=code.indexOf(",")>0?code.indexOf(",")+1:
              code.indexOf("-")>0?code.indexOf("-")+1:-1;
    if(cut>0)parts=[code.slice(0,cut).trim(),code.slice(cut).trim()].filter(Boolean);
  }
  const longest=Math.max(...parts.map(p=>p.length));
  return {parts,n:parts.length,fs:Math.max(.15,Math.min(.5,w/(longest*W)))};
}
const NS="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
function E(t,a){const e=document.createElementNS(NS,t);for(const k in a)e.setAttribute(k,a[k]);return e}
function paint(){
  const svg=$("#plan"); if(!svg)return; svg.textContent="";
  AR.forEach(a=>{
    const g=E("g",{class:"ar"}),cx=a.x+a.w/2,cy=a.y+a.h/2;
    g.appendChild(E("rect",{x:a.x,y:a.y,width:a.w,height:a.h,rx:.2}));
    /* ป้ายโซนแนวนอนเสมอ ตัดคำลงบรรทัดใหม่แทนการตะแคง */
    const L=wrapLabel(a.name,a.w-.3,a.h-.3);
    let y=cy-(L.n*L.fs*1.12)/2+L.fs*.86;
    L.lines.forEach(t=>{
      const e=E("text",{x:cx,y:y,"text-anchor":"middle","font-size":L.fs});
      e.textContent=t; g.appendChild(e); y+=L.fs*1.12;
    });
    svg.appendChild(g);
  });
  const s=q.trim().toLowerCase();
  B.forEach(b=>{
    const st=ST[b.st],g=E("g",{class:"bt"+(sel===b.code?" sel":""),tabindex:0,role:"button"});
    g.setAttribute("aria-label",b.code+" "+st.l);
    g.appendChild(E("rect",{x:b.x+.06,y:b.y+.06,width:b.w-.12,height:b.h-.12,rx:.17,fill:cv(st.c)}));
    const cx=b.x+b.w/2,cy=b.y+b.h/2;
    /* ตัวหนังสือแนวนอนเสมอ ถ้าไม่พอกว้างก็ตัดเป็นสองบรรทัดแล้วย่อขนาดให้พอดี */
    const lines=fitLines(b.code,b.w-.22);
    const room=b.h-.2;
    const nameOK=b.co&&room>lines.fs*lines.n+.42;
    const total=lines.fs*1.06*lines.n+(nameOK?.34:0);
    let ty=cy-total/2+lines.fs*.86;
    lines.parts.forEach(part=>{
      const t=E("text",{x:cx,y:ty,"text-anchor":"middle","font-size":lines.fs,fill:st.t});
      t.textContent=part; g.appendChild(t); ty+=lines.fs*1.06;
    });
    if(nameOK){
      const nf=Math.min(.34,Math.max(.16,(b.w-.24)/(Math.min(b.co.length,14)*.5)));
      const mx=Math.max(3,Math.floor((b.w-.24)/(nf*.5)));
      const n=E("text",{x:cx,y:ty+nf*.5,"text-anchor":"middle","font-size":nf,fill:st.t,opacity:.9});
      n.textContent=b.co.length>mx?b.co.slice(0,mx-1)+"…":b.co;
      g.appendChild(n);
    }
    const hs2=holdState(b);
    if(hs2&&(hs2.k==="warn"||hs2.k==="over")){
      g.insertBefore(E("rect",{x:b.x-.04,y:b.y-.04,width:b.w+.08,height:b.h+.08,rx:.24,
        fill:"none",stroke:cv("--risk"),"stroke-width":.16}),g.firstChild.nextSibling||null);
    }
    const qn=(Q[b.code]||[]).length;
    if(qn){g.appendChild(E("circle",{cx:b.x+b.w-.28,cy:b.y+.3,r:.26,fill:cv("--risk")}));
      const c=E("text",{x:b.x+b.w-.28,y:b.y+.43,"text-anchor":"middle","font-size":.36,fill:"#fff","font-weight":700});
      c.textContent=qn;g.appendChild(c)}
    const okQ=!s||b.code.toLowerCase().includes(s)||(b.co||"").toLowerCase().includes(s);
    const okZ=!fZone||b.zone===fZone;
    const okP=!fPkg||b.pkg===fPkg;
    if(!(okQ&&okZ&&okP))g.classList.add("dim");
    g.addEventListener("click",()=>{sel=b.code;paint();sidebar()});
    g.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"||e.key===" "){e.preventDefault();sel=b.code;paint();sidebar()}});
    svg.appendChild(g);
  });
}
function sidebar(){
  const sd=$("#sd"); if(!sd)return;
  if(!sel){sd.hidden=true; sd.innerHTML=""; return}
  sd.hidden=false;
  const b=B.find(x=>x.code===sel),st=ST[b.st],qs=Q[b.code]||[],hs=holdState(b);
  const own=ROLE[role].scope==="all"||!b.sales||b.sales===me, wr=can("floorplan")==="write";
  let h='<h3>'+esc(b.code)+'</h3><p class="s"><span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(st.c)+';color:'+st.t+'">'+
   st.l+'</span>'+(b.pkg?" · "+esc(b.pkg):"")+'</p>';
  if(b.co&&own){
    h+='<div class="lgrow">'+logoTile(b.co,46)+'<div class="meta"><b>'+esc(b.co)+'</b>'+
      (SITE[b.co]?'<a href="'+esc(SITE[b.co])+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+esc(SITE[b.co])+'</a>':
       '<span style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink3)">No website</span>')+'</div></div>';
    if(can("floorplan")==="write"){
      h+='<input id="site" placeholder="Company website, e.g. example.com" value="'+esc(SITE[b.co]||"")+
        '" style="width:100%;padding:8px 11px;border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:8px;'+
        'background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);margin-bottom:7px">'+
        '<div class="lgbtns"><button data-a="fetch">Fetch logo from website</button>'+
        '<label>Upload logo<input type="file" id="up" accept="image/*"></label>'+
        (LOGO[b.co]?'<button data-a="rmlogo">Remove logo</button>':'')+'</div>'+
        '<p class="simnote">this demo cannot fetch from the internet because the published page blocks cross-site requests '+
        'you will get initials from the domain name instead · <b>uploading your own works properly</b> Try picking a file</p>';
    }
  }
  const pk=(EV.packages||{})[b.pkg];
  h+='<dl class="kv">'+
   (b.co&&!own?'<dt>Client</dt><dd><span style="color:var(--ink2)">Other reps’ deals</span></dd>':'')+
   (!b.co?'<dt>Client</dt><dd><span style="color:var(--ink2)">None yet</span></dd>':'')+
   (b.sales?'<dt>Sales</dt><dd>'+esc(b.sales)+'</dd>':'')+
   (b.contact&&own?'<dt>Contact</dt><dd>'+esc(b.contact)+(b.phone?"<br>"+esc(b.phone):"")+'</dd>':'')+
   '<dt>List price</dt><dd>'+(canPrice()
     ?'<span class="inl" contenteditable="plaintext-only" id="bprice">'+f(b.list)+'</span> THB'+
      '<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">Editable — press Enter to save</small>'
     :f(b.list)+' THB')+'</dd>'+
   (hs?'<dt>Deposit</dt><dd><span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(hs.c)+'">'+hs.t+'</span>'+
     (b.due?'<br><small style="color:var(--ink2)">Due '+esc(b.due)+'</small>':'')+'</dd>':'')+
   '</dl><div class="acts">';
  if(hs&&wr&&own){
    h+='<label style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink2)">Deposit term</label>'+
      '<select id="hd">'+HOLD_OPTS.map(n=>'<option value="'+n+'"'+(b.hold===n?" selected":"")+'>'+
        n+' day</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
      '<button class="act" data-a="sethold">Reset the due date from today</button>';
  }
  if(!wr)h+='<p style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:13.5px;margin:0">This role has view access only</p>';
  else if(b.st==="free")h+='<input id="cu" placeholder="Company">'+
    '<input id="cs" placeholder="Company website (optional)">'+
    '<label style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink2)">Deposit term</label>'+
    '<select id="hd2">'+HOLD_OPTS.map(n=>'<option value="'+n+'"'+(n===30?" selected":"")+'>'+
      n+' day</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
    '<button class="act go" data-a="book">Booked under '+esc(me)+'</button>';
  else if(!own)h+='<p style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:13.5px;margin:0 0 6px">Booths of '+esc(b.sales)+' you cannot edit</p>'+
    '<input id="cu" placeholder="Client to queue next"><button class="act" data-a="q">Join queue</button>';
  else h+=(NEXT[b.st]?'<button class="act go" data-a="next">moved to '+ST[NEXT[b.st]].l+'</button>':'')+
    '<input id="cu" placeholder="Client to queue next"><button class="act" data-a="q">Join queue</button>'+
    '<button class="act warn" data-a="rel">Cancel and move the queue up</button>';
  h+='</div>';
  if(qs.length)h+='<ul class="ql">'+qs.map((x,i)=>'<li><b>Que '+(i+1)+'</b><span>'+esc(x.co)+
    ' <small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+esc(x.sales)+'</small></span></li>').join("")+'</ul>';
  h+=pk?pkgHTML(b.pkg,pk)
      :'<p class="simnote" style="margin-top:12px;padding-top:12px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)">'+
       'This booth has no package set, so there are no terms to show — set it on the packages and pricing page</p>';
  sd.innerHTML='<button class="x" aria-label="Off">×</button>'+h;
  sd.querySelector(".x").addEventListener("click",()=>{sel=null;sidebar();paint()});
  sd.querySelectorAll("[data-a]").forEach(x=>x.addEventListener("click",()=>act(x.dataset.a)));
  const bp=$("#bprice");
  if(bp){
    bp.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();bp.blur()}});
    bp.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const v=+(bp.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g,""));
      if(!v||v===b.list){bp.textContent=f(b.list);return}
      b.list=v; paint(); sidebar();
    });
  }
  const up=$("#up");
  if(up)up.addEventListener("change",e=>{
    const fl=e.target.files[0]; if(!fl)return;
    if(fl.size>500000){alert("File is larger than 500KB");return}
    const r=new FileReader();
    r.onload=()=>{LOGO[b.co]=r.result;paint();sidebar()};
    r.readAsDataURL(fl);
  });
}
function act(a){
  const b=B.find(x=>x.code===sel),i=$("#cu"),n=i?i.value.trim():"";
  if(a==="book"){if(!n){i.focus();return}b.st="booked";b.co=n;b.sales=me;
    const hd=$("#hd2")?+$("#hd2").value:30; b.hold=hd; b.due=addDays(hd);
    const u=$("#cs")&&$("#cs").value.trim(); if(u)SITE[n]=norm(u);}
  else if(a==="sethold"){const hd=+$("#hd").value; b.hold=hd; b.due=addDays(hd);}
  else if(a==="fetch"){
    const u=$("#site").value.trim(); if(!u){$("#site").focus();return}
    SITE[b.co]=norm(u); LOGO[b.co]=fakeLogo(SITE[b.co]);}
  else if(a==="rmlogo")delete LOGO[b.co];
  else if(a==="next"){b.st=NEXT[b.st]||b.st; if(b.st!=="booked"){b.due=null;b.hold=null}}
  else if(a==="q"){if(!n){i.focus();return}(Q[b.code]=Q[b.code]||[]).push({co:n,sales:me})}
  else if(a==="rel"){const qs=Q[b.code]||[];
    if(qs.length){const x=qs.shift();b.st="booked";b.co=x.co;b.sales=x.sales;
      b.hold=30;b.due=addDays(30);if(!qs.length)delete Q[b.code]}
    else{b.st="free";b.co=null;b.sales=null;b.due=null;b.hold=null}}
  paint();sidebar();
}



/* ---------- ดาวน์โหลด CSV ----------
   หน้าเผยแพร่ต้องขอผ่าน claude.use("downloads") ผู้ดูกดยืนยันเองทุกครั้ง
   ส่วนตอนขึ้นเซิร์ฟเวอร์เองใช้ลิงก์ดาวน์โหลดปกติได้ จึงมีทางสำรองไว้ */
let DL=undefined;
async function downloader(){
  if(DL!==undefined)return DL;
  DL = (window.claude && claude.use) ? await claude.use("downloads").catch(()=>null) : null;
  return DL;
}
function csvCell(v){
  const t=v==null?"":String(v);
  return /[",\n]/.test(t) ? '"'+t.replace(/"/g,'""')+'"' : t;
}
function toCSV(rows){
  // BOM ข้างหน้า ไม่งั้น Excel เปิดภาษาไทยเป็นตัวยึกยือ
  return "﻿"+rows.map(r=>r.map(csvCell).join(",")).join("\r\n");
}
async function saveCSV(name,rows,btn){
  const data=toCSV(rows);
  const label=btn?btn.textContent:null;
  try{
    const d=await downloader();
    if(d){
      if(btn)btn.textContent="Saving…";
      await d.save({filename:name, data});
      if(btn)btn.textContent="Saved";
    }else{
      // ทางสำรองสำหรับตอนรันเองบนเซิร์ฟเวอร์เรา
      const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([data],{type:"text/csv"}));
      const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=url; a.download=name;
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
      setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
      if(btn)btn.textContent="Saved";
    }
  }catch(e){
    if(btn)btn.textContent = e && e.code==="declined" ? "Cancelled" : "Could not save";
  }finally{
    if(btn&&label)setTimeout(()=>{btn.textContent=label},2500);
  }
}
function dlBtn(id,text){ return '<button class="act" id="'+id+'" style="padding:9px 15px">'+text+'</button>' }

/* ---------- แพ็กเกจและราคา ---------- */
function pPkg(){
  const wr=canPrice(), PK=EV.packages||{};
  const names=Object.keys(PK);
  const count=n=>B.filter(b=>b.pkg===n).length;
  const sold=n=>B.filter(b=>b.pkg===n&&b.st!=="free").length;
  let h=(wr?'<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p>List prices and terms are editable right in the table '+
    'A price change applies immediately to booths of that package that are still free <b>Booths already sold do not change</b> '+
    'because a price already agreed with the client has to stay put</p></div>'
   :'<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p>Role '+ROLE[role].n+
    ' can see prices but not change them — changing prices needs a sales lead or above</p></div>');

  h+='<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Package</th><th>Size</th><th>Type</th>'+
   '<th class="num">List price</th><th class="num">Booths</th><th class="num">Sold</th>'+
   '<th>Terms</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   names.map(n=>{const p=PK[n];
     return '<tr><td><b>'+esc(n)+'</b></td>'+
      '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(p.size)+'</td>'+
      '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(p.build)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+(wr?'<span class="inl" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-pk="'+esc(n)+
        '">'+f(p.price)+'</span>':f(p.price))+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+count(n)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+sold(n)+'</td>'+
      '<td><ul style="margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px">'+
        p.b.map((x,i)=>'<li style="display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:flex-start">'+
          '<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(KIND[x[0]][1])+';font-size:10.5px;flex:0 0 auto">'+
          KIND[x[0]][0]+'</span>'+
          (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-pb="'+esc(n)+'" data-bi="'+i+
            '" style="font-size:12.5px">'+esc(x[1])+'</span>'
            :'<span style="font-size:12.5px">'+esc(x[1])+'</span>')+'</li>').join("")+
        '</ul></td></tr>'}).join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>';

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Zone name</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Code</th>'+
   '<th>Zone display name</th><th class="num">Booths</th><th class="num">Sold</th>'+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>'+Object.keys(EV.zoneNames||{}).map(z=>
    '<tr><td><b>'+esc(z)+'</b></td>'+
    '<td>'+(wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-zn="'+esc(z)+'">'+
      esc(EV.zoneNames[z])+'</span>':esc(EV.zoneNames[z]))+'</td>'+
    '<td class="num">'+B.filter(b=>b.zone===z).length+'</td>'+
    '<td class="num">'+B.filter(b=>b.zone===z&&b.st!=="free").length+'</td></tr>').join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>'+
   '<p class="rdonly">Zone names are used as filters on the floor plan and printed on hall signage</p>'+
   '<h2 class="sec">Add-ons</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>'+
   '<th>Items</th><th class="num">Price</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   (EV.addons||[]).map((a,i)=>'<tr><td>'+esc(a[0])+'</td><td class="num">'+
     (wr?'<span class="inl" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-ad="'+i+'">'+f(a[1])+'</span>'
      :f(a[1]))+'</td></tr>').join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>'+
   '<p class="rdonly">these prices are in the budget sheet but every quantity is 0 '+
   'showing it at close time lets reps see there is something more to sell</p>';


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-pk]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const n=el.dataset.pk, v=+(el.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g,""));
      if(!v){el.textContent=f(PK[n].price);return}
      PK[n].price=v;
      B.forEach(b=>{if(b.pkg===n&&b.st==="free")b.list=v});   // เฉพาะบูธที่ยังว่าง
      pPkg();
    });
  });
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-pb]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(); if(t)PK[el.dataset.pb].b[+el.dataset.bi][1]=t; else pPkg();
  }));
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-zn]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(); if(t)EV.zoneNames[el.dataset.zn]=t; pPkg();
  }));
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-ad]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const v=+(el.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")); if(v)EV.addons[+el.dataset.ad][1]=v; pPkg();
    });
  });
}

/* ---------- ดีล ---------- */
function pDeals(){
  const list=DEALS.filter(mine);
  $("#page").innerHTML=(ROLE[role].scope==="own"
    ?'<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p>This role sees only their own deals — showing '+
     list.length+' of '+DEALS.length+'</p></div>':'')+
   '<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Client</th><th>Package</th><th>Booths</th>'+
   '<th class="num">List price</th><th>Status</th><th>Sales</th><th>Contact</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   list.map(d=>'<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+logoTile(d.co,28)+
    '<b>'+esc(d.co)+'</b></span></td><td>'+esc(d.pkg||"—")+'</td>'+
    '<td style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(d.booths.slice(0,4).join(", "))+
      (d.booths.length>4?" +"+(d.booths.length-4):"")+'</td>'+
    '<td class="num">'+f(d.list)+'</td>'+
    '<td><span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(ST[d.st].c)+';color:'+ST[d.st].t+'">'+ST[d.st].l+'</span></td>'+
    '<td>'+esc(d.sales||"—")+'</td>'+
    '<td style="font-size:13px">'+(d.contact?esc(d.contact):'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">—</span>')+
      (d.phone?'<br><span style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(d.phone)+'</span>':'')+'</td></tr>').join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>';
}



/* ---------- จัดการการขาย ---------- */
const STAGES=[["lead","Not contacted","--ink3"],["booking","On hold","--s-booked"],
 ["quoted","Quote sent","--s-deposit"],["confirmed","Won","--accent"],
 ["billed","Billed","--s-deposit"],["paid","Paid in full","--s-paid"],["lost","Lost","--risk"]];
const ACTS=[["call","Phone"],["line","LINE"],["email","Email"],["meet","Meeting"],
 ["quote","Quote sent"],["visit","Visit"],["other","Other"]];
const stName=k=>(STAGES.find(x=>x[0]===k)||["","—","--ink3"])[1];
const stCol=k=>cv((STAGES.find(x=>x[0]===k)||["","","--ink3"])[2]);
function daysAgo(iso){ if(!iso)return null;
  const a=new Date(iso+"T00:00:00"),b=new Date(); b.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  return Math.round((b-a)/86400000); }
function lastAct(d){ return d.acts.length?d.acts[d.acts.length-1]:null }
function isStale(d){ const l=lastAct(d); if(!l)return true; return daysAgo(l.date)>14 }
function noNext(d){ return !d.next||!d.nextDate }
function overdue(d){ return d.nextDate&&daysAgo(d.nextDate)>0 }

function pSales(){
  const list=DEALS.filter(mine);
  const open=list.filter(d=>!["paid","lost"].includes(d.stage));
  const cover=open.length?Math.round(open.filter(d=>!noNext(d)).length/open.length*100):100;
  const todo=open.filter(d=>overdue(d)||noNext(d)||isStale(d))
    .sort((a,b)=>(overdue(b)-overdue(a))||(noNext(b)-noNext(a)));

  let h='<div class="bar2"><div class="seg">'+
   '<button data-sv="board" aria-pressed="'+(salesView==="board")+'">Board</button>'+
   '<button data-sv="table" aria-pressed="'+(salesView==="table")+'">Table</button></div>'+
   '<span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)">Drag a card across columns to change its stage</span></div>'+
   '<div class="cards">'+
   card(list.length,"Deals owned",ROLE[role].scope==="own"?"Yours only":"Whole team","--line2")+
   card(open.length,"Still open","--s-booked")+
   card(cover+"%","Has a next step",cover<100?"the rest have no next step":"Every deal covered",cover<60?"--risk":"--s-paid")+
   card(list.filter(isStale).length,"No contact for over 14 days","--risk")+
   '</div>';

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Sales pipeline</h2>'+
   (salesView==="board"?board(list)
    :'<div class="cards" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(118px,1fr))">'+
     STAGES.map(st=>{const g=list.filter(d=>d.stage===st[0]);
       return '<div class="c" style="--t:'+cv(st[2])+'"><b>'+g.length+'</b><span>'+st[1]+'</span>'+
        '<small>'+f(g.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0))+'</small></div>'}).join("")+'</div>');

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Do first</h2>';
  h+=todo.length?'<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Client</th><th>Stage</th>'+
    '<th>Last contact</th><th>Next step</th><th>Why it is here</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
    todo.slice(0,25).map(d=>{const l=lastAct(d);
      const why=overdue(d)?'<span style="color:var(--risk)">overdue by '+daysAgo(d.nextDate)+' day</span>'
        :noNext(d)?'<span style="color:var(--warn)">No next step set</span>'
        :'<span style="color:var(--warn)">quiet for '+daysAgo(l.date)+' day</span>';
      return '<tr data-d="'+d.id+'" style="cursor:pointer"><td><b>'+esc(d.co)+'</b>'+
       '<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+esc(d.booths.slice(0,3).join(", "))+'</small></td>'+
       '<td><span class="pill" style="background:'+stCol(d.stage)+'">'+stName(d.stage)+'</span></td>'+
       '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+(l?daysAgo(l.date)+" days ago":"Never")+'</td>'+
       '<td>'+(d.next?esc(d.next)+'<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+esc(d.nextDate)+'</small>':'—')+'</td>'+
       '<td>'+why+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+'</tbody></table></div>'
    :'<div class="note"><p>Nothing outstanding — every deal has a next step and was contacted within 14 days</p></div>';

  h+='<h2 class="sec">All deals</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>'+
   '<th>Client</th><th>Booths</th><th class="num">Value</th><th>Stage</th>'+
   '<th>Last contact</th><th>Next step</th>'+(ROLE[role].scope==="all"?'<th>Sales</th>':'')+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>'+list.map(d=>{const l=lastAct(d);
     return '<tr data-d="'+d.id+'" style="cursor:pointer"><td><b>'+esc(d.co)+'</b></td>'+
      '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(d.booths.slice(0,3).join(", "))+
        (d.booths.length>3?" +"+(d.booths.length-3):"")+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(d.list)+'</td>'+
      '<td><span class="pill" style="background:'+stCol(d.stage)+'">'+stName(d.stage)+'</span></td>'+
      '<td style="color:'+(isStale(d)?"var(--risk)":"var(--ink2)")+'">'+
        (l?daysAgo(l.date)+" days ago":"Never")+'</td>'+
      '<td style="color:'+(overdue(d)?"var(--risk)":"var(--ink2)")+'">'+
        (d.next?esc(d.next)+" · "+esc(d.nextDate):"—")+'</td>'+
      (ROLE[role].scope==="all"?'<td>'+esc(d.sales||"—")+'</td>':'')+'</tr>'}).join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>';

  if(ROLE[role].scope==="all"){
    const reps={};
    DEALS.forEach(d=>{const k=d.sales||"—";(reps[k]=reps[k]||[]).push(d)});
    h+='<h2 class="sec">Sales team</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Sales</th>'+
     '<th class="num">Deals</th><th class="num">Value</th><th class="num">Has next step</th>'+
     '<th class="num">Quiet for over 14 days</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
     Object.keys(reps).sort((a,b)=>reps[b].reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0)-reps[a].reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0))
     .map(k=>{const g=reps[k],op=g.filter(d=>!["paid","lost"].includes(d.stage));
       const c=op.length?Math.round(op.filter(d=>!noNext(d)).length/op.length*100):100;
       return '<tr><td><b>'+esc(k)+'</b></td><td class="num">'+g.length+'</td>'+
        '<td class="num">'+f(g.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0))+'</td>'+
        '<td class="num" style="color:'+(c<60?"var(--risk)":"var(--ok)")+'">'+c+'%</td>'+
        '<td class="num">'+g.filter(isStale).length+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+
     '</tbody></table></div>';
  }


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-d]").forEach(tr=>
    tr.addEventListener("click",()=>openDeal(+tr.dataset.d)));
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-sv]").forEach(b=>
    b.addEventListener("click",()=>{salesView=b.dataset.sv;pSales()}));
  if(salesView==="board")bindBoard();
}
let dealOpen=null;
/* บอร์ดท่อการขาย ลากการ์ดข้ามคอลัมน์เพื่อเปลี่ยนขั้นตอน
   เขียนคืนหลังจากหลุดไปตอนตัดโค้ดพลาด */
function board(list){
  const wr=can("deal")==="write";
  return '<div class="kb">'+STAGES.map(st=>{
    const g=list.filter(d=>d.stage===st[0])
      .sort((a,b)=>(overdue(b)-overdue(a))||(b.list-a.list));
    const sum=g.reduce((s,d)=>s+(d.list||0),0);
    return '<div class="kbcol" data-st="'+st[0]+'">'+
      '<div class="kbh" style="background:'+cv(st[2])+'">'+esc(st[1])+
        '<span class="n">'+g.length+'</span></div>'+
      '<div class="kbsum">'+f(sum)+'<small>Value at list price</small></div>'+
      '<div class="kbbody">'+(g.length?g.map(d=>{
        const l=lastAct(d);
        const chips=(overdue(d)?'<span class="chip red">Overdue by '+daysAgo(d.nextDate)+' day</span>':"")+
          (noNext(d)?'<span class="chip amber">No next step</span>':"")+
          (!overdue(d)&&!noNext(d)&&isStale(d)?'<span class="chip amber">quiet '+
             (l?daysAgo(l.date):"—")+' day</span>':"");
        return '<div class="kbc" data-d="'+d.id+'"'+(wr?' draggable="true"':'')+'>'+
          '<b>'+esc(d.co)+'</b>'+
          '<div class="booths">'+esc(d.booths.slice(0,3).join(", "))+
            (d.booths.length>3?" +"+(d.booths.length-3):"")+'</div>'+
          '<div class="row"><span class="val">'+f(d.list)+'</span>'+
            '<span class="chip">'+esc(d.sales||"No rep assigned")+'</span></div>'+
          (chips?'<div class="row">'+chips+'</div>':"")+'</div>'}).join("")
        :'<div class="kbempty">No deals at this stage</div>')+'</div></div>'}).join("")+'</div>';
}
function bindBoard(){
  const P=$("#page"), wr=can("deal")==="write";
  P.querySelectorAll(".kbc").forEach(c=>{
    c.addEventListener("click",()=>openDeal(+c.dataset.d));
    if(!wr)return;
    c.addEventListener("dragstart",e=>{ e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain",c.dataset.d);
      e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed="move"; c.classList.add("drag") });
    c.addEventListener("dragend",()=>c.classList.remove("drag"));
  });
  if(!wr)return;
  P.querySelectorAll(".kbcol").forEach(col=>{
    col.addEventListener("dragover",e=>{e.preventDefault();e.dataTransfer.dropEffect="move";
      col.classList.add("over")});
    col.addEventListener("dragleave",()=>col.classList.remove("over"));
    col.addEventListener("drop",e=>{ e.preventDefault(); col.classList.remove("over");
      const d=DEALS.find(x=>x.id===+e.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain"));
      if(!d)return; const to=col.dataset.st;
      if(d.stage===to)return;
      d.stage=to;
      /* ขั้นตอนดีลกับสถานะบูธต้องไปด้วยกัน ไม่งั้นผังกับ Feasibility จะไม่ตรง */
      const bst=({booking:"booked",quoted:"booked",confirmed:"deposit",
                  billed:"deposit",paid:"paid",lost:"free",lead:"free"})[to];
      if(bst)d.booths.forEach(code=>{const b=B.find(x=>x.code===code); if(b)b.st=bst});
      pSales();
    });
  });
}
function openDeal(id){ dealOpen=DEALS.find(d=>d.id===id); drawDealPanel() }
function drawDealPanel(){
  let sd=document.getElementById("dealpanel");
  if(!sd){ sd=document.createElement("aside"); sd.id="dealpanel"; sd.className="side2";
    document.body.appendChild(sd); }
  const d=dealOpen; if(!d){sd.remove();return}
  const today=ymd(new Date());
  sd.hidden=false;
  sd.innerHTML='<button class="x" aria-label="Off">×</button>'+
   '<h3>'+esc(d.co)+'</h3><p class="s">'+esc(d.pkg||"—")+' · '+esc(d.booths.join(", "))+
   ' · '+f(d.list)+' THB</p>'+
   '<dl class="kv"><dt>Stage</dt><dd><select id="dstage">'+STAGES.map(x=>
     '<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+(d.stage===x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+
   '</select></dd>'+
   (d.contact?'<dt>Contact</dt><dd>'+esc(d.contact)+(d.phone?'<br>'+esc(d.phone):'')+'</dd>':'')+
   '<dt>Next step</dt><dd>'+(d.next?esc(d.next)+' · '+esc(d.nextDate):'<span style="color:var(--warn)">None yet</span>')+'</dd></dl>'+
   '<div class="acts"><b style="font-size:13px">Log a contact</b>'+
   '<select id="atype">'+ACTS.map(a=>'<option value="'+a[0]+'">'+a[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   '<input id="anote" placeholder="what was discussed, what came of it">'+
   '<input id="anext" placeholder="What is a next step">'+
   '<input id="adate" type="date" value="'+today+'">'+
   '<button class="act go" id="asave">Save</button></div>'+
   (d.acts.length?'<ul class="ql">'+d.acts.slice().reverse().map(a=>
     '<li><b>'+esc(a.date)+'</b><span>'+esc((ACTS.find(x=>x[0]===a.type)||["","?"])[1])+
     ' — '+esc(a.note||"—")+'</span></li>').join("")+'</ul>'
    :'<p style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:13px;margin:12px 0 0;padding-top:10px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)">No contact history</p>');
  sd.querySelector(".x").addEventListener("click",()=>{dealOpen=null;sd.remove()});
  sd.querySelector("#dstage").addEventListener("change",e=>{d.stage=e.target.value;pSales();drawDealPanel()});
  sd.querySelector("#asave").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const note=sd.querySelector("#anote").value.trim();
    const nx=sd.querySelector("#anext").value.trim();
    const dt=sd.querySelector("#adate").value||today;
    if(!note&&!nx){sd.querySelector("#anote").focus();return}
    d.acts.push({date:dt,type:sd.querySelector("#atype").value,note:note});
    if(nx){d.next=nx; d.nextDate=dt;}
    pSales(); drawDealPanel();
  });
}


/* ---------- P&L แบบต้นไม้ ---------- */
let pnlAll=true, open1={};
function evNumbers(e){
  const held=e.booths.filter(b=>b.st!=="free");
  const income={
    list:e.booths.reduce((s,b)=>s+(b.list||0),0),
    con:held.reduce((s,b)=>s+(b.list||0),0),
    got:held.reduce((s,b)=>s+(b.list||0)*(b.st==="paid"?1:b.st==="deposit"?dep:0),0)};
  const cats={};
  (e.budget||[]).forEach(l=>{const c=cats[l.cat]=cats[l.cat]||{fc:0,ac:0};
    c.fc+=lineTotal(l); c.ac+=(l.ac||0)});
  const ST=e.catTotals||{};
  Object.keys(cats).forEach(c=>{cats[c].sheet=ST[c]!=null?ST[c]:null;
    cats[c].plan=ST[c]!=null?ST[c]:cats[c].fc;});
  const exFc=Object.values(cats).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.plan,0);
  const exAc=Object.values(cats).reduce((s,c)=>s+c.ac,0);
  const mismatch=Object.keys(cats).filter(c=>cats[c].sheet!=null&&
    Math.abs(cats[c].sheet-cats[c].fc)>1).map(c=>({c,sheet:cats[c].sheet,sum:cats[c].fc}));
  const boothCost=held.reduce((s,b)=>s+((b.pkg==="Food"?1800:2500)+750),0);
  return {e,income,cats,exFc,exAc,boothCost,mismatch,
    goal:e.target!=null?e.target:Math.round(income.list*.64)};
}
function pPnl(){
  const rows=pnlAll?EVENTS.map(evNumbers):[evNumbers(EV)];
  const wr=can("budget")==="write"&&!pnlAll;
  const T={goal:0,con:0,got:0,exFc:0,exAc:0,boothCost:0};
  rows.forEach(r=>{T.goal+=r.goal;T.con+=r.income.con;T.got+=r.income.got;
    T.exFc+=r.exFc;T.exAc+=r.exAc;T.boothCost+=r.boothCost});
  const gpPlan=T.goal-T.exFc, gpNow=T.con-T.exAc-T.boothCost;
  const mgPlan=T.goal?gpPlan/T.goal*100:0, mgNow=T.con?gpNow/T.con*100:0;

  let h='<div class="bar2"><div class="seg">'+
   '<button data-a="1" aria-pressed="'+pnlAll+'">All events combined</button>'+
   '<button data-a="0" aria-pressed="'+(!pnlAll)+'">Only '+esc(EV.short)+'</button></div>'+
   '<label for="dp3">Deposit</label><select id="dp3">'+
     [["0.5","50%"],["0.3","30%"],["1","100%"]].map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+
       (dep==+x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   dlBtn("dlpnl","Download CSV")+
   '<button class="act" id="expall">'+(Object.keys(open1).length?"Collapse all":"Expand all")+'</button>'+
   '</div>';

  h+='<div class="cards">'+
   card(f(T.goal),"Planned revenue","--line2")+card(f(T.con),"Won","--s-deposit")+
   card(f(T.exFc),"Planned expenses","--warn")+card(f(T.exAc+T.boothCost),"Actually paid","--risk")+
   card(f(gpPlan),"Planned profit",Math.round(mgPlan*10)/10+"%","--accent")+
   card(f(gpNow),"Profit so far",Math.round(mgNow*10)/10+"%",gpNow<0?"--risk":"--s-paid")+
   '</div>';

  const m=(n)=>'<td class="num">'+f(n)+'</td>';
  const sg=(n)=>'<td class="num" style="color:'+(n<0?cv("--risk"):cv("--ok"))+
    ';font-weight:600">'+f(n)+'</td>';
  const key=(...p)=>p.join("›");
  const caret=(k,has)=>has?'<button class="exp" data-e2="'+esc(k)+'">'+(open1[k]?"▾":"▸")+'</button>'
    :'<span class="exp"></span>';
  const bar=(a,b2)=>{const p=b2?Math.min(100,a/b2*100):0;
    return '<span class="bar"><i style="width:'+p+'%;background:'+
      (a>b2?cv("--risk"):cv("--ok"))+'"></i></span>'};

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Profit & loss</h2>'+
   (wr?'<p class="edhint"><span><i></i>Dashed fields can be clicked and typed over</span>'+
     '<span>Expand to the lowest level to edit amounts <b>On plan</b> and <b>Now</b> line by line</span>'+
     '<span>Enter to save · Esc to cancel</span></p>'
    :(pnlAll?'<p class="edhint">Select "Only '+esc(EV.short)+'" above to edit amounts line by line</p>'
      :'<p class="edhint">Role '+ROLE[role].n+' cannot edit budget figures</p>'))+
   '<div class="tw"><table class="tree" style="min-width:760px">'+
   '<thead><tr><th>Items</th><th class="num" style="width:150px">On plan</th>'+
   '<th class="num" style="width:150px">Now</th><th class="num" style="width:140px">Difference</th>'+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>';

  // ---- รายได้
  const kInc="Revenue";
  h+='<tr class="lv0"><td>'+caret(kInc,true)+'Revenue'+bar(T.con,T.goal)+'</td>'+
   m(T.goal)+m(T.con)+sg(T.con-T.goal)+'</tr>';
  if(open1[kInc]){
    rows.forEach(r=>{
      const kEv=key(kInc,r.e.id);
      const pk={}; r.e.booths.forEach(b=>{const p=b.pkg||"No package set";
        const x=pk[p]=pk[p]||{list:0,con:0}; x.list+=b.list||0;
        if(b.st!=="free")x.con+=b.list||0;});
      h+='<tr class="lv1"><td>'+caret(kEv,true)+esc(r.e.short)+'</td>'+
        m(r.goal)+m(r.income.con)+sg(r.income.con-r.goal)+'</tr>';
      if(open1[kEv])Object.keys(pk).sort((a,b2)=>pk[b2].con-pk[a].con).forEach(p=>
        h+='<tr class="lv2"><td>'+caret("",false)+esc(p)+'</td>'+
          '<td class="num">'+f(pk[p].list)+'</td>'+m(pk[p].con)+
          '<td class="num">—</td></tr>');
    });
    h+='<tr class="lv1"><td>'+caret("",false)+'Money in the bank</td>'+
      '<td class="num">—</td>'+m(T.got)+'<td class="num">—</td></tr>';
  }

  // ---- รายจ่าย
  const kEx="Expenses";
  const allCats={};
  rows.forEach(r=>Object.keys(r.cats).forEach(c=>{const a=allCats[c]=allCats[c]||{plan:0,ac:0};
    a.plan+=r.cats[c].plan; a.ac+=r.cats[c].ac}));
  h+='<tr class="lv0"><td>'+caret(kEx,true)+'Expenses'+bar(T.exAc+T.boothCost,T.exFc)+'</td>'+
   m(T.exFc)+m(T.exAc+T.boothCost)+sg(T.exFc-T.exAc-T.boothCost)+'</tr>';
  if(open1[kEx]){
    Object.keys(allCats).forEach(c=>{
      const kC=key(kEx,c);
      const lines=pnlAll?EVENTS.flatMap(e=>(e.budget||[]).filter(l=>l.cat===c))
                        :(EV.budget||[]).filter(l=>l.cat===c);
      h+='<tr class="lv1"><td>'+caret(kC,lines.length>0)+esc(c)+
        bar(allCats[c].ac,allCats[c].plan)+'</td>'+
        m(allCats[c].plan)+m(allCats[c].ac)+sg(allCats[c].plan-allCats[c].ac)+'</tr>';
      if(!open1[kC])return;
      const subs=[]; lines.forEach(l=>{const k2=l.sub||"— no sub-category —";
        if(!subs.includes(k2))subs.push(k2)});
      subs.forEach(sb=>{
        const kS=key(kC,sb);
        const g=lines.filter(l=>(l.sub||"— no sub-category —")===sb);
        const gf=g.reduce((s,l)=>s+lineTotal(l),0), ga=g.reduce((s,l)=>s+(l.ac||0),0);
        h+='<tr class="lv2"><td>'+caret(kS,true)+esc(sb)+
          ' <small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+g.length+' Item</small></td>'+
          m(gf)+m(ga)+sg(gf-ga)+'</tr>';
        if(!open1[kS])return;
        g.forEach(l=>{
          const i=(EV.budget||[]).indexOf(l);
          h+='<tr class="lv3"><td>'+caret("",false)+
            (wr&&i>=0?'<span class="ediT" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-pl="'+i+
              '" data-pf="name" title="Click to edit the name">'+esc(l.name)+'</span>':esc(l.name))+
            (l.calc?' <small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+(l.qty||1)+
              (l.qty2?"×"+l.qty2:"")+"×"+f(l.price)+'</small>':'')+'</td>'+
            '<td class="num">'+(wr&&i>=0
              ?'<span class="inl ediT" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-pl="'+i+'" data-pf="fc" '+
                'title="'+(l.calc?"Calculated from quantity — type over it to set your own total":"Click to edit the budget amount")+'">'+
                f(lineTotal(l))+'</span>':f(lineTotal(l)))+'</td>'+
            '<td class="num">'+(wr&&i>=0
              ?'<span class="inl ediT" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-pl="'+i+'" data-pf="ac" '+
                'title="Click to enter actual spend">'+(l.ac?f(l.ac):"—")+'</span>'
              :(l.ac?f(l.ac):"—"))+'</td>'+
            sg(lineTotal(l)-(l.ac||0))+'</tr>';
        });
        if(wr)h+='<tr class="add lv3"><td colspan="4"><button data-pa="'+esc(c)+'|'+esc(sb)+
          '">+ Item in '+esc(sb)+'</button></td></tr>';
      });
      if(wr)h+='<tr class="add lv2"><td colspan="4"><button data-pg="'+esc(c)+
        '">+ Sub-category in '+esc(c)+'</button></td></tr>';
    });
    h+='<tr class="lv1"><td>'+caret("",false)+'Build cost of sold booths</td>'+
      '<td class="num">—</td>'+m(T.boothCost)+'<td class="num">—</td></tr>';
    if(wr)h+='<tr class="add lv1"><td colspan="4"><button id="pnewcat">+ Main category</button></td></tr>';
  }

  h+='<tr class="lv0"><td>'+caret("",false)+'Gross profit</td>'+m(gpPlan)+sg(gpNow)+
   sg(gpNow-gpPlan)+'</tr>'+
   '<tr class="lv0"><td>'+caret("",false)+'Gross margin</td>'+
   '<td class="num">'+(Math.round(mgPlan*10)/10)+'%</td>'+
   '<td class="num" style="color:'+(mgNow<0?cv("--risk"):cv("--ok"))+'">'+
     (Math.round(mgNow*10)/10)+'%</td><td class="num">—</td></tr>'+
   '</tbody></table></div>';

  const mm=rows.flatMap(r=>r.mismatch||[]);


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const P=$("#page");
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-a]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    pnlAll=b.dataset.a==="1"; pPnl();}));
  $("#dp3").addEventListener("change",e=>{dep=+e.target.value;pPnl()});
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-e2]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const k=b.dataset.e2; if(open1[k])delete open1[k]; else open1[k]=1; pPnl();}));
  $("#expall").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(Object.keys(open1).length){open1={};}
    else{open1["Revenue"]=1; open1["Expenses"]=1;
      EVENTS.forEach(e=>open1["Revenue\u203a"+e.id]=1);
      Object.keys(allCats).forEach(c=>{open1["Expenses›"+c]=1;
        ((pnlAll?EVENTS.flatMap(e=>e.budget||[]):(EV.budget||[]))).filter(l=>l.cat===c)
          .forEach(l=>open1["Expenses›"+c+"›"+(l.sub||"— no sub-category —")]=1);});}
    pPnl();});
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-pl]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("focus",()=>{el.dataset.was=el.textContent});
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
      if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}
      if(e.key==="Escape"){el.textContent=el.dataset.was||"";el.blur()}});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const l=EV.budget[+el.dataset.pl], fld=el.dataset.pf, t=el.textContent.trim();
      if(fld==="name"){ if(t)l.name=t; }
      else { const v=+(t.replace(/[^0-9.]/g,"")); l[fld]=v||null;
             if(fld==="fc"){l.calc=false;}
             if(fld==="ac"&&v&&(l.st||"planned")==="planned")l.st="paid"; }
      pPnl();});
  });
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-pa]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const [c,sb]=b.dataset.pa.split("|");
    EV.budget.push({cat:c,sub:sb==="— no sub-category —"?null:sb,name:"New item",
      qty:null,unit:null,qty2:null,unit2:null,price:null,calc:false,fc:0,ac:0,
      st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null});
    pPnl();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-pg]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    EV.budget.push({cat:b.dataset.pg,sub:"New sub-category",name:"New item",
      qty:null,unit:null,qty2:null,unit2:null,price:null,calc:false,fc:0,ac:0,
      st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null});
    open1["Expenses›"+b.dataset.pg+"\u203aNew sub-category"]=1; pPnl();}));
  const pc=P.querySelector("#pnewcat");
  if(pc)pc.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    EV.budget.push({cat:"New category",sub:null,name:"New item",qty:null,unit:null,
      qty2:null,unit2:null,price:null,calc:false,fc:0,ac:0,st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null});
    open1["Expenses\u203aNew category"]=1; pPnl();});
