  P.querySelector("#dlpnl").addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const out=[["Level","Item","On plan","Now","Difference"],
               ["1","Revenue",T.goal,T.con,T.con-T.goal]];
    rows.forEach(r=>out.push(["2",r.e.short,r.goal,r.income.con,r.income.con-r.goal]));
    out.push(["2","Money in the bank","",T.got,""]);
    out.push(["1","Expenses",T.exFc,T.exAc+T.boothCost,T.exFc-T.exAc-T.boothCost]);
    Object.keys(allCats).forEach(c=>{
      out.push(["2",c,allCats[c].plan,allCats[c].ac,allCats[c].plan-allCats[c].ac]);
      const lines=pnlAll?EVENTS.flatMap(e=>(e.budget||[]).filter(l=>l.cat===c))
                        :(EV.budget||[]).filter(l=>l.cat===c);
      const subs=[]; lines.forEach(l=>{const k2=l.sub||"—"; if(!subs.includes(k2))subs.push(k2)});
      subs.forEach(sb=>{const g=lines.filter(l=>(l.sub||"—")===sb);
        out.push(["3",sb,g.reduce((s,l)=>s+lineTotal(l),0),g.reduce((s,l)=>s+(l.ac||0),0),""]);
        g.forEach(l=>out.push(["4",l.name,lineTotal(l),l.ac||0,lineTotal(l)-(l.ac||0)]));});
    });
    out.push(["2","Build cost of sold booths","",T.boothCost,""]);
    out.push(["1","Gross profit",gpPlan,gpNow,gpNow-gpPlan]);
    saveCSV((pnlAll?"pnl-all-events":"pnl-"+EV.id)+".csv",out,e.currentTarget);
  });
}

/* ---------- งบรายจ่าย ทำงบได้จริง ----------
   โครงตามชีต Feasibility — หมวดหลัก > หมวดย่อย > รายการ
   แต่ละรายการมี จำนวน x หน่วย x ราคาต่อหน่วย = ตั้งงบ แล้วเทียบกับจ่ายจริง */
const CATC=["--accent","--s-deposit","--s-booked","--zN","--zF","--zH","--zA","--zE"];
const catColor=(cats,c)=>cv(CATC[cats.indexOf(c)%CATC.length]);
const BST=[["planned","Budgeted","--ink3"],["quoted","Has a quote","--s-booked"],
 ["committed","Agreed","--s-deposit"],["paid","Paid","--s-paid"]];
const FILES={};
let catOpen={}, costOpen=null;
const lineKey=l=>l.cat+"|"+(l.sub||"")+"|"+l.name;
/* ชีตมีตัวคูณสองชั้น เช่น 1 ตัว x 3 วัน x 2,000
   บรรทัดที่ตรวจแล้วว่า จำนวน x จำนวน2 x ราคา ตรงกับยอดในชีต ถึงจะคำนวณสด
   ที่เหลือใช้ยอดที่ชีตเขียนไว้ เพราะบางบรรทัดจำนวนเป็นข้อมูลประกอบ ไม่ใช่ตัวคูณ */
function lineTotal(l){
  if(l.calc&&l.price)return Math.round((l.qty||1)*(l.qty2||1)*l.price);
  return l.fc||0;
}
function recalcLine(l){
  if(l.price&&(l.qty||l.qty2)){l.calc=true; l.fc=lineTotal(l);}
  else l.calc=false;
}
function pCost(){
  const wr=can("budget")==="write";
  const L=EV.budget||[];
  if(!L.length&&!wr){$("#page").innerHTML='<div class="deny" style="border-left-color:var(--warn)">'+
    '<h3>the budget for this event has not been imported yet</h3></div>';return}

  const fc=L.reduce((s,l)=>s+lineTotal(l),0);
  const ac=L.reduce((s,l)=>s+(l.ac||0),0);
  const cats=[]; L.forEach(l=>{if(!cats.includes(l.cat))cats.push(l.cat)});

  let h='<div class="bar2">'+dlBtn("dlcost","Download CSV")+
   (wr?'<button class="act" id="addcat">+ Main category</button>'+
      '<button class="act" id="usestd">'+(L.length?"Add the standard items that are missing":"Use standard set")+
      '</button>':'')+
   '<span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);align-self:center">'+
   (wr?'Click any cell to type over it · qty x unit price calculates the total for you'
      :'This role has view access only')+'</span></div>'+
   '<div class="cards">'+
   card(f(fc),"Budgeted","--line2")+card(f(ac),"Actually paid","--s-paid")+
   card(f(fc-ac),"left",(fc?Math.round((fc-ac)/fc*100):0)+"%","--accent")+
   card(L.filter(l=>!(FILES[lineKey(l)]||[]).length).length,"No quote yet","--risk")+
   card(L.filter(l=>!l.sup).length,"No supplier set","--warn")+'</div>';

  cats.forEach(c=>{
    const g=L.filter(l=>l.cat===c);
    const gf=g.reduce((s,l)=>s+lineTotal(l),0), ga=g.reduce((s,l)=>s+(l.ac||0),0);
    const on=catOpen[c]!==false;
    const col=catColor(cats,c);
    h+='<div class="catwrap"><div class="cathead" style="background:'+col+'">'+
      '<button class="tog" data-c="'+esc(c)+'">'+(on?"▾":"▸")+'</button>'+
      (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-cn="'+esc(c)+'">'+esc(c)+'</span>'
        :'<span>'+esc(c)+'</span>')+
      '<span class="sum"><span>Set <b>'+f(gf)+'</b></span>'+
      '<span>Actual spend <b>'+f(ga)+'</b></span>'+
      '<span>'+(gf-ga>=0?"left ":"over ")+'<b>'+f(Math.abs(gf-ga))+'</b></span>'+
      '<span>'+g.length+' Item</span></span></div>';
    if(!on){h+='</div>';return}
    const subs=[]; g.forEach(l=>{const k=l.sub||""; if(!subs.includes(k))subs.push(k)});
    h+='<div class="tw"><table style="min-width:1000px">'+
     '<thead><tr><th style="width:250px">Items</th><th class="num" style="width:66px">Qty</th>'+
     '<th style="width:66px">Unit</th><th class="num" style="width:66px">x</th>'+
     '<th style="width:66px">Unit</th><th class="num" style="width:104px">Price/unit</th>'+
     '<th class="num" style="width:118px">Budgeted</th><th class="num" style="width:118px">Actual spend</th>'+
     '<th class="num" style="width:104px">Difference</th><th style="width:126px">Status</th>'+
     '<th style="width:140px">Supplier</th><th style="width:74px">Document</th>'+
     (wr?'<th style="width:44px"></th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>';
    subs.forEach(sb=>{
      const rows=g.filter(l=>(l.sub||"")===sb);
      if(sb){const sf=rows.reduce((x,l)=>x+lineTotal(l),0), sa=rows.reduce((x,l)=>x+(l.ac||0),0);
        h+='<tr class="subrow"><td colspan="6" style="border-left:3px solid '+col+'">'+
        (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-sn="'+esc(c)+'|'+esc(sb)+'">'+esc(sb)+
          '</span>':esc(sb))+'</td>'+
        '<td class="num">'+f(sf)+'</td><td class="num">'+f(sa)+'</td>'+
        '<td class="num"'+(sf-sa<0?' class="overrun"':'')+'>'+f(sf-sa)+'</td>'+
        '<td colspan="'+(wr?4:3)+'"></td></tr>';}
      rows.forEach(l=>{
        const i=L.indexOf(l), k=lineKey(l), tot=lineTotal(l), d=tot-(l.ac||0);
        const st=BST.find(x=>x[0]===(l.st||"planned"));
        // ตัวเลขแสดงมีจุลภาค ตอนแก้ตัวจัดการ blur ตัดอักขระที่ไม่ใช่เลขออกอยู่แล้ว
        const num2=fld=>["qty","qty2","price","fc","ac"].includes(fld);
        const ed=(v,fld,cls)=>{
          const show=v==null||v===""?"":(num2(fld)?f(v):v);
          return wr?'<span'+(cls?' class="'+cls+'"':'')+
            ' contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-l="'+i+'" data-lf="'+fld+'">'+esc(show)+
            '</span>':esc(show||"—");
        };
        h+='<tr><td>'+ed(l.name,"name")+'</td>'+
         '<td class="num">'+ed(l.qty,"qty","inl")+'</td>'+
         '<td>'+ed(l.unit,"unit")+'</td>'+
         '<td class="num">'+ed(l.qty2,"qty2","inl")+'</td>'+
         '<td>'+ed(l.unit2,"unit2")+'</td>'+
         '<td class="num">'+ed(l.price,"price","inl")+'</td>'+
         '<td class="num">'+(l.calc?'<span style="color:var(--ink2)" title="Calculated from quantity">'+
           f(tot)+'</span>':ed(l.fc,"fc","inl"))+'</td>'+
         '<td class="num">'+ed(l.ac||"","ac","inl")+'</td>'+
         '<td class="num" style="color:'+(d<0?cv("--risk"):"var(--ink2)")+'">'+
           (l.ac?f(d):"—")+'</td>'+
         '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-l="'+i+'" data-lf="st">'+BST.map(x=>
           '<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+((l.st||"planned")===x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+
           '</option>').join("")+'</select>'
           :'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(st[2])+'">'+st[1]+'</span>')+'</td>'+
         '<td style="font-size:12.5px">'+ed(l.sup,"sup")+
           (l.phone?'<br><small style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(l.phone)+'</small>':'')+'</td>'+
         '<td><button class="act" data-fl="'+i+'" style="padding:4px 9px;font-size:12px">'+
           ((FILES[k]||[]).length?(FILES[k].length+" File"):"Attach")+'</button></td>'+
         (wr?'<td><button class="rowdel" data-lx="'+i+'">×</button></td>':'')+'</tr>';
      });
    });
    h+='</tbody></table></div>';
    if(wr)h+='<div class="addbar">'+
      '<button data-al="'+esc(c)+'">+ Item</button>'+
      '<button data-ag="'+esc(c)+'">+ Sub-category</button></div>';
    h+='</div>';
  });


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const P=$("#page");
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-c]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    catOpen[b.dataset.c]=catOpen[b.dataset.c]===false; pCost();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[contenteditable][data-l]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const l=L[+el.dataset.l], fld=el.dataset.lf, t=el.textContent.trim();
      if(["qty","qty2","price","fc","ac"].includes(fld)){
        const v=+(t.replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
        l[fld]= v||null;
        if(fld==="ac"&&v&&(l.st||"planned")==="planned")l.st="paid";
        if(["qty","qty2","price"].includes(fld))recalcLine(l);
        if(fld==="fc"){l.calc=false;}          // พิมพ์ยอดเองแปลว่าไม่คำนวณจากจำนวน
      } else l[fld]=t||null;
      pCost();
    });
  });
  P.querySelectorAll("select.cell[data-l]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    L[+el.dataset.l].st=el.value; pCost();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-cn]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(), old=el.dataset.cn;
    if(t&&t!==old){L.forEach(l=>{if(l.cat===old)l.cat=t}); pCost();}}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-sn]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const [c,sb]=el.dataset.sn.split("|"), t=el.textContent.trim();
    if(t&&t!==sb){L.forEach(l=>{if(l.cat===c&&(l.sub||"")===sb)l.sub=t}); pCost();}}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-lx]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){L.splice(+b.dataset.lx,1);pCost();return}
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="×";b.style.color=""}},4000);
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-al]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const c=b.dataset.al;
    const last=[...L].reverse().find(l=>l.cat===c);
    L.push({cat:c,sub:last?last.sub:null,name:"New item",qty:null,unit:null,price:null,
      fc:0,ac:0,st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null});
    pCost();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-ag]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    L.push({cat:b.dataset.ag,sub:"New sub-category",name:"New item",qty:null,unit:null,
      price:null,fc:0,ac:0,st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null});
    pCost();}));
  const us=P.querySelector("#usestd");
  if(us)us.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    // เทียบด้วยชื่อรายการอย่างเดียว เพราะงานเดิมตั้งชื่อหมวดย่อยไม่เหมือนกัน
    const key=l=>String(l.name||"").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9ก-๙]/g,"");
    const have=new Set(L.map(key));
    const add=(D.template||[]).filter(t=>!have.has(key(t)));
    if(!add.length){us.textContent="Already complete";setTimeout(()=>pCost(),1500);return}
    if(!us.dataset.armed){
      us.dataset.armed="1";
      us.textContent="Confirm and add "+add.length+" Item";
      us.style.color=cv("--accent");
      setTimeout(()=>{if(us.dataset.armed){delete us.dataset.armed;pCost()}},5000);
      return;
    }
    add.forEach(t=>L.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(t))));
    EV.budget=L; pCost();
  });
  const ac2=P.querySelector("#addcat");
  if(ac2)ac2.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    L.push({cat:"New category",sub:null,name:"New item",qty:null,unit:null,price:null,
      fc:0,ac:0,st:"planned",sup:null,phone:null});
    pCost();});
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-fl]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    costOpen=+b.dataset.fl; drawCostPanel();}));
  P.querySelector("#dlcost").addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const out=[["Category","Sub-category","Item","Qty","Unit","Qty2","Unit2","Price/unit",
                "Budgeted","Actual spend","Difference","Status","Supplier","Phone","Attachment"]];
    L.forEach(l=>{const t=lineTotal(l),st=BST.find(x=>x[0]===(l.st||"planned"));
      out.push([l.cat,l.sub||"",l.name,l.qty||"",l.unit||"",l.qty2||"",l.unit2||"",l.price||"",t,l.ac||0,
                t-(l.ac||0),st[1],l.sup||"",l.phone||"",(FILES[lineKey(l)]||[]).length]);});
    out.push(["Total","","","","","","","",fc,ac,fc-ac,"","","",""]);
    saveCSV("expense-budget-"+EV.id+".csv",out,e.currentTarget);
  });
}
function drawCostPanel(){
  let sd=document.getElementById("costpanel");
  if(!sd){sd=document.createElement("aside");sd.id="costpanel";sd.className="side2";document.body.appendChild(sd)}
  const L=EV.budget||[], l=L[costOpen];
  if(!l){sd.remove();return}
  const k=lineKey(l), fl=FILES[k]||[];
  sd.innerHTML='<button class="x" aria-label="Off">×</button>'+
   '<h3>'+esc(l.name)+'</h3><p class="s">'+esc(l.cat)+(l.sub?' · '+esc(l.sub):'')+'</p>'+
   '<dl class="kv"><dt>Budgeted</dt><dd>'+f(lineTotal(l))+'</dd>'+
   '<dt>Actual spend</dt><dd>'+f(l.ac||0)+'</dd></dl>'+
   '<div class="acts">'+
   '<label style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink2)">Supplier phone</label>'+
   '<input id="csp" value="'+esc(l.phone||"")+'" placeholder="Contact number">'+
   '<button class="act go" id="csave">Save phone</button>'+
   '<div class="lgbtns" style="margin-top:4px"><label>Attach quote'+
     '<input type="file" id="cup" accept="image/*,.pdf"></label></div>'+
   '<p class="simnote">attachments work — images show a preview, and the file stays on this page only '+
   'in production these are stored on the server and linked to the budget line</p></div>'+
   (fl.length?'<ul class="ql">'+fl.map((x,i)=>'<li><b>'+(i+1)+'</b><span>'+
     (x.url&&x.url.startsWith("data:image")?'<img src="'+x.url+'" alt="" style="width:100%;'+
       'max-height:150px;object-fit:contain;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:7px;'+
       'background:#fff;display:block;margin-bottom:4px">':'')+esc(x.name)+'</span></li>').join("")+'</ul>'
    :'<p style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:13px;margin:12px 0 0;padding-top:10px;'+
     'border-top:1px solid var(--line)">No attachment</p>');
  sd.querySelector(".x").addEventListener("click",()=>{costOpen=null;sd.remove()});
  sd.querySelector("#csave").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    l.phone=sd.querySelector("#csp").value.trim()||null; pCost(); drawCostPanel();});
  sd.querySelector("#cup").addEventListener("change",e=>{
    const f0=e.target.files[0]; if(!f0)return;
    if(f0.size>1200000){alert("File is larger than 1.2MB");return}
    const r=new FileReader();
    r.onload=()=>{(FILES[k]=FILES[k]||[]).push({name:f0.name,url:r.result});
      if((l.st||"planned")==="planned")l.st="quoted"; pCost(); drawCostPanel();};
    r.readAsDataURL(f0);});
}

/* ---------- ภาพรวมการขาย ---------- */
/* น้ำหนักตามขั้น ใช้ประมาณยอดที่น่าจะปิดได้จริง ไม่ใช่ยอดท่อดิบ */
const PROB={lead:.1,booking:.3,quoted:.5,confirmed:.8,billed:.9,paid:1,lost:0};
function pSalesMon(){
  const list=DEALS.filter(mine);
  const open=list.filter(d=>!["paid","lost"].includes(d.stage));
  const pipe=open.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0);
  const weighted=open.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list*(PROB[d.stage]||0),0);
  const won=list.filter(d=>d.stage==="paid").reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0);
  const todo=open.filter(d=>overdue(d)||noNext(d)||isStale(d));
  const seeMoney=can("budget")!=="none"||ROLE[role].scope==="all";

  let h='<div class="cards">'+
   card(list.length,"Deals owned",ROLE[role].scope==="own"?"Yours only":"Whole team","--line2")+
   card(open.length,"Still open","--s-booked")+
   card(f(pipe),"Pipeline value","--s-deposit")+
   card(f(weighted),"Expected to close","Weighted by stage","--accent")+
   card(f(won),"Won","--s-paid")+
   card(todo.length,"Touch today",todo.filter(overdue).length+" Overdue by","--risk")+
   '</div>';

  const byFC=FC.map(x=>({l:x[1],c:cv(x[2]),
    v:open.filter(d=>fcOf(d)===x[0]).reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0),
    n:open.filter(d=>fcOf(d)===x[0]).length}));
  h+='<h2 class="sec">Forecast — how firm is the rep’s commitment</h2><div class="cards">'+
   byFC.map(x=>'<div class="c" style="--t:'+x.c+'"><b>'+f(x.v)+'</b><span>'+x.l+
     '</span><small>'+x.n+' Deal</small></div>').join("")+'</div>';

  // มูลค่าตามขั้น
  const byStage=STAGES.filter(x=>x[0]!=="lost").map(x=>({
    l:x[1],c:cv(x[2]),n:list.filter(d=>d.stage===x[0]).length,
    v:list.filter(d=>d.stage===x[0]).reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0)}));
  const mx=Math.max(...byStage.map(x=>x.v))||1;
  h+='<h2 class="sec">Pipeline by value</h2><div class="w"><div class="body">'+
   byStage.map(x=>'<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;margin-bottom:9px">'+
     '<span style="width:110px;font-size:13.5px;flex:0 0 auto">'+x.l+'</span>'+
     '<div style="flex:1;height:22px;background:var(--line);border-radius:6px;overflow:hidden">'+
       '<div style="width:'+(x.v/mx*100)+'%;height:100%;background:'+x.c+'"></div></div>'+
     '<span style="width:56px;text-align:right;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)">'+x.n+' Deal</span>'+
     '<span style="width:110px;text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;font-weight:600">'+
       f(x.v)+'</span></div>').join("")+
   '</div></div>';

  // เซลล์แต่ละคนถืออะไรอยู่
  if(ROLE[role].scope==="all"){
    const reps=[...new Set(list.map(d=>d.sales||"—"))].map(n=>{
      const g=list.filter(d=>(d.sales||"—")===n);
      const o=g.filter(d=>!["paid","lost"].includes(d.stage));
      return {n,deals:g.length,open:o.length,pipe:o.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0),
        w:o.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list*(PROB[d.stage]||0),0),
        todo:o.filter(d=>overdue(d)||noNext(d)||isStale(d)).length};
    }).sort((a,b)=>b.pipe-a.pipe);
    h+='<h2 class="sec">What each rep is holding</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>'+
     '<th>Sales</th><th class="num">Deals</th><th class="num">Still open</th>'+
     '<th class="num">Pipeline value</th><th class="num">Expected to close</th>'+
     '<th class="num">Must touch</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
     reps.map(r=>'<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+
       avatar(r.n,26)+'<b>'+esc(r.n)+'</b></span></td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+r.deals+'</td><td class="num">'+r.open+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(r.pipe)+'</td><td class="num">'+f(r.w)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num" style="color:'+(r.todo?cv("--risk"):"inherit")+'">'+r.todo+'</td></tr>').join("")+
     '</tbody></table></div>';
  }

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Touch first</h2>';
  h+=todo.length?'<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Client</th><th>Stage</th>'+
    '<th>Sales</th><th class="num">Value</th><th>Reason</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
    todo.slice(0,15).map(d=>{const l=lastAct(d);
      const why=overdue(d)?'<span style="color:var(--risk)">Overdue by '+daysAgo(d.nextDate)+' day</span>'
        :noNext(d)?'<span style="color:var(--warn)">No next step set</span>'
        :l?'<span style="color:var(--warn)">quiet '+daysAgo(l.date)+' day</span>'
        :'<span style="color:var(--warn)">No contact has ever been logged</span>';
      return '<tr data-d="'+d.id+'" style="cursor:pointer"><td><b>'+esc(d.co)+'</b></td>'+
       '<td><span class="pill" style="background:'+stCol(d.stage)+'">'+stName(d.stage)+'</span></td>'+
       '<td>'+esc(d.sales||"—")+'</td><td class="num">'+f(d.list)+'</td>'+
       '<td>'+why+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+'</tbody></table></div>'
    +(todo.length>15?'<p class="rdonly">Showing 15 of '+todo.length+' items — see them all in the working grid</p>':'')
   :'<div class="note"><p>Nothing outstanding</p></div>';

  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-d]").forEach(tr=>
    tr.addEventListener("click",()=>openDeal(+tr.dataset.d)));
}

/* ---------- ตารางทำงาน แก้ในช่องได้ เพิ่มดีลได้ ---------- */
let gRep="", gStage="", gPkg="", gQ="";
function pGrid(){
  const wr=can("deal")==="write";
  let list=DEALS.filter(mine);
  const q2=gQ.trim().toLowerCase();
  list=list.filter(d=>(!gRep||(d.sales||"—")===gRep)&&(!gStage||d.stage===gStage)
    &&(!gPkg||d.pkg===gPkg)&&(!q2||(d.co||"").toLowerCase().includes(q2)));
  const own=EV.tasks.filter(t=>t.by==="exhibitor");

  let h='<div class="bar2">'+
   '<input type="search" id="gq" placeholder="Search client name" value="'+esc(gQ)+'">'+
   (ROLE[role].scope==="all"?'<select id="grep"><option value="">All reps</option>'+
     REPS.filter(r=>r.active).map(r=>'<option value="'+esc(r.name)+'"'+
       (gRep===r.name?" selected":"")+'>'+esc(r.name)+'</option>').join("")+'</select>':'')+
   '<select id="gst"><option value="">Every stage</option>'+STAGES.map(x=>
     '<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+(gStage===x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   '<select id="gpk"><option value="">All packages</option>'+Object.keys(EV.packages||{}).map(k=>
     '<option value="'+esc(k)+'"'+(gPkg===k?" selected":"")+'>'+esc(k)+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   ((gRep||gStage||gPkg||gQ)?'<button class="act" id="gclr" style="padding:9px 14px">Clear</button>':'')+
   '<span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);align-self:center">'+
   list.length+' deals · '+f(list.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0))+'</span></div>';

  h+='<div class="tw" style="max-height:66vh;overflow:auto"><table style="min-width:1180px">'+
   '<thead><tr><th style="width:210px">Client</th><th style="width:150px">Package</th>'+
   '<th style="width:130px">Booths</th><th class="num" style="width:110px">Value</th>'+
   '<th style="width:140px">Stage</th><th style="width:120px">Sales</th>'+
   '<th style="width:150px">Forecast</th>'+
   '<th style="width:190px">Next step</th><th style="width:120px">Exhibitor tasks</th>'+
   (wr?'<th style="width:52px"></th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+
   list.map(d=>{
     const done=own.filter(t=>d.tasks[t.code]).length;
     const cell=(v,f2,ph)=>wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-g="'+d.id+
       '" data-gf="'+f2+'" data-ph="'+esc(ph||"")+'">'+esc(v||"")+'</span>':esc(v||"—");
     return '<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">'+logoTile(d.co,24)+
       cell(d.co,"co","Client name")+'</span></td>'+
      '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-g="'+d.id+'" data-gf="pkg">'+
        '<option value="">—</option>'+Object.keys(EV.packages||{}).map(k=>
        '<option value="'+esc(k)+'"'+(d.pkg===k?" selected":"")+'>'+esc(k)+'</option>').join("")+
        '</select>':esc(d.pkg||"—"))+'</td>'+
      '<td style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(d.booths.slice(0,3).join(", "))+
        (d.booths.length>3?" +"+(d.booths.length-3):"")+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+(wr?'<span class="inl" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-g="'+d.id+
        '" data-gf="list">'+f(d.list)+'</span>':f(d.list))+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-g="'+d.id+'" data-gf="stage">'+STAGES.map(x=>
        '<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+(d.stage===x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+
        '</select>':stName(d.stage))+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+(wr&&ROLE[role].scope==="all"?'<select class="cell" data-g="'+d.id+'" data-gf="sales">'+
        REPS.filter(r=>r.active).map(r=>'<option value="'+esc(r.name)+'"'+
        (d.sales===r.name?" selected":"")+'>'+esc(r.name)+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
        :esc(d.sales||"—"))+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-g="'+d.id+'" data-gf="fc">'+FC.map(x=>
        '<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+(fcOf(d)===x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+
        '</select>':fcName(fcOf(d)))+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+cell(d.next,"next","No next step")+
        (d.nextDate?'<br><small style="color:'+(overdue(d)?cv("--risk"):"var(--ink3)")+'">'+
          esc(d.nextDate)+'</small>':'')+'</td>'+
      '<td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px"><div class="prog" style="min-width:52px">'+
        '<i style="width:'+(done/own.length*100)+'%;background:'+cv("--ok")+'"></i></div>'+
        '<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink2)">'+done+'/'+own.length+'</span></div></td>'+
      (wr?'<td><button class="rowdel" data-gx="'+d.id+'">×</button></td>':'')+'</tr>'}).join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>';

  if(wr)h+='<div class="addbar">'+
   '<input id="newco" placeholder="New client name" style="flex:0 0 220px;padding:9px 12px;'+
     'border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink)">'+
   '<select id="newpk"><option value="">Choose a package</option>'+
     Object.keys(EV.packages||{}).map(k=>'<option value="'+esc(k)+'">'+esc(k)+'</option>').join("")+
     '</select>'+
   '<button class="act go" id="gadd" style="padding:9px 18px">Add deal</button>'+
   '<span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);align-self:center">'+
   'New deals start at stage "Not contacted" Booths can be linked later from the floor plan</span></div>';


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const P=$("#page");
  P.querySelector("#gq").addEventListener("input",e=>{gQ=e.target.value;pGrid();
    const el=$("#gq"); if(el){el.focus();el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length,el.value.length)}});
  const gr=P.querySelector("#grep");
  if(gr)gr.addEventListener("change",e=>{gRep=e.target.value;pGrid()});
  P.querySelector("#gst").addEventListener("change",e=>{gStage=e.target.value;pGrid()});
  P.querySelector("#gpk").addEventListener("change",e=>{gPkg=e.target.value;pGrid()});
  const gc=P.querySelector("#gclr");
  if(gc)gc.addEventListener("click",()=>{gRep=gStage=gPkg=gQ="";pGrid()});

  P.querySelectorAll("[contenteditable][data-g]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const d=DEALS.find(x=>x.id===+el.dataset.g); if(!d)return;
      const f2=el.dataset.gf, t=el.textContent.trim();
      if(f2==="list"){const v=+(t.replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")); if(v)d.list=v;}
      else if(f2==="next"){ d.next=t||null; if(t&&!d.nextDate)d.nextDate=ymd(new Date()); }
      else if(t)d[f2]=t;
      pGrid();
    });
  });
  P.querySelectorAll("select.cell[data-g]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const d=DEALS.find(x=>x.id===+el.dataset.g); if(!d)return;
    const f2=el.dataset.gf;
    d[f2]=el.value||null;
    if(f2==="sales")B.forEach(b=>{if(d.booths.includes(b.code))b.sales=el.value});
    if(f2==="pkg"){const pk=(EV.packages||{})[el.value];
      if(pk&&!d.booths.length)d.list=pk.price;}
    pGrid();
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-gx]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){
      const i=DEALS.findIndex(x=>x.id===+b.dataset.gx);
      if(i>=0){const d=DEALS[i];
        B.forEach(x=>{if(d.booths.includes(x.code)){x.st="free";x.co=null;x.sales=null}});
        DEALS.splice(i,1);}
      pGrid(); return}
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="×";b.style.color=""}},4000);
  }));
  const ga=P.querySelector("#gadd");
  if(ga)ga.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const co=P.querySelector("#newco").value.trim();
    if(!co){P.querySelector("#newco").focus();return}
    const pk=P.querySelector("#newpk").value;
    const tasks={}; EV.tasks.forEach(t=>tasks[t.code]=false);
    DEALS.push({id:Math.max(0,...DEALS.map(d=>d.id))+1, co, sales:me, pkg:pk||null,
      booths:[], list:pk?(EV.packages[pk].price||0):0, st:"booked", stage:"lead",
      contact:null, phone:null, form:false, tasks, acts:[], next:null, nextDate:null});
    gQ=""; pGrid();
  });
}


/* ---------- หมวดพยากรณ์ ----------
   ขั้นตอนบอกว่าดีลไปถึงไหน หมวดพยากรณ์บอกว่าเซลล์กล้ารับปากแค่ไหน
   สองอย่างนี้ไม่เหมือนกัน ดีลที่ส่งใบเสนอราคาแล้วอาจยังไม่กล้า commit */
const FC=[["commit","Promised to close","--s-paid"],["best","if everything goes to plan","--s-deposit"],
 ["pipe","Unsure","--ink3"],["omit","excluded","--risk"]];
const fcName=k=>(FC.find(x=>x[0]===k)||["","—","--ink3"])[1];
const fcCol=k=>cv((FC.find(x=>x[0]===k)||["","","--ink3"])[2]);
function fcOf(d){ return d.fc||(d.stage==="paid"?"commit":d.stage==="lost"?"omit":"pipe") }

/* ---------- โอกาสขาย ---------- */
let oppPick=null;
function normCo(s){
  return String(s||"").toLowerCase().replace(/\b(co|ltd|inc|จำกัด|บริษัท|บจก|หจก)\b/g,"")
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9ก-๙]/g,"");
}
function pOpp(){
  const wr=can("deal")==="write";
  const here=new Set(DEALS.map(d=>normCo(d.co)));
  // ลูกค้าจากงานอื่นในเครือที่ยังไม่มาออกงานนี้
  const others=EVENTS.filter(e=>e.id!==EV.id);
  const rows=[];
  others.forEach(e=>e.deals.forEach(d=>{
    if(!d.co)return;
    const k=normCo(d.co);
    if(here.has(k))return;
    if(rows.some(r=>normCo(r.co)===k))return;
    rows.push({co:d.co,from:e.short,pkg:d.pkg,list:d.list,phone:d.phone,contact:d.contact});
  }));
  rows.sort((a,b)=>b.list-a.list);
  const both=EVENTS.length>1
    ? DEALS.filter(d=>others.some(e=>e.deals.some(x=>normCo(x.co)===normCo(d.co)))).length : 0;

  let h='<div class="cards">'+
   card(rows.length,"Clients from other events not yet at this one","Names you can invite right away","--accent")+
   card(both,"already exhibit at both events","--s-paid")+
   card(f(rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.list||0),0)),"Value based on what they paid at other events","--s-deposit")+
   '</div>';

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Clients from other events in the group</h2>';
  h+=rows.length?'<div class="tw" style="max-height:56vh;overflow:auto"><table>'+
   '<thead><tr><th>Company</th><th>Exhibited before</th><th>Package bought before</th>'+
   '<th class="num">Paid before</th><th>Contact</th>'+(wr?'<th></th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+
   rows.map((r,i)=>'<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+
     logoTile(r.co,26)+'<b>'+esc(r.co)+'</b></span></td>'+
    '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(r.from)+'</td>'+
    '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(r.pkg||"—")+'</td>'+
    '<td class="num">'+f(r.list)+'</td>'+
    '<td style="font-size:13px">'+(r.contact?esc(r.contact):'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">—</span>')+
      (r.phone?'<br><small style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(r.phone)+'</small>':'')+'</td>'+
    (wr?'<td><button class="act" data-op="'+i+'" style="padding:6px 12px;font-size:13px">'+
      'Create deal</button></td>':'')+'</tr>').join("")+'</tbody></table></div>'
   :'<div class="note"><p>No other event to compare against</p></div>';


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-op]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const r=rows[+b.dataset.op];
    const tasks={}; EV.tasks.forEach(t=>tasks[t.code]=false);
    DEALS.push({id:Math.max(0,...DEALS.map(d=>d.id))+1, co:r.co, sales:me,
      pkg:(EV.packages||{})[r.pkg]?r.pkg:null, booths:[],
      list:(EV.packages||{})[r.pkg]?EV.packages[r.pkg].price:(r.list||0),
      st:"booked", stage:"lead", fc:"pipe",
      contact:r.contact||null, phone:r.phone||null, form:false, tasks, acts:[],
      next:"First contact", nextDate:ymd(new Date())});
    b.textContent="Created"; b.disabled=true;
    setTimeout(pOpp,700);
  }));
}

/* ---------- ผลงานเซลล์ ---------- */
let repPick="all";
function canMove(){ return can("deal")==="write"&&ROLE[role].scope==="all" }
function repStat(name){
  const g=DEALS.filter(d=>(d.sales||"—")===name);
  const open=g.filter(d=>!["paid","lost"].includes(d.stage));
  const con=g.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list,0);
  const got=g.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.list*(d.st==="paid"?1:d.st==="deposit"?dep:0),0);
  const booths=g.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.booths.length,0);
  const rep=REPS.find(r=>r.name===name);
  return {name,rep,deals:g,open,con,got,booths,
    avg:g.length?con/g.length:0,
    cover:open.length?Math.round(open.filter(d=>!noNext(d)).length/open.length*100):null,
    stale:g.filter(isStale).length,
    comm:con*((rep?rep.rate:0)/100)};
}
function pPerf(){
  const seeMoney=ROLE[role].scope==="all";
  const names=[...new Set(DEALS.map(d=>d.sales||"—"))];
  const list=(ROLE[role].scope==="own"?[me]:names).filter(n=>names.includes(n));
  const pick=(ROLE[role].scope==="own")?me:repPick;
  const rows=(pick==="all"?list:[pick]).map(repStat);
  const T=rows.reduce((a,r)=>({con:a.con+r.con,got:a.got+r.got,booths:a.booths+r.booths,
    deals:a.deals+r.deals.length,comm:a.comm+r.comm}),{con:0,got:0,booths:0,deals:0,comm:0});

  let h='';
  if(ROLE[role].scope==="all")
    h+='<div class="bar2"><label for="rp">View performance of</label><select id="rp">'+
     '<option value="all"'+(pick==="all"?" selected":"")+'>Everyone</option>'+
     list.map(n=>'<option value="'+esc(n)+'"'+(pick===n?" selected":"")+'>'+esc(n)+
       ' · '+(KINDN[(REPS.find(r=>r.name===n)||{}).kind]||"—")+'</option>').join("")+
     '</select><label for="dp4">Deposit</label><select id="dp4">'+
     [["0.5","50%"],["0.3","30%"],["1","100%"]].map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+
       (dep==+x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select></div>';
  else h+='<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p>the sales role sees only their own performance</p></div>';

  h+='<div class="cards">'+
   card(T.deals,"Deal","--line2")+
   card(T.booths,"Booths sold","--s-deposit")+
   card(f(T.con),"Won value","--accent")+
   card(f(T.got),"Payment received","--s-paid")+
   card(f(rows.length?T.con/T.deals:0),"Average per deal","--ink2")+
   (seeMoney?card(f(T.comm),"Estimated commission","--s-booked"):"")+
   '</div>';

  if(pick==="all"){
    const real=rows.filter(r=>(r.rep||{}).kind!=="house");
    const house=rows.filter(r=>(r.rep||{}).kind==="house");
    h+='<h2 class="sec">Compare by person</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>'+
     '<th>Sales</th><th>Type</th><th class="num">Deals</th><th class="num">Booths</th>'+
     '<th class="num">Won</th><th class="num">Cash in</th><th class="num">Avg/deal</th>'+
     '<th class="num">Has next step</th><th class="num">Quiet &gt;14 days</th>'+
     (seeMoney?'<th class="num">Commission</th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+
     real.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.con-a.con).map(r=>
      '<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+avatar(r.name,28)+
        '<span><b>'+esc(r.name)+'</b>'+((r.rep&&r.rep.th)?
          '<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+esc(r.rep.th)+'</small>':'')+'</span></span></td>'+
      '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:13px">'+esc(KINDN[(r.rep||{}).kind]||"—")+
        ((r.rep&&r.rep.rate)?' · '+r.rep.rate+'%':'')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+r.deals.length+'</td><td class="num">'+r.booths+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(r.con)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num" style="color:var(--ok)">'+f(r.got)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(r.avg)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num" style="color:'+(r.cover==null?"var(--ink3)":r.cover<60?cv("--risk"):cv("--ok"))+
        '">'+(r.cover==null?"All closed":r.cover+"%")+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num" style="color:'+(r.stale?cv("--warn"):"inherit")+'">'+r.stale+'</td>'+
      (seeMoney?'<td class="num">'+f(r.comm)+'</td>':'')+'</tr>').join("")+
     '</tbody></table></div>';
    if(house.length)h+='<h2 class="sec">Booths with no commission</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>'+
     '<th>Group</th><th class="num">Deals</th><th class="num">Booths</th><th class="num">Value at list price</th>'+
     '</tr></thead><tbody>'+house.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.con-a.con).map(r=>
      '<tr><td><b>'+esc((r.rep&&r.rep.label)||r.name)+'</b>'+
        '<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+esc(r.name)+'</small></td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+r.deals.length+'</td><td class="num">'+r.booths+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(r.con)+'</td></tr>').join("")+
     '</tbody></table></div>'+
     '<p class="rdonly">These booths are company-owned or held back, so they are excluded from the commission base '+
     'but it still counts as a booth someone has to look after and it takes floor space</p>';
  }else{
    const r=rows[0];
    h+='<h2 class="sec">Deals of '+esc(r.name)+'</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr>'+
     '<th>Client</th><th>Package</th><th>Booths</th><th class="num">Value</th>'+
     '<th>Stage</th><th>Next step</th><th>Reassign to</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
     r.deals.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.list-a.list).map(d=>
      '<tr><td><b>'+esc(d.co)+'</b></td><td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(d.pkg||"—")+'</td>'+
      '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(d.booths.slice(0,4).join(", "))+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(d.list)+'</td>'+
      '<td><span class="pill" style="background:'+stCol(d.stage)+'">'+stName(d.stage)+'</span></td>'+
      '<td style="color:'+(overdue(d)?cv("--risk"):"var(--ink2)")+'">'+
        (d.next?esc(d.next)+" · "+esc(d.nextDate):"—")+'</td>'+
      '<td>'+(canMove()?'<select class="cell" data-mv="'+d.id+'">'+
        REPS.filter(r=>r.active).map(r=>'<option value="'+esc(r.name)+'"'+
          (d.sales===r.name?" selected":"")+'>'+esc(r.name)+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
        :esc(d.sales||"—"))+'</td></tr>').join("")+
     '</tbody></table></div>';
  }


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-mv]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const d=DEALS.find(x=>x.id===+el.dataset.mv); if(!d)return;
    d.sales=el.value;
    B.forEach(b=>{if(d.booths.includes(b.code))b.sales=el.value});
    pPerf();
  }));
  const rp=$("#rp");
  if(rp)rp.addEventListener("change",e=>{repPick=e.target.value;pPerf()});
  const d4=$("#dp4");
  if(d4)d4.addEventListener("change",e=>{dep=+e.target.value;pPerf()});
}

/* ---------- เป้าและ run rate ---------- */
let tOverride={};          // id งาน -> {target, date}
function booths(amt,avg,days){
  if(!avg)return "—";
  const n=amt/avg;
  if(n>=1)return (Math.round(n*10)/10)+" booths";
  return "1 booth per "+Math.round(days/n)+" day";
}
function ymd(d){                    // สร้างสตริงวันจากเวลาท้องถิ่น ไม่ผ่าน UTC
  return d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+
         String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
}
function pTarget(){
  const o=tOverride[EV.id]||{};
  const target=o.target!=null?o.target:(EV.target!=null?EV.target:
    Math.round(EV.booths.reduce((s,b)=>s+(b.list||0),0)*.64));
  const guessed=EV.target==null&&o.target==null;

  const held=EV.booths.filter(b=>b.st!=="free");
  const con=held.reduce((s,b)=>s+(b.list||0),0);
  const got=held.reduce((s,b)=>s+(b.list||0)*(b.st==="paid"?1:b.st==="deposit"?dep:0),0);
  const gap=Math.max(0,target-con);

  const today=new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const raw=o.date||EV.event_date||"";
  let show=raw, past=false, rolled=false;
  if(raw){
    const d=new Date(raw+"T00:00:00");
    if(d<today){ past=true;
      if(!o.date){ d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear()+1); show=ymd(d); rolled=true; } }
  }
  const days=show?Math.max(0,Math.ceil((new Date(show+"T00:00:00")-today)/86400000)):null;
  const perDay=days?gap/days:null;
  const avg=held.length?con/held.length:50000;
  const boothsLeft=avg?Math.ceil(gap/avg):null;

  const pctCon=target?Math.min(100,con/target*100):0, pctGot=target?Math.min(100,got/target*100):0;

  let h='<div class="bar2">'+
   '<label for="tg">Revenue target</label>'+
   '<input id="tg" type="text" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(target)+'" style="width:150px;padding:9px 12px;'+
     'border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink);text-align:right">'+
   '<label for="dt">Show day</label>'+
   '<input id="dt" type="date" value="'+esc(show)+'" style="padding:9px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line2);'+
     'border-radius:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink)">'+
   '<label for="dp2">Deposit</label><select id="dp2">'+
     [["0.5","50%"],["0.3","30%"],["1","100%"]].map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+
       (dep==+x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select></div>';

  if(!show)h+='<div class="note" style="border-left-color:var(--warn);margin-bottom:16px">'+
    '<p><b>This event has no show date yet</b> enter the show date above and the run rate is calculated for you</p></div>';
  else if(rolled)h+='<div class="note" style="border-left-color:var(--warn);margin-bottom:16px">'+
    '<p><b>the show date in the sheet is '+esc(EV.event_date)+' which has already passed</b> '+
    'so this page counts from the next edition '+esc(show)+' for now — the date can be changed in the field above</p></div>';
  if(guessed)h+='<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:16px"><p><b>This event has no target in the sheet</b> '+
    'The figure set here is the full list price less a 36% discount, the real average discount in 2026 — editable</p></div>';

  h+='<div class="cards">'+
   card(f(target),"Revenue target",EV.target_note&&!guessed?"from the Feasibility sheet":"Set manually","--accent")+
   card(f(con),"Won",Math.round(pctCon)+"% of target","--s-deposit")+
   card(f(got),"Payment received",Math.round(pctGot)+"% of target","--s-paid")+
   card(f(gap),"Still short by",gap?"":"Target reached","--s-booked")+
   (days!=null?card(days,"Days left","to "+esc(show),"--ink2"):"")+
   (perDay!=null?card(f(perDay),"to close per day","average up to the show date","--risk"):"")+
   '</div>';

  h+='<h2 class="sec">Progress against target</h2>'+
   '<div class="c" style="--t:'+cv("--accent")+';padding:20px 22px">'+
   '<div style="height:26px;background:var(--line);border-radius:7px;overflow:hidden;display:flex">'+
     '<div style="width:'+pctGot+'%;background:'+cv("--s-paid")+'"></div>'+
     '<div style="width:'+Math.max(0,pctCon-pctGot)+'%;background:'+cv("--s-deposit")+'"></div>'+
   '</div>'+
   '<div style="display:flex;gap:20px;margin-top:11px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);flex-wrap:wrap">'+
     '<span><i class="dot" style="background:'+cv("--s-paid")+'"></i>Payment received '+f(got)+'</span>'+
     '<span><i class="dot" style="background:'+cv("--s-deposit")+'"></i>Won but not fully paid '+f(con-got)+'</span>'+
     '<span><i class="dot" style="background:'+cv("--line")+'"></i>Short by '+f(gap)+'</span>'+
   '</div></div>';

  if(days){
    h+='<h2 class="sec">how much has to be done to make it</h2><div class="tw"><table>'+
     '<thead><tr><th>Time slot</th><th class="num">Must close</th><th>Compare by booth</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
     [["per day",1],["per week",7],["per month",30]].map(([l,n])=>{
       const amt=perDay*n; if(n>days)return "";
       return '<tr><td>'+l+'</td><td class="num"><b>'+f(amt)+'</b></td>'+
        '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+booths(amt,avg,n)+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+
     '<tr><td>up to the show date</td><td class="num"><b>'+f(gap)+'</b></td>'+
       '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+(boothsLeft!=null?boothsLeft+" booths":"—")+'</td></tr>'+
     '</tbody></table></div>'+
     '<p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);margin:10px 0 0">'+
     'Based on the average price per booth already won '+f(avg)+' THB, with booths still free '+
     EV.booths.filter(b=>b.st==="free").length+' Field</p>';
  }


  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const save=()=>{tOverride[EV.id]={
    target:+($("#tg").value.replace(/[^0-9]/g,""))||0, date:$("#dt").value};pTarget()};
  $("#tg").addEventListener("change",save);
  $("#dt").addEventListener("change",save);
  $("#dp2").addEventListener("change",e=>{dep=+e.target.value;pTarget()});
}

/* ---------- Feasibility ---------- */
function pBudget(){
  const g={};
  B.forEach(b=>{const k=b.pkg||"No package set",r=g[k]||(g[k]={n:0,h:0,list:0,con:0,got:0});
    r.n++;r.list+=b.list||0;
    if(b.st!=="free"){r.h++;r.con+=b.list||0;r.got+=(b.list||0)*(b.st==="paid"?1:b.st==="deposit"?dep:0)}});
  const ORD=["Title Sponsor","Platinum Sponsor","Gold Sponsor","Silver Sponsor","Restech","Martech",
   "Equipment","Cafe","Banking/Fintech","Food","No package set"];
  const ks=Object.keys(g).sort((a,b)=>ORD.indexOf(a)-ORD.indexOf(b));
  const T=ks.reduce((a,k)=>{const r=g[k];["n","h","list","con","got"].forEach(x=>a[x]+=r[x]);return a},
   {n:0,h:0,list:0,con:0,got:0});
  const cost=B.filter(x=>x.st!=="free").reduce((s,x)=>s+((x.pkg==="Food"?1800:2500)+750),0);
  $("#page").innerHTML='<div class="bar2"><label for="dp">Deposit rate</label>'+
   '<select id="dp"><option value="0.5">50% (standard)</option><option value="0.3">30%</option><option value="1">100%</option></select>'+
   '<span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)">Every number on this page is calculated from the floor plan — nothing is typed in by hand</span>'+
   dlBtn("dlinc","Download CSV")+'</div>'+
   '<div class="cards" style="margin-bottom:16px">'+card(f(T.list),"List price, whole plan","--line2")+
   card(f(T.con),"Won","--accent")+card(f(T.got),"Payment received","--s-paid")+
   card(f(T.con-T.got),"Outstanding","--s-booked")+'</div>'+
   '<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Package</th><th class="num">Booths</th><th class="num">On hold</th>'+
   '<th class="num">List price</th><th class="num">Won</th><th class="num">Cash in</th><th class="num">Outstanding</th>'+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>'+ks.map(k=>{const r=g[k];
     return '<tr><td>'+esc(k)+'</td><td class="num">'+r.n+'</td><td class="num">'+r.h+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(r.list)+'</td><td class="num">'+f(r.con)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num" style="color:var(--ok);font-weight:600">'+f(r.got)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num" style="color:var(--warn)">'+f(r.con-r.got)+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+
   '</tbody><tfoot><tr style="font-weight:700;border-top:2px solid var(--line2)"><td>Total</td>'+
   '<td class="num">'+T.n+'</td><td class="num">'+T.h+'</td><td class="num">'+f(T.list)+'</td>'+
   '<td class="num">'+f(T.con)+'</td><td class="num" style="color:var(--ok)">'+f(T.got)+'</td>'+
   '<td class="num" style="color:var(--warn)">'+f(T.con-T.got)+'</td></tr>'+
   '<tr><td colspan="4" style="color:var(--ink2)">Build cost of booths already sold</td>'+
   '<td class="num" style="color:var(--risk)">-'+f(cost)+'</td><td colspan="2"></td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  $("#dp").value=String(dep);
  $("#dp").addEventListener("change",e=>{dep=+e.target.value;pBudget()});
  $("#dlinc").addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const out=[["Package","booth","On hold","List price","Won","Cash in","Outstanding"]];
    ks.forEach(k=>{const r=g[k];out.push([k,r.n,r.h,r.list,r.con,r.got,r.con-r.got])});
    out.push(["Total",T.n,T.h,T.list,T.con,T.got,T.con-T.got]);
    saveCSV("revenue-by-package-"+EV.id+".csv",out,e.currentTarget);
  });
}

/* ---------- เวที ---------- */
/* รายชื่อเวทีมาจากข้อมูลของงานนั้น ไม่ฝังไว้ในโค้ด
   งานใหม่หรืองานที่เปลี่ยนชื่อเวทีจึงไม่ต้องแก้โค้ดตาม */
function SG(){
  const o=(EV.stages||[]).slice();
  SES.forEach(s=>{ if(s.stage&&!o.includes(s.stage))o.push(s.stage) });
  return o.length?o:["Main stage"];
}
const SKIND={keynote:"Keynote",panel:"Panel Talk",talk:"Talk",workshop:"Workshop",
 ceremony:"Ceremony",networking:"Networking",break:"Break",registration:"Register"};
const SCOL={keynote:"--accent",panel:"--s-deposit",talk:"--s-deposit",workshop:"--risk",
 ceremony:"--warn",networking:"--s-booked",break:"--ink3",registration:"--ink3"};
/* คำอธิบายสีของช่วงบนเวที ไม่งั้นกล่องสีไม่บอกอะไรเลย
   talk กับ panel ใช้สีเดียวกันเพราะเป็นเนื้อหาบนเวทีเหมือนกัน ต่างแค่จำนวนคนพูด */
const SLEG=[["keynote","Keynote","The headline session of the day"],
 ["talk","Talk / panel","Content sessions, one speaker or several"],
 ["workshop","Workshop","Hands-on session, usually ticketed separately"],
 ["ceremony","Ceremony","Opening, closing and awards"],
 ["networking","Networking","Networking night and matching sessions"],
 ["registration","Registration","Doors, check-in and house-keeping"],
 ["break","Break","Breaks and stage turnaround"]];
function stageLegend(){
  const used=new Set(SES.map(s=>s.kind));
  const seen=new Set();
  const rows=SLEG.filter(([k])=>{
    if(!used.has(k)&&k!=="talk")return false;
    const c=SCOL[k]; if(seen.has(c))return false; seen.add(c); return true});
  return '<div class="sleg">'+rows.map(([k,n,d])=>
    '<span class="slg" title="'+esc(d)+'"><i style="background:'+cv(SCOL[k]||"--ink3")+'"></i>'+
    esc(n)+'</span>').join("")+
    '<span class="slgh">Colour key · every block is one slot, its height is how long it runs</span></div>';
}
let stg=null, sview="cards";
const SOPT=[["","—"],["Confirm","Confirm"],["Maybe CF","Maybe CF"],["Maybe not","Maybe not"],["Cancel","Cancel"]];
function pStage(){
  if(!SES.length){$("#page").innerHTML='<div class="deny" style="border-left-color:var(--warn)">'+
    '<h3>the stage schedule for this event has not been imported yet</h3>'+
    '<p>the module is ready — it only needs the Agenda file for '+esc(EV.name)+'</p>'+
    '<p>send the sheet and it can be imported the same way as Restech x TRC 2026</p></div>';return}
  stg===null?stageList():stageOne();
}

/* เปลี่ยนชื่อเวที ต้องไล่แก้ทุกช่วงที่อ้างชื่อเดิมด้วย ไม่งั้นช่วงจะหลุดจากเวที */
function renameStage(oldName,newName){
  const n=String(newName||"").trim();
  if(!n||n===oldName)return false;
  if(SG().includes(n)){ alert("There is already a stage with that name"); return false }
  EV.stages=(EV.stages||SG()).map(x=>x===oldName?n:x);
  SES.forEach(s=>{ if(s.stage===oldName)s.stage=n });
  return true;
}
function stageList(){
  const wr=can("stage")==="write";
  $("#page").innerHTML='<h2 class="sec">Choose a stage</h2><div class="cards">'+
   SG().map((g,i)=>{
     const all=SES.filter(s=>s.stage===g);
     const real=all.filter(s=>["talk","keynote","panel","workshop"].includes(s.kind));
     const ppl=new Set(); all.forEach(s=>s.people.forEach(p=>ppl.add(p.n)));
     const bad=real.filter(s=>!s.title||!s.cf||!s.people.length).length;
     return '<div class="c stgc" style="--t:'+cv("--accent")+'">'+
      (wr?'<b class="stgn" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-sn="'+esc(g)+
          '" title="Click to rename this stage">'+esc(g)+'</b>'
         :'<b class="stgn">'+esc(g)+'</b>')+
      '<span>'+all.length+' slots · '+real.length+' slots with content · '+ppl.size+' people</span>'+
      '<small style="color:'+(bad?cv("--risk"):cv("--ok"))+'">'+
       (bad?bad+" slots not ready":"Every slot covered")+'</small>'+
      '<span class="stgb"><button class="act" data-g="'+i+'">Open stage →</button>'+
      (wr&&!all.length?'<button class="rowdel" data-sx="'+esc(g)+'">Remove</button>':'')+
      '</span></div>'}).join("")+'</div>'+
   (wr?'<div class="addbar"><button id="stgadd">+ Add stage</button></div>':'')+
   '<h2 class="sec">all three stages at once</h2>'+timelineHTML(1)+
   '<div style="margin-top:10px"><div class="seg">'+[1,2,3].map(d=>
    '<button data-d="'+d+'" aria-pressed="'+(day===d)+'">day '+d+'</button>').join("")+'</div></div>';
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-g]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{stg=+b.dataset.g;pStage()}));
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-sn]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{ if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}
      if(e.key==="Escape"){el.textContent=el.dataset.sn;el.blur()} });
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      if(!renameStage(el.dataset.sn,el.textContent))el.textContent=el.dataset.sn;
      pStage();
    });
  });
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-sx]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=b.dataset.sx;
    if(SES.some(s=>s.stage===g)){ alert("This stage still has sessions on it"); return }
    EV.stages=(EV.stages||SG()).filter(x=>x!==g); pStage();
  }));
  const sa=$("#stgadd");
  if(sa)sa.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const base="New stage"; let n=base, k=1;
    while(SG().includes(n))n=base+" "+(++k);
    EV.stages=(EV.stages||SG()).concat(n); pStage();
  });
  wireDayCal($("#page"));
  bindDay(()=>{ $("#page").innerHTML=""; pStage() });
}

function timelineHTML(){
  const T0=560,T1=1330,PX=2.3,H=(T1-T0)*PX;
  const hh=m=>String(Math.floor(m/60)).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m%60).padStart(2,"0");
  let h=stageLegend()+'<div class="pw" style="padding:0"><div class="tlh"><div>Time</div>'+
   SG().map(s=>'<div>'+s+'</div>').join("")+'</div><div class="tl" style="height:'+H+'px">'+
   '<div class="gut" style="height:'+H+'px">';
  for(let m=Math.ceil(T0/60)*60;m<=T1;m+=60)h+='<div class="hr" style="top:'+((m-T0)*PX-7)+'px">'+hh(m)+'</div>';
  h+='</div>';
  /* เส้นชั่วโมงพาดยาวทับทุกคอลัมน์ อ่านง่ายกว่ามีตัวเลขอยู่ช่องซ้ายอย่างเดียว */
  h+='<div class="hrlines">';
  for(let m=Math.ceil(T0/60)*60;m<=T1;m+=60){
    const half=m+30;
    h+='<div class="hl" style="top:'+((m-T0)*PX)+'px"></div>';
    if(half<=T1)h+='<div class="hl half" style="top:'+((half-T0)*PX)+'px"></div>';
  }
  h+='</div>';
  SG().forEach(g=>{h+='<div class="col" style="height:'+H+'px">';
    SES.filter(s=>s.day===day&&s.stage===g).forEach(s=>{
      /* ช่วงสั้นๆ กล่องเตี้ยกว่าตัวหนังสือ เลยต้องมีความสูงขั้นต่ำ
         ผลคือกล่องล้นไปทับช่วงถัดไป กดแล้วให้เด้งขึ้นหน้าสุดถึงจะอ่านออก */
      const top=(s.a-T0)*PX, real=(s.b-s.a)*PX-4, ht=Math.max(26,real);
      h+='<button class="ev'+(real<58?" evtiny":"")+'" data-ei="'+SES.indexOf(s)+
       '" style="top:'+top+'px;height:'+ht+'px;background:'+cv(SCOL[s.kind]||"--ink3")+
       '" title="Click to bring this slot to the front">'+
       '<span class="t">'+s.time+(s.min?' · '+s.min+' min':'')+'</span>'+
       '<span class="n">'+esc(s.title||"—")+'</span>'+
       (s.people.length?'<span class="fa">'+s.people.slice(0,4).map(p=>
          avatar(p.n,22,1)).join("")+
          '<span class="nm">'+esc(s.people.map(p=>
            p.n+(p.pos?" · "+p.pos:"")).join(" / "))+'</span></span>':'')+
       '</button>'});
    h+='</div>'});
  return h+'</div></div>';
}
/* กล่องในตารางเวลาซ้อนกันเพราะช่วงสั้นๆ กินที่มากกว่าเวลาจริง
   กดกล่องไหนให้กล่องนั้นขึ้นหน้าสุดและกางเต็ม กดซ้ำหรือกดที่ว่างเพื่อพับกลับ */
function wireDayCal(P){
  const box=P.querySelector(".tl"); if(!box)return;
  const shut=()=>box.querySelectorAll(".ev.open").forEach(x=>{
    x.classList.remove("open"); x.parentNode.classList.remove("hasopen")});
  box.querySelectorAll(".ev[data-ei]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",e=>{
    e.stopPropagation();
    const was=b.classList.contains("open");
    shut();
    if(was)return;
    b.classList.add("open"); b.parentNode.classList.add("hasopen");
    b.title="Click again to fold it back";
  }));
  document.addEventListener("click",shut);
  document.addEventListener("keydown",function esc(e){ if(e.key==="Escape")shut() });
}

function stageOne(){
  const g=SG()[stg], rows=SES.filter(s=>s.stage===g&&s.day===day);
  const wrS=can("stage")==="write";
  const real=rows.filter(s=>["talk","keynote","panel","workshop"].includes(s.kind));
  $("#page").innerHTML='<div class="bar2">'+
   '<button class="act" id="back">← All stages</button>'+
   (wrS?'<b class="stgn1" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-sn1="'+esc(g)+
        '" title="Click to rename this stage">'+esc(g)+'</b>'
       :'<b style="font-size:16px">'+esc(g)+'</b>')+
   '<div class="seg">'+[1,2,3].map(d=>{
     const dt=(SES.find(x=>x.day===d)||{}).date;
     const lbl=dt?dt.slice(8)+" Aug":"";
     return '<button data-d="'+d+'" aria-pressed="'+(day===d)+'">day '+d+
       (lbl?' · '+lbl:'')+'</button>'}).join("")+'</div>'+
   '<span class="sp" style="flex:1"></span>'+
   '<div class="seg"><button data-v="cards" aria-pressed="'+(sview==="cards")+'">Sessions</button>'+
   '<button data-v="table" aria-pressed="'+(sview==="table")+'">Sheet</button>'+
   '<button data-v="time" aria-pressed="'+(sview==="time")+'">Timeline</button></div></div>'+
   '<div class="cards" style="margin-bottom:14px">'+
    card(rows.length,"slots today","--line2")+
    card(real.length,"slots with content","--accent")+
    card(real.filter(s=>!s.people.length).length,"No speaker yet","--risk")+
    card(real.filter(s=>s.title&&!s.cf).length,"Topic not confirmed","--warn")+'</div>'+
   (sview==="cards"?stageCards(rows):sview==="table"?sheet(rows):oneTimeline(g));
  $("#back").addEventListener("click",()=>{stg=null;pStage()});
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-sn1]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{ if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}
      if(e.key==="Escape"){el.textContent=el.dataset.sn1;el.blur()} });
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const n=el.textContent.trim();
      if(renameStage(el.dataset.sn1,n))stg=SG().indexOf(n);
      pStage();
    });
  });
  bindDay(pStage);
  if(sview==="time")wireDayCal($("#page"));
  if(sview==="table"||sview==="cards")bindSheet();
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-v]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{sview=b.dataset.v;pStage()}));
}

/* ───────── มุมมองการ์ดของช่วงบนเวที ─────────
   ตารางเดิมมี 16 คอลัมน์ ต้องเลื่อนแนวนอนและใช้การรวมช่อง อ่านยาก
   แบบการ์ดจัดใหม่ตามลำดับที่คนถามจริง เวลา หัวข้อ พร้อมหรือยัง ใครพูด แล้วค่อยรายละเอียดหลังบ้าน */
function stageCards(rows){
  const ed=can("stage")==="write";
  if(!rows.length)return '<div class="note"><p>No slots on this day yet</p></div>'+
    (ed?'<div class="addbar"><button id="addslot">+ Add time slot</button></div>':'');
  const idx=r=>SES.indexOf(r);
  const isReal=k=>["talk","keynote","panel","workshop"].includes(k);
  const st=(v,si,fld,on,off,tip)=>ed
    ?'<button class="tog ynb" data-si="'+si+'" data-tg="'+fld+'" title="'+esc(tip)+'">'+
      '<span class="ynp'+(v?" on":"")+'">'+(v?"✓ ":"")+(v?on:off)+'</span></button>'
    :'<span class="ynp'+(v?" on":"")+'">'+(v?on:off)+'</span>';

  return '<div class="sess">'+rows.map(s=>{
    const si=idx(s), need=isReal(s.kind), ppl=s.people||[];
    const m=/^(\d{1,2}:\d{2})\s*-\s*(\d{1,2}:\d{2})/.exec(s.time||"");
    const pad=t=>{const x=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(t||"");
      return x?String(+x[1]).padStart(2,"0")+":"+x[2]:""};
    const t1=pad(m?m[1]:(s.time||"").trim()), t2=pad(m?m[2]:"");
    const warn=need&&(!s.title||!ppl.length||!s.cf);

    return '<article class="sc'+(need?"":" sc-b")+(warn?" sc-w":"")+'" style="--k:'+
      cv(SCOL[s.kind]||"--ink3")+'">'+
     '<header class="sch">'+
      '<span class="sct">'+(ed
        ? '<input type="time" value="'+t1+'" data-si="'+si+'" data-tp="a" aria-label="Start time">'+
          '<i>–</i><input type="time" value="'+t2+'" data-si="'+si+'" data-tp="b" aria-label="End time">'
        : (t1||"—")+(t2?' – '+t2:''))+'</span>'+
      '<span class="scm">'+(s.min?s.min+' min':'')+'</span>'+
      '<span class="sck">'+(ed
        ? '<select class="cell" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="-1" data-f="kind">'+
          Object.keys(SKIND).map(k=>'<option value="'+k+'"'+(s.kind===k?" selected":"")+'>'+
            SKIND[k]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
        : esc(SKIND[s.kind]||s.kind||""))+'</span>'+
      '<span class="scs">'+st(s.cf,si,"cf","Final","Draft",
        s.cf?"This topic is agreed":"Still being worked on — click to mark it final")+
        st(s.lock,si,"lock","Locked","Open",
        s.lock?"The time is fixed":"The time can still move — click to lock it")+'</span>'+
      (ed?'<button class="rowdel scx" data-del="'+si+'" title="Delete this slot">×</button>':'')+
     '</header>'+

     '<div class="scti">'+(ed
       ? '<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="-1" data-f="title" '+
         'data-ph="Untitled slot">'+esc(s.title||"")+'</span>'
       : (s.title?esc(s.title):'<span class="scmiss">No topic yet</span>'))+'</div>'+

     (need||ppl.length?'<div class="scp">'+
       (ppl.length?ppl.map((p,pi)=>'<div class="scr">'+avatar(p.n,30)+
         '<span class="scn">'+(ed
            ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" data-f="n">'+
              esc(p.n||"")+'</span>'
            :esc(p.n||"—"))+
          '<small>'+(ed
            ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" data-f="pos" '+
             'data-ph="Position">'+esc(p.pos||"")+'</span>'
            :esc(p.pos||"—"))+'</small></span>'+
         '<span class="scc">'+(ed
            ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" data-f="contact" '+
             'data-ph="No phone number">'+esc(p.contact||"")+'</span>'
            :esc(p.contact||"—"))+'</span>'+
         '<span class="scst">'+(ed
            ?'<select class="cell" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" data-f="st">'+
              SOPT.map(o=>'<option value="'+o[0]+'"'+((p.st||"")===o[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+
                o[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
            :esc(p.st||"—"))+'</span>'+
         (ed?'<button class="rowdel" data-rmp="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" title="Remove">×</button>':'')+
         '</div>').join("")
        :'<div class="scempty">'+(need?"No speaker yet":"—")+'</div>')+
       (ed?'<button class="scadd" data-addp="'+si+'">+ Add speaker</button>':'')+
      '</div>':'')+

     '<footer class="scf">'+
       '<span><b>MOD</b>'+(ed
         ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="-1" data-f="mod" data-ph="—">'+
          esc(s.mod||"")+'</span>':esc(s.mod||"—"))+'</span>'+
       '<span><b>Coordinator</b>'+(ed
         ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="-1" data-f="coord" data-ph="—">'+
          esc(s.coord||"")+'</span>':esc(s.coord||"—"))+'</span>'+
       '<span><b>Slides</b>'+(ed
         ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="-1" data-f="slides" data-ph="Not yet">'+
          esc(s.slides||"")+'</span>':esc(s.slides||"—"))+'</span>'+
       '<span><b>Script</b>'+(s.script
         ?'<a href="'+esc(s.script)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open</a>'
         :(ed?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="-1" data-f="script" '+
              'data-ph="Paste a Google Doc link">'+'</span>':'—'))+'</span>'+
     '</footer></article>'}).join("")+'</div>'+
    (ed?'<div class="addbar"><button id="addslot">+ Add time slot</button></div>':'');
}
function bindSheet(){
  const P=$("#page");
  const get=(si,pi)=>pi>=0?SES[si].people[pi]:SES[si];
  P.querySelectorAll("[contenteditable]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
      if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}
      if(e.key==="Escape"){el.textContent=el.dataset.was||"";el.blur()}
    });
    el.addEventListener("focus",()=>{el.dataset.was=el.textContent});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const si=+el.dataset.si, pi=+el.dataset.pi, f=el.dataset.f;
      const v=el.textContent.trim();
      if(f==="newp"){ if(!v)return; SES[si].people.push({n:v,real:null,pos:null,st:"",contact:null});
        pStage(); return; }
      const o=get(si,pi); if(!o)return;
      if(o[f]===(v||null))return;
      o[f]=v||null;
      if(f==="time"||f==="min"){recalc(SES[si]); pStage(); return;}
      pStage();
    });
  });
  P.querySelectorAll("select.cell").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const o=get(+el.dataset.si,+el.dataset.pi); if(!o)return;
    o[el.dataset.f]=el.value; pStage();
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tg]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const s=SES[+b.dataset.si]; s[b.dataset.tg]=!s[b.dataset.tg]; pStage();
  }));
  /* ช่องเลือกเวลา เขียนกลับเป็น 09:00-09:40 แล้วคำนวณนาทีให้เอง */
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tp]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const s=SES[+el.dataset.si]; if(!s)return;
    const box=el.closest(".tpick");
    const a=box.querySelector('[data-tp="a"]').value;
    let b=box.querySelector('[data-tp="b"]').value;
    if(!a){ pStage(); return }
    if(!b){                          // ใส่แต่เวลาเริ่ม ต่อความยาวเดิมให้
      const len=+s.min||20, t=a.split(":"), m=+t[0]*60+ +t[1]+len;
      b=String(Math.floor(m/60)%24).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m%60).padStart(2,"0");
    }
    if(b<=a){                        // เวลาจบไม่ควรก่อนเวลาเริ่ม ดันไปอีก 20 นาที
      const t=a.split(":"), m=+t[0]*60+ +t[1]+20;
      b=String(Math.floor(m/60)%24).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m%60).padStart(2,"0");
    }
    s.time=a+"-"+b; recalc(s); pStage();
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-del]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){SES.splice(+b.dataset.del,1);pStage();return}
    P.querySelectorAll("[data-del]").forEach(x=>{delete x.dataset.armed;x.textContent="×";
      x.style.color="";x.title="Delete this slot"});
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    b.title="Press again to delete";
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="×";b.style.color=""}},4000);
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-addp]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    SES[+b.dataset.addp].people.push({n:"",real:null,pos:null,st:"",contact:null}); pStage();
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-rmp]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const s=SES[+b.dataset.rmp]; if(!s)return;
    s.people.splice(+b.dataset.pi,1); pStage();
  }));
  const ns=$("#addslot");
  if(ns)ns.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=SG()[stg], last=SES.filter(x=>x.stage===g&&x.day===day).slice(-1)[0];
    const start=last?last.b:600;
    const hh=m=>String(Math.floor(m/60)).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m%60).padStart(2,"0");
    SES.push({stage:g,day:day,date:last?last.date:null,
      time:hh(start+5)+"-"+hh(start+45),a:start+5,b:start+45,min:"40",no:null,
      title:null,kind:"talk",cf:false,lock:false,mod:null,coord:null,slides:null,people:[]});
    SES.sort((x,y)=>(x.day-y.day)||(x.a-y.a));
    pStage();
  });
  const asc=$("#allscript");
  if(asc)asc.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const u=prompt("Paste the Google Doc link used as the running script for this stage all day");
    if(!u)return;
    SES.filter(x=>x.stage===SG()[stg]&&x.day===day).forEach(x=>{if(!x.script)x.script=u.trim()});
    pStage();
  });
  const add=$("#addses");
  if(add)add.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=SG()[stg], last=SES.filter(x=>x.stage===g&&x.day===day).slice(-1)[0];
    const start=last?last.b:600;
    const hh=m=>String(Math.floor(m/60)).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m%60).padStart(2,"0");
    SES.push({stage:g,day:day,date:last?last.date:null,
      time:hh(start+5)+"-"+hh(start+45),a:start+5,b:start+45,min:"40",no:null,
      title:null,kind:"talk",cf:false,lock:false,mod:null,coord:null,slides:null,people:[]});
    SES.sort((x,y)=>(x.day-y.day)||(x.a-y.a));
    pStage();
  });
}

function bindDay(fn){
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-d]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{day=+b.dataset.d;fn()}));
}
function oneTimeline(g){
  const T0=560,T1=1330,PX=2.6,H=(T1-T0)*PX;
  const hh=m=>String(Math.floor(m/60)).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m%60).padStart(2,"0");
  let h='<div class="pw" style="padding:0"><div class="tlh" style="grid-template-columns:56px 1fr;min-width:420px">'+
   '<div>Time</div><div>'+esc(g)+'</div></div>'+
   '<div class="tl" style="height:'+H+'px;grid-template-columns:56px 1fr;min-width:420px">'+
   '<div class="gut" style="height:'+H+'px">';
  for(let m=Math.ceil(T0/60)*60;m<=T1;m+=60)h+='<div class="hr" style="top:'+((m-T0)*PX-7)+'px">'+hh(m)+'</div>';
  h+='</div><div class="col" style="height:'+H+'px">';
  SES.filter(s=>s.day===day&&s.stage===g).forEach(s=>{
    const top=(s.a-T0)*PX, real=(s.b-s.a)*PX-4, ht=Math.max(26,real);
    h+='<button class="ev'+(real<62?" evtiny":"")+'" data-ei="'+SES.indexOf(s)+
     '" style="top:'+top+'px;height:'+ht+'px;background:'+cv(SCOL[s.kind]||"--ink3")+
     '" title="Click to bring this slot to the front">'+
     '<span class="t">'+s.time+(s.min?' · '+s.min+' min':'')+'</span>'+
     '<span class="n">'+esc(s.title||"—")+'</span>'+
     (s.people.length?'<span class="fa">'+s.people.slice(0,5).map(p=>avatar(p.n,26,1)).join("")+
       '<span class="nm">'+esc(s.people.map(p=>p.n+(p.pos?" · "+p.pos:"")).join(" / "))+'</span></span>':'')+
     '</button>'});
  return h+'</div></div></div>';
}

/* ตารางแบบชีต แก้ได้ในช่องเลย ช่องระดับช่วงถูก merge เมื่อมีหลายคน */
function mins2(t){const m=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/.exec(t||"");return m?+m[1]*60+ +m[2]:null}
function recalc(s){
  const a=mins2(s.time); if(a==null)return;
  s.a=a;
  const m=/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}\s*-\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/.exec(s.time);
  s.b=m?+m[1]*60+ +m[2]:a+(+s.min||20);
  if(m)s.min=String(s.b-s.a);
}
function sheet(rows){
  const ed=can("stage")==="write";
  /* ชีตเดิมเก็บเป็น TRUE/FALSE ซึ่งไม่บอกว่าหมายถึงอะไร
     เปลี่ยนเป็นป้ายที่อ่านแล้วรู้เลยว่าหัวข้อนี้สรุปแล้วหรือยัง และเวลาถูกล็อกหรือยัง */
  const YN={
    cf:{on:["Final","This topic is agreed and will not change"],
        off:["Draft","Still being worked on — click to mark it final"]},
    lock:{on:["Locked","The time slot is fixed and should not be moved"],
          off:["Open","The time can still move — click to lock it"]},
  };
  /* เวลาเดิมพิมพ์เป็นข้อความ 09:00-09:40 พิมพ์ผิดง่ายและระบบอ่านไม่ออก
     เปลี่ยนเป็นช่องเลือกเวลาสองช่อง เบราว์เซอร์มีตัวเลือกเวลาให้กดเอง
     นาทีคำนวณจากผลต่างให้เอง ไม่ต้องกรอกซ้ำ */
  const tpick=(s,si)=>{
    const m=/^(\d{1,2}:\d{2})\s*-\s*(\d{1,2}:\d{2})/.exec(s.time||"");
    const pad=t=>{const x=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(t||"");
      return x?String(+x[1]).padStart(2,"0")+":"+x[2]:""};
    const a=pad(m?m[1]:(s.time||"").trim()), b=pad(m?m[2]:"");
    if(!ed)return '<span class="tv">'+(a||"—")+(b?'<i>–</i>'+b:"")+'</span>';
    return '<span class="tpick">'+
      '<input type="time" value="'+a+'" data-si="'+si+'" data-tp="a" aria-label="Start time">'+
      '<input type="time" value="'+b+'" data-si="'+si+'" data-tp="b" aria-label="End time">'+
      '</span>';
  };
  const yn=(v,si,fld)=>{
    const w=YN[fld]||{on:["Yes",""],off:["No",""]};
    const t=v?w.on:w.off;
    const body='<span class="ynp'+(v?" on":"")+'">'+(v?"✓ ":"")+t[0]+'</span>';
    return ed?'<button class="tog ynb" data-si="'+si+'" data-tg="'+fld+'" title="'+esc(t[1])+'">'+
      body+'</button>':body;
  };
  const cell=(v,si,pi,fld,ph)=>ed
    ?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" data-f="'+fld+
      '" data-ph="'+esc(ph||"")+'">'+esc(v||"")+'</span>'
    :esc(v||"—");
  const sel=(v,si,pi,fld,opts)=>ed
    ?'<select class="cell" data-si="'+si+'" data-pi="'+pi+'" data-f="'+fld+'">'+
      opts.map(o=>'<option value="'+o[0]+'"'+(v===o[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+o[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
    :esc((opts.find(o=>o[0]===v)||["",v||"—"])[1]);
  const KOPT=Object.keys(SKIND).map(k=>[k,SKIND[k]]);
  let body="";
  rows.forEach(s=>{
    const si=SES.indexOf(s);
    const need=["talk","keynote","panel","workshop"].includes(s.kind);
    const ppl=s.people.length?s.people:[null];
    const n=ppl.length;
    ppl.forEach((p,i)=>{
      const pi=s.people.length?i:-1;
      const span=i===0?' rowspan="'+n+'"':"";
      body+='<tr>';
      if(i===0)body+=
        '<td'+span+' class="num">'+cell(s.no,si,-1,"no")+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+' class="tcell">'+tpick(s,si)+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+' class="num">'+cell(s.min,si,-1,"min")+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+'><span class="dot" style="background:'+cv(SCOL[s.kind]||"--ink3")+'"></span>'+
          sel(s.kind,si,-1,"kind",KOPT)+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+'>'+(ed?cell(s.title,si,-1,"title","No topic yet")
          :(s.title?'<b>'+esc(s.title)+'</b>':'<span style="color:var(--risk)">— no topic yet —</span>'))+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+' class="num">'+yn(s.cf,si,"cf")+'</td>';
      body+=
        '<td>'+(p?'<span style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">'+avatar(p.n,26)+
          cell(p.n,si,pi,"n")+'</span>'
          :(ed?cell("",si,-1,"newp","+ Add speaker name")
            :(need?'<span style="color:var(--risk)">No speaker yet</span>':'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">—</span>')))+'</td>'+
        '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+(p?cell(p.real,si,pi,"real"):"—")+'</td>'+
        '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+(p?cell(p.pos,si,pi,"pos"):"—")+'</td>'+
        '<td>'+(p?sel(p.st||"",si,pi,"st",SOPT):"")+'</td>'+
        '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+(p?cell(p.contact,si,pi,"contact","No phone number"):"—")+'</td>';
      if(i===0)body+=
        '<td'+span+' style="color:var(--ink2)">'+cell(s.mod,si,-1,"mod")+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+' class="num">'+yn(s.lock,si,"lock")+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+' style="color:var(--ink2)">'+cell(s.coord,si,-1,"coord")+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+'>'+cell(s.slides,si,-1,"slides","Not yet")+'</td>'+
        '<td'+span+'>'+(s.script
          ?'<a href="'+esc(s.script)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" '+
            'style="color:var(--accent);font-size:12.5px;word-break:break-all">Open script</a>'+
            (ed?'<br>'+cell(s.script,si,-1,"script","Paste a link"):'')
          :(ed?cell("",si,-1,"script","Paste a Google Doc link")
             :'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">—</span>'))+'</td>'+
        (ed?'<td'+span+'><button class="rowdel" data-del="'+si+'" title="Delete this slot">×</button>'+
            '<br><button class="rowdel" data-addp="'+si+'" title="Add person" style="font-size:13px">+Person</button></td>':'');
      body+='</tr>';
    });
  });
  const CW=[54,104,60,132,300,84,168,168,220,118,124,170,96,140,104,132];
  if(ed)CW.push(64);
  const total=CW.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
  const HD=['Que','Time','min','Build type','Topic','Topic status','Speaker (short name)','Full name',
    'Position','Status SPK','Contact','MOD','Time slot','Coordinator','Slides','MC script'];
  if(ed)HD.push("");
  return '<div class="tw" style="max-height:74vh;overflow:auto">'+
   '<table style="min-width:'+total+'px;table-layout:fixed">'+
   '<colgroup>'+CW.map(w=>'<col style="width:'+w+'px">').join("")+'</colgroup>'+
   '<thead><tr>'+HD.map((h,i)=>'<th'+([0,1,2,5,12].includes(i)?' class="num"':'')+'>'+h+'</th>').join("")+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody></table></div>'+
   (ed?'<div class="addbar"><button id="addses">+ Add time slot</button>'+
     '<button id="allscript">Add the running script link for this stage</button>'+
     '<span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2);align-self:center">'+
     'Click any cell and type over it, Tab moves to the next · edits save immediately</span></div>'
    :'<p class="rdonly">Role '+ROLE[role].n+' View only, cannot edit the schedule</p>');
}


/* ตั้งค่ารายการงานที่ผู้ออกบูธต้องส่ง แก้ได้ทั้งชื่อ กำหนดส่ง และคนที่รับผิดชอบ */
function pTaskSetup(){
  const wr=can("exhibitor")==="write";
  const T=EV.tasks;
  let h=(wr?'<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p>Editable right in the table '+
    '<b>Due dates count back from the show date</b> enter 30 and it must be submitted 30 days before the show '+
    'when the event is cloned to next year, due dates move with it</p></div>'
   :'<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:14px"><p>This role has view access only</p></div>');
  h+='<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Tasks</th><th class="num">Days before the show</th>'+
   '<th>Owner</th><th class="num">Required</th><th class="num">Submitted</th>'+(wr?'<th></th>':'')+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>'+T.map((t,i)=>{
     const n=DEALS.filter(d=>d.tasks[t.code]).length;
     return '<tr><td>'+(wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-tl="'+i+'">'+
       esc(t.label)+'</span>':esc(t.label))+
       '<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">'+esc(t.code)+' · due '+taskDue(EV,t)+'</small></td>'+
     '<td class="num">'+(wr?'<span class="inl" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-td="'+i+'">'+
       t.days+'</span>':t.days)+'</td>'+
     '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-tb2="'+i+'">'+
       '<option value="exhibitor"'+(t.by==="exhibitor"?" selected":"")+'>Exhibitor</option>'+
       '<option value="organiser"'+(t.by==="organiser"?" selected":"")+'>Our team</option></select>'
       :(t.by==="exhibitor"?"Exhibitor":"Our team"))+'</td>'+
     '<td class="num">'+(wr?'<button class="tog" data-tr="'+i+'">'+
       (t.req?'<span style="color:var(--ok);font-weight:700">Required</span>'
             :'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">Optional</span>')+'</button>'
       :(t.req?"Required":"Optional"))+'</td>'+
     '<td class="num">'+n+'/'+DEALS.length+'</td>'+
     (wr?'<td><button class="rowdel" data-tx="'+i+'">Delete</button></td>':'')+'</tr>'}).join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>'+
   (wr?'<div class="addbar"><button id="taddb">+ Add checklist item</button></div>':'');
  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const P=$("#page");
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tl]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(); if(t)T[+el.dataset.tl].label=t; pTaskSetup();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-td]").forEach(el=>{
    el.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();el.blur()}});
    el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
      const v=+(el.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")); if(v)T[+el.dataset.td].days=v; pTaskSetup();});
  });
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tb2]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    T[+el.dataset.tb2].by=el.value; pTaskSetup();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tr]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    T[+b.dataset.tr].req=!T[+b.dataset.tr].req; pTaskSetup();}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tx]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){const t=T.splice(+b.dataset.tx,1)[0];
      DEALS.forEach(d=>delete d.tasks[t.code]); pTaskSetup(); return}
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Confirm delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="Delete";b.style.color=""}},4000);
  }));
  const ab=P.querySelector("#taddb");
  if(ab)ab.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const code="task"+(T.length+1);
    T.push({code,label:"New task",phase:"asset",days:30,by:"exhibitor",req:false});
    DEALS.forEach(d=>d.tasks[code]=false);
    pTaskSetup();
  });
}

/* ---------- ดูแลผู้ออกบูธ ---------- */
let exhFilter="all", exhOpen=null, exhView="team";
/* วันงานที่ใช้คำนวณ ถ้าวันในข้อมูลผ่านมาแล้วให้เลื่อนไปรอบถัดไป
   เหมือนหน้าเป้าและ run rate จะได้ไม่ขึ้นว่าเลยกำหนดทั้งหมดโดยไม่มีความหมาย */
function effEventDate(e){
  const raw=e.eventDate||e.event_date;
  if(!raw)return {date:null,rolled:false};
  const d=new Date(raw+"T00:00:00"), t=new Date(); t.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  if(d>=t)return {date:raw,rolled:false};
  d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear()+1);
  return {date:ymd(d),rolled:true,orig:raw};
}
function taskDue(e,t){                       // กำหนดส่ง นับถอยหลังจากวันงาน
  const ev=effEventDate(e);
  if(!ev.date)return null;
  const base=new Date(ev.date+"T00:00:00");
  base.setDate(base.getDate()-t.days);
  return ymd(base);
}
function taskState(e,d,t){
  if(d.tasks[t.code])return {k:"done",l:"Submitted",c:"--s-paid"};
  const due=taskDue(e,t);
  if(!due)return {k:"open",l:"No show date set",c:"--ink2"};
  const left=-daysAgo(due);
  if(left<0)return {k:"over",l:"Overdue "+(-left)+" day",c:"--risk"};
  if(left<=14)return {k:"soon",l:"more "+left+" day",c:"--warn"};
  return {k:"open",l:"more "+left+" day",c:"--ink2"};
}
function dealTasks(d){ return EV.tasks.filter(t=>t.by==="exhibitor") }
function pExh(){
  const list=DEALS.filter(mine), T=EV.tasks;
  const own=T.filter(t=>t.by==="exhibitor");
  let done=0, total=0, over=0, req=0;
  list.forEach(d=>own.forEach(t=>{
    total++; if(d.tasks[t.code])done++;
    else{ const st=taskState(EV,d,t); if(st.k==="over")over++; if(t.req)req++; }
  }));
  const ev=effEventDate(EV);
  let h=(ev.rolled?'<div class="note" style="border-left-color:var(--warn);margin-bottom:14px">'+
    '<p><b>the show date in the data is '+esc(ev.orig)+' which has already passed</b> '+
    'so every due date counts back from the next edition '+esc(ev.date)+' for now '+
    'the show date can be changed on the target & run rate page</p></div>':'')+
   '<div class="bar2"><div class="seg">'+
   '<button data-xv="team" aria-pressed="'+(exhView==="team")+'">Our team’s view</button>'+
   '<button data-xv="portal" aria-pressed="'+(exhView==="portal")+'">What exhibitors see</button>'+
   '</div><span style="font-size:13px;color:var(--ink2)">'+
   'Tasks the exhibitor does themselves '+own.length+' such as due dates counted back from the show date</span></div>';

  h+='<div class="cards">'+
   card(list.length,"Exhibitor","--line2")+
   card(done+"/"+total,"Fully collected",Math.round(done/Math.max(1,total)*100)+"%","--s-paid")+
   card(over,"Overdue","--risk")+
   card(req,"Required and not submitted","--warn")+'</div>';

  if(exhView==="portal"){ h+=portalPreview(list[0]); $("#page").innerHTML=h; bindExh(); return }

  h+='<div class="bar2" style="margin:18px 0 12px"><div class="seg">'+
   [["all","All"],["incomplete","Incomplete"],["required","Required task"],["overdue","Overdue"]]
   .map(x=>'<button data-xf="'+x[0]+'" aria-pressed="'+(exhFilter===x[0])+'">'+x[1]+'</button>').join("")+
   '</div></div>';

  const rows=list.map(d=>{
    const items=own.map(t=>({t,st:taskState(EV,d,t)}));
    return {d,items,
      done:items.filter(x=>x.st.k==="done").length,
