      over:items.filter(x=>x.st.k==="over").length,
      reqLeft:items.filter(x=>x.st.k!=="done"&&x.t.req).length};
  }).filter(r=>exhFilter==="all"||(exhFilter==="incomplete"&&r.done<own.length)||
      (exhFilter==="required"&&r.reqLeft)||(exhFilter==="overdue"&&r.over))
    .sort((a,b)=>b.over-a.over||b.reqLeft-a.reqLeft);

  h+='<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Exhibitor</th><th>Booths</th><th>Sales</th>'+
   '<th style="width:190px">Progress</th><th>Outstanding</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   (rows.length?rows.map(r=>
    '<tr data-x="'+r.d.id+'" style="cursor:pointer"><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+
      logoTile(r.d.co,28)+'<b>'+esc(r.d.co)+'</b></span></td>'+
    '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(r.d.booths.slice(0,3).join(", "))+'</td>'+
    '<td>'+esc(r.d.sales||"—")+'</td>'+
    '<td><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px"><div class="prog">'+
      '<i style="width:'+(r.done/own.length*100)+'%;background:'+
      (r.over?cv("--risk"):cv("--ok"))+'"></i></div>'+
      '<span style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink2);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums">'+
      r.done+'/'+own.length+'</span></div></td>'+
    '<td>'+(r.over?'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv("--risk")+'">Overdue '+r.over+'</span> ':'')+
      (r.reqLeft?'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv("--warn")+'">Required '+r.reqLeft+'</span>':'')+
      (!r.over&&!r.reqLeft?'<span style="color:var(--ok)">Complete</span>':'')+'</td></tr>').join("")
    :'<tr><td colspan="5" style="color:var(--ink2)">Nothing matches this filter</td></tr>')+
   '</tbody></table></div>';

  $("#page").innerHTML=h; bindExh();
}
/* หน้าที่ผู้ออกบูธเห็นเมื่อล็อกอินเข้าพอร์ทัล */
/* คู่มือผู้ออกบูธ ปลดล็อกเมื่อจ่ายมัดจำแล้วเท่านั้น
   สถานะ deposit หรือ paid ถือว่าจ่ายมัดจำแล้ว ส่วน booked คือจองเฉย ๆ */
function depositPaid(d){
  return ["deposit","paid"].includes(d.st) || ["billed","paid"].includes(d.stage);
}
function manualBlock(d){
  const ok=depositPaid(d), m=EV.manual||{};
  return '<div style="margin-top:16px;padding-top:14px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)">'+
   '<b style="font-size:14px">Exhibitor documents</b>'+
   '<div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;margin-top:9px;flex-wrap:wrap">'+
   '<span style="flex:1;min-width:200px;font-size:13.5px">'+esc(m.name||"Exhibitor Manual")+
     '<br><small style="color:var(--ink2)">'+
     (ok?'Ready to download':'Unlocks after the deposit is paid')+'</small></span>'+
   (ok?'<button class="act go" data-dlman="1" style="padding:8px 16px">Download manual</button>'
      :'<button class="act" disabled style="padding:8px 16px;opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed">'+
       'Not downloadable yet</button>')+
   '</div>'+
   (ok?'':'<p class="simnote" style="margin:10px 0 0">'+
     'This booth is <b>'+(ST[d.st]||{l:d.st}).l+'</b> Deposit not paid '+
     'so the manual is not released for download yet — the button opens itself once the deposit is recorded</p>')+
   '</div>';
}
function portalPreview(d){
  if(!d)return '<div class="note"><p>No exhibitors yet</p></div>';
  const own=EV.tasks.filter(t=>t.by==="exhibitor");
  const items=own.map(t=>({t,st:taskState(EV,d,t)}));
  const done=items.filter(x=>x.st.k==="done").length;
  return '<div class="note" style="margin:16px 0 14px"><p>This is the job of '+esc(d.co)+
   ' is what they see in the exhibitor portal — only their own tasks, not other people’s plans, prices or budget</p></div>'+
   '<div class="w"><h3>'+esc(d.co)+' · '+esc(EV.short)+'</h3><div class="body">'+
   '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap">'+
     '<span style="font-size:26px;font-weight:700">'+done+'/'+own.length+'</span>'+
     '<span class="sub" style="margin:0">Tasks done</span>'+
     '<div class="prog" style="flex:1;min-width:160px"><i style="width:'+
       (done/own.length*100)+'%;background:'+cv("--ok")+'"></i></div></div>'+
   items.sort((a,b)=>(a.st.k==="done")-(b.st.k==="done")).map(x=>
    '<div style="display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;padding:12px 0;'+
     'border-top:1px solid var(--line)">'+
     '<span style="width:19px;height:19px;border-radius:5px;flex:0 0 auto;margin-top:2px;'+
       'border:2px solid '+(x.st.k==="done"?cv("--ok"):cv("--line2"))+';background:'+
       (x.st.k==="done"?cv("--ok"):"transparent")+';color:#fff;font-size:12px;text-align:center;'+
       'line-height:16px">'+(x.st.k==="done"?"✓":"")+'</span>'+
     '<span style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b style="font-size:14.5px">'+esc(x.t.label)+'</b>'+
       '<br><small style="color:var(--ink2)">Due '+taskDue(EV,x.t)+'</small></span>'+
     '<span style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:flex-end">'+
       (x.t.req?'<span class="pill" style="background:var(--ink3)">Required</span>':'')+
       '<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv(x.st.c)+'">'+x.st.l+'</span></span>'+
     '</div>').join("")+
   manualBlock(d)+
   '</div></div>';
}
function bindExh(){
  const P=$("#page");
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-dlman]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const m=EV.manual||{};
    const body="This document is a sample — the real system sends the PDF the team uploaded\n\n"+
      "Event: "+EV.name+"\nFile: "+(m.name||"Exhibitor Manual");
    saveCSV((m.name||"exhibitor-manual").replace(/\.pdf$/,"")+".txt",[[body]],e.currentTarget);
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-xv]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{exhView=b.dataset.xv;pExh()}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-xf]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{exhFilter=b.dataset.xf;pExh()}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-x]").forEach(tr=>tr.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    exhOpen=+tr.dataset.x; drawExhPanel();}));
}
function drawExhPanel(){
  let sd=document.getElementById("exhpanel");
  if(!sd){sd=document.createElement("aside");sd.id="exhpanel";sd.className="side2";document.body.appendChild(sd)}
  const d=DEALS.find(x=>x.id===exhOpen); if(!d){sd.remove();return}
  const own=EV.tasks.filter(t=>t.by==="exhibitor");
  const wr=can("exhibitor")==="write";
  sd.innerHTML='<button class="x" aria-label="Off">×</button>'+
   '<h3>'+esc(d.co)+'</h3><p class="s">'+esc(d.booths.join(", "))+' · Sales '+esc(d.sales||"—")+'</p>'+
   own.map(t=>{const st=taskState(EV,d,t);
    return '<div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;padding:9px 0;'+
     'border-top:1px solid var(--line)">'+
     (wr?'<button class="tog" data-tk="'+t.code+'" style="padding:0;margin-top:1px">':'<span>')+
     '<span style="display:inline-block;width:17px;height:17px;border-radius:5px;'+
       'border:2px solid '+(st.k==="done"?cv("--ok"):cv("--line2"))+';background:'+
       (st.k==="done"?cv("--ok"):"transparent")+';color:#fff;font-size:11px;line-height:14px;'+
       'text-align:center">'+(st.k==="done"?"✓":"")+'</span>'+(wr?'</button>':'</span>')+
     '<span style="flex:1;min-width:0;font-size:13.5px"><b>'+esc(t.label)+'</b>'+
       (t.req?' <span class="pill" style="background:var(--ink3);font-size:11px">Required</span>':'')+
       '<br><small style="color:'+cv(st.c)+'">'+st.l+' · due '+taskDue(EV,t)+'</small></span></div>'}).join("")+
   (wr?'<p class="simnote" style="margin-top:12px">Tick the box to mark it as received '+
     'in reality exhibitors tick these off in the portal — our team only chases the overdue ones</p>':'');
  sd.querySelector(".x").addEventListener("click",()=>{exhOpen=null;sd.remove()});
  sd.querySelectorAll("[data-tk]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    d.tasks[b.dataset.tk]=!d.tasks[b.dataset.tk]; pExh(); drawExhPanel();}));
}


/* ---------- แชร์งาน ---------- */
const HOME='penguinx.co';
/* ขอบเขตที่แชร์ได้ พร้อมคำอธิบายว่าเห็นอะไรและเห็นเงินหรือไม่
   money:true คือเห็นตัวเลขเงินและคอมมิชชัน ต้องเตือนตอนเชิญคนนอก */
const SCOPES=[
 ["","Whole event, at the level granted",
  "Every module they are allowed to see — floor plan, deals, budget, stage, exhibitors and timeline",true],
 ["floorplan","Floor plan only",
  "Booth layout, zones and booth status. No client names, no prices, no budget",false],
 ["deal","Deals & clients only",
  "Client list, deal stages and next steps. Deal values only, not the event budget",true],
 ["stage","Stage only",
  "Stage schedule, sessions and speakers. Nothing about booths, clients or money",false],
 ["budget","Budget only",
  "The P&L and every budget line, planned against actual",true],
 ["exhibitor","Exhibitor care only",
  "The exhibitor checklist and what each one still owes us. No prices, no budget",false],
 ["movein","Build work only",
  "Build-up and teardown schedule for contractors. Floor plan without any commercial detail",false]];
const PERMS=[["view","View only","Can read everything in the scope below, cannot change anything"],
 ["edit","Can edit","Can change data inside the scope below, cannot invite other people"]];
const scopeInfo=(k)=>(SCOPES.find(x=>x[0]===k)||SCOPES[0]);
const SHARES={};
function shareList(){ return SHARES[EV.id]=SHARES[EV.id]||[] }
/* ───────── ส่งออกข้อมูล ─────────
   ทุกตารางในระบบดาวน์โหลดเป็น CSV ได้ เห็นเฉพาะชุดที่บทบาทนั้นมีสิทธิ์ดู
   และมีไฟล์สำรองทั้งงานเป็น JSON ไว้ย้ายระบบหรือกู้คืน */
function dsList(){
  const E=EV, T=(x)=>x||[];
  return [
   {k:"booths", m:"floorplan", t:"Booths & floor plan", n:T(E.booths).length,
    d:"Booth code, zone, grid position, status, client and list price",
    rows:()=>[["Booth code","Zone","Zone name","x","y","Width","Height","Status","Client","Sales",
      "Package","Product","Contact","Phone","Email","List price"]].concat(
      T(E.booths).map(b=>[b.code,b.zone,(E.zoneNames||{})[b.zone]||"",b.x,b.y,b.w,b.h,
        (ST[b.st]||{}).l||b.st,b.co||"",b.sales||"",b.pkg||"",b.product||"",
        b.contact||"",b.phone||"",b.email||"",b.list||0]))},
   {k:"deals", m:"deal", t:"Deals & clients", n:T(E.deals).length,
    d:"Every deal with its stage, value, owner, next step and booths held",
    rows:()=>[["Client","booth","Value","Stage","Booth status","Sales","Next step","Meeting date",
      "Last contact","Contacts logged"]].concat(
      T(E.deals).map(d=>{const l=(d.acts||[]).slice(-1)[0];
        return [d.co,(d.booths||[]).join(" "),d.list||0,stName(d.stage),d.st||"",
          d.sales||"",d.next||"",d.nextDate||"",l?l.date:"",(d.acts||[]).length]}))},
   {k:"budget", m:"budget", t:"Budget P&L", n:T(E.budget).length,
    d:"Every line of the chart of accounts, with budget and actual",
    rows:()=>[["Main category","Sub-category","Item","Qty","Unit","Qty 2","Unit 2",
      "Unit price","Budgeted","Actual","Variance","Status","Supplier"]].concat(
      T(E.budget).map(l=>{const fc=lineTotal(l), ac=l.ac||0;
        return [l.cat||"",l.sub||"",l.name||"",l.qty||"",l.unit||"",l.qty2||"",l.unit2||"",
          l.price||"",fc,ac,ac-fc,l.st||"",l.sup||""]}))},
   {k:"sessions", m:"stage", t:"Stage schedule", n:T(E.sessions).length,
    d:"Every slot on every stage, split by day, time, moderator and confirmation status",
    rows:()=>[["Stage","day","Date","Time","min","Topic","Type","Confirmed",
      "Lock time","Moderator","Speakers"]].concat(
      T(E.sessions).map(s=>[s.stage,s.day,s.date||"",s.time||"",s.min||"",s.title||"",
        s.kind||"",s.cf?"Confirmed":"Not confirmed",s.lock?"Locked":"",s.mod||"",
        (s.people||[]).length]))},
   {k:"speakers", m:"stage", t:"Speakers",
    n:T(E.sessions).reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.people||[]).length,0),
    d:"Every speaker, with the stage and slot they are on, their position and acceptance status",
    rows:()=>[["Stage","day","Time","Topic","Display name","Full name","Position","Status",
      "Contact channel","Coordinator","Slides"]].concat(
      T(E.sessions).flatMap(s=>(s.people||[]).map(p=>[s.stage,s.day,s.time||"",s.title||"",
        p.n||"",p.real||"",p.pos||"",p.st||"",p.contact||"",p.coord||"",p.slides||""])))},
   {k:"timeline", m:"timeline", t:"Project timeline", n:T(E.timeline).length,
    d:"All three phases — pre-event, on-site and post-event — with plan against actual",
    rows:()=>{
      const out=[["Slot","Category","Task","Team","Status","Plan start","Plan finish","Actual start",
        "Actual finish","Variance","Note"].concat((E.tlCols||[]).map(c=>c.l))];
      T(E.timeline).forEach(r=>{
        const ph={pre:"Pre-event",on:"On-site",post:"Post-event"}[r.ph||"pre"];
        const x=(E.tlCols||[]).map(c=>(r.x||{})[c.k]||"");
        if(r.ph==="on"){
          const dd=tlDays()[r.d]||{};
          out.push([ph,r.grp,r.name,r.by||"",(TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan).l,
            (dd.l||"")+" "+r.t1,(dd.l||"")+" "+r.t2,r.at1||"",r.at2||"",
            slipMin(r)==null?"":slipMin(r)+" min",r.note||""].concat(x));
        }else{
          out.push([ph,r.grp,r.name,r.by||"",(TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan).l,
            tlDate(pA(r)),tlDate(pB(r)),hasAct(r)?tlDate(aA(r)):"",
            hasAct(r)?tlDate(aB(r)):"",slip(r)==null?"":slip(r)+" weeks",
            r.note||""].concat(x));
        }});
      return out}},
   {k:"tasks", m:"exhibitor", t:"What exhibitors have to submit", n:T(E.tasks).length,
    d:"Every checklist item, with each exhibitor’s submission status",
    rows:()=>[["Client","booth"].concat(T(E.tasks).map(t=>t.label))].concat(
      T(E.deals).map(d=>[d.co,(d.booths||[]).join(" ")].concat(
        T(E.tasks).map(t=>(d.tasks||{})[t.code]?"Submitted":"Not submitted"))))},
   {k:"packages", m:"price", t:"Packages & pricing",
    n:Object.keys(E.packages||{}).length+T(E.addons).length,
    d:"List price of every package, what is included, and the add-ons",
    rows:()=>[["Type","Name","Size","Build type","Price","Badges","Terms"]].concat(
      Object.entries(E.packages||{}).map(([k,v])=>["Package",k,v.size||"",v.build||"",
        v.price||0,v.badge||"",(v.b||[]).map(x=>x[1]).join(" / ")]))
      .concat(T(E.addons).map(a=>["Add-ons",a[0],"","",a[1],"",""]))},
   {k:"reps", m:"deal", t:"Sales team", n:(D.reps||[]).length,
    d:"Names, type and commission rate of the sales team",
    rows:()=>[["Name","Short name","Type","Rate %","Active","Deals","Total value"]].concat(
      (D.reps||[]).map(r=>{const g=T(E.deals).filter(d=>d.sales===r.name);
        return [r.name,r.th||"",r.kind||"",r.rate||0,r.active?"Active":"Off",
          g.length,g.reduce((a,d)=>a+(d.list||0),0)]}))},
  ].filter(x=>!x.m||can(x.m)!=="none");
}
/* หน้าเปิดปิดโมดูลของงานนี้ */
function pModules(){
  const wr=can("user")==="write";
  const m=EV.modules||(EV.modules={});
  const count={
    sales:(EV.deals||[]).length, plan:(EV.booths||[]).length,
    money:(EV.budget||[]).length, stage:(EV.sessions||[]).length,
    exh:(EV.tasks||[]).length, tl:(EV.timeline||[]).length,
  };
  const WHAT={
    sales:"Deals, board, working grid, opportunities and rep performance",
    plan:"Booth layout, zones, packages and pricing",
    money:"P&L, revenue target, run rate and expense budget",
    stage:"Stage schedule, sessions and speakers",
    exh:"Exhibitor checklist, portal preview and checklist setup",
    tl:"Project timeline for all three phases",
  };
  let h='<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:16px"><p>'+
   '<b>Turn off what this event does not use</b> and the menu disappears from the sidebar '+
   'for this event only. Other events are not affected.</p>'+
   '<p>Turning a module off only hides it. Nothing is deleted — the row counts below stay '+
   'exactly as they are and come back the moment you switch it on again.</p></div>';
  h+='<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Module</th><th>What it covers</th>'+
   '<th class="num">Rows kept</th><th class="num">On</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   MODULES.map(([id,name])=>{
     const on=m[id]!==false;
     return '<tr><td><b>'+esc(name)+'</b></td>'+
      '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(WHAT[id])+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+f(count[id]||0)+'</td>'+
      '<td class="num">'+(wr
        ?'<button class="tog" data-mod="'+id+'">'+(on
          ?'<span style="color:var(--ok);font-weight:700">On</span>'
          :'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">Off</span>')+'</button>'
        :(on?"On":"Off"))+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>';
  h+='<div class="note" style="margin-top:16px"><p><b>Overview and Settings cannot be turned off</b> — '+
   'without Settings there would be no way to switch anything back on</p>'+
   '<p><b>Hiding a menu is not a permission</b> — what each role may see is still enforced on the '+
   'server on every request, exactly as before. This only tidies the sidebar.</p></div>';
  /* ที่เดียวที่ล้างของที่บันทึกไว้ในเบราว์เซอร์ได้ วางไว้ท้ายสุด ไม่ให้กดโดนง่าย */
  h+='<h2 class="sec">Saved work</h2>'+
   '<div class="note"><p><b>Every change is saved in this browser as you make it</b> — '+
   'no save button. It survives a refresh and a new version of this page.</p>'+
   '<p>It is per browser, so a teammate on another machine sees the figures this page shipped with, '+
   'not yours. Shared editing needs the server.</p>'+
   (saveOn?'<p id="svmeta" style="color:var(--ink3)">'+
     (saveAt?"Last saved "+esc(saveWhen()):"Nothing changed yet in this browser")+'</p>'
    :'<p style="color:var(--risk)"><b>Saving is off</b> — browser storage is full</p>')+
   '</div>'+
   '<div class="addbar"><button id="svreset" class="rowdel" '+
     'title="Throw away every change made in this browser">Reset to the data this page shipped with</button></div>';
  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const rb=$("#svreset");
  if(rb)rb.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(rb.dataset.armed){ saveClear(); return }
    rb.dataset.armed="1"; rb.textContent="Throw away every change? Click again to confirm";
    setTimeout(()=>{ if(rb.dataset.armed){ delete rb.dataset.armed;
      rb.textContent="Reset to the data this page shipped with" } },5000);
  });
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-mod]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const id=b.dataset.mod;
    m[id]=m[id]===false;            // ปิดอยู่ให้เปิด เปิดอยู่ให้ปิด
    if(page===id&&m[id]===false)page="dash";
    draw();
  }));
}
function pExport(){
  const L=dsList();
  const hidden=[{m:"budget",t:"Budget"},{m:"deal",t:"Deals & clients"},
    {m:"stage",t:"Stage schedule"},{m:"price",t:"Packages & pricing"}]
    .filter(x=>can(x.m)==="none");
  let h='<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:16px"><p>'+
   '<b>Every table in the system can be downloaded as CSV</b> Opens directly in Excel and Google Sheets '+
   'a BOM is included, so Thai text is not garbled</p>'+
   '<p>Only the data sets the role <b>'+esc(ROLE[role].n)+'</b> has view access'+
   (hidden.length?' currently hidden '+hidden.length+' set ('+
     hidden.map(x=>esc(x.t)).join(", ")+')':'')+'</p></div>';
  h+='<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Data set</th><th class="num">Rows</th>'+
   '<th>What is in the file</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   L.map(x=>'<tr><td><b>'+esc(x.t)+'</b></td><td class="num">'+f(x.n)+'</td>'+
     '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(x.d)+'</td>'+
     '<td><button class="act" data-ex="'+x.k+'" style="padding:7px 13px;font-size:13px">'+
     'Download CSV</button></td></tr>').join("")+'</tbody></table></div>';
  h+='<h2 class="sec">Download everything at once</h2>'+
   '<div class="note"><p>The browser confirms one file at a time — to take everything at once, '+
   'pick one of the options below to get a single file</p></div>'+
   '<div class="addbar"><button id="exAll">Combine every table into one CSV</button>'+
   '<button id="exJson">Back up the whole event as JSON</button></div>'+
   '<div class="note"><p><b>Single CSV</b> Every table one after another, separated by a header line '+
   'Good for reading or forwarding, but you have to split the tables yourself to use them</p>'+
   '<p><b>JSON</b> Keeps every field intact, including booth coordinates and contact history '+
   'and values CSV cannot hold — use it to migrate or restore</p></div>';
  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const P=$("#page");
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-ex]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const x=L.find(y=>y.k===b.dataset.ex);
    saveCSV(EV.id+"-"+x.k+".csv",x.rows(),e.currentTarget)}));
  P.querySelector("#exAll").addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const out=[];
    L.forEach((x,i)=>{ if(i)out.push([""]);
      out.push(["### "+x.t+" ("+x.n+" rows)"]);
      x.rows().forEach(r=>out.push(r)) });
    saveCSV(EV.id+"-all.csv",out,e.currentTarget)});
  P.querySelector("#exJson").addEventListener("click",async e=>{
    const btn=e.currentTarget, label=btn.textContent;
    const keep=Object.assign({},EV);
    /* ไม่เอาโลโก้กับไฟล์ผังไปด้วย เพราะเป็น data URI ก้อนใหญ่ */
    delete keep.logo; delete keep.planFile;
    const data=JSON.stringify({งาน:keep,ทีมขาย:D.reps||[],
      โครงงบมาตรฐาน:D.template||[],โครงไทม์ไลน์มาตรฐาน:D.timelineTemplate||[]},null,1);
    try{
      const d=await downloader();
      btn.textContent="Saving…";
      if(d)await d.save({filename:EV.id+"-backup.json",data});
      else{ const u=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([data],{type:"application/json"}));
        const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=u; a.download=EV.id+"-backup.json";
        document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
        setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(u),1000) }
      btn.textContent="Saved";
    }catch(err){ btn.textContent = err&&err.code==="declined"?"Cancelled":"Could not save" }
    setTimeout(()=>{btn.textContent=label},2500);
  });
}
function pShare(){
  const wr=can("share")==="write";
  const rows=shareList();
  const ext=rows.filter(r=>r.external).length;
  let h='<div class="cards">'+
   card(rows.length,"People with access to this event","--line2")+
   card(rows.filter(r=>!r.accepted).length,"Not accepted yet","--warn")+
   card(ext,"Outside the company",ext?"Check permissions carefully":"","--risk")+
   card(rows.filter(r=>r.permission==="edit").length,"Can edit","--s-deposit")+'</div>';
  if(wr)h+='<h2 class="sec">Invite someone to this event</h2>'+
   '<div class="w" style="padding:18px"><div class="bar2" style="margin:0">'+
   '<input id="sem" type="email" placeholder="Email" style="flex:1;min-width:200px;padding:9px 12px;'+
     'border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink)">'+
   '<select id="sp"><option value="view">View only</option><option value="edit">Can edit</option></select>'+
   '<select id="ss">'+SCOPES.map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'">'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
   '<button class="act go" id="sadd" style="padding:9px 18px">Send invitation</button></div>'+
   '<div id="scopehelp" class="scopehelp"></div>'+
   '<p class="simnote" style="margin:10px 0 0">Outside the domain '+HOME+
   ' will be flagged as external and always default to view-only</p></div>';
  h+='<h2 class="sec">What each level of access can see'+
   '<button class="ihelp" id="scopetog" title="Show the full comparison">i</button></h2>'+
   '<div class="tw" id="scopetab" hidden><table><thead><tr><th>Scope</th><th>What they see</th>'+
   '<th class="num">Money figures</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+
   SCOPES.map(x=>'<tr><td><b>'+esc(x[1])+'</b></td>'+
     '<td style="color:var(--ink2);font-size:12.5px">'+esc(x[2])+'</td>'+
     '<td class="num">'+(x[3]
       ?'<span style="color:var(--risk);font-weight:700">Visible</span>'
       :'<span style="color:var(--ok);font-weight:600">Hidden</span>')+'</td></tr>').join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>';
  h+='<h2 class="sec">People with access</h2>';
  h+=rows.length?'<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Email</th><th>Permission</th>'+
    '<th>What they see</th><th>Status</th><th>Invited on</th>'+(wr?'<th></th>':'')+
    '</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.map((r,i)=>
     '<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+avatar(r.email,28)+
       '<span><b>'+esc(r.email)+'</b>'+(r.external?
         '<br><small style="color:var(--risk)">Outside the company</small>':'')+'</span></span></td>'+
     '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-si2="'+i+'" data-sf="permission">'+
       '<option value="view"'+(r.permission==="view"?" selected":"")+'>View only</option>'+
       '<option value="edit"'+(r.permission==="edit"?" selected":"")+'>Can edit</option></select>'
       :(r.permission==="edit"?"Can edit":"View only"))+'</td>'+
     '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-si2="'+i+'" data-sf="scope">'+
       SCOPES.map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+(r.scope===x[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join("")+
       '</select>':esc((SCOPES.find(x=>x[0]===r.scope)||["","Whole event"])[1]))+'</td>'+
     '<td>'+(r.accepted?'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv("--s-paid")+'">Active</span>'
       :'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv("--warn")+'">Pending</span>')+'</td>'+
     '<td style="color:var(--ink2)">'+esc(r.at)+'</td>'+
     (wr?'<td><button class="rowdel" data-srm="'+i+'">Revoke</button></td>':'')+'</tr>').join("")+
    '</tbody></table></div>'
   :'<div class="note"><p>This event has not been shared with anyone — only team members with an account can get in</p></div>';
  if(wr)h+='<h2 class="sec">Create a new event from this one</h2><div class="note">'+
   '<p>Cloning lives in one place with creating from scratch — press <b>All events</b> at the top '+
   'of the sidebar, then <b>Create new event</b>, and choose <b>Clone an existing event</b> at the top of the form</p></div>';
  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const tog=$("#scopetog");
  if(tog)tog.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const t=$("#scopetab"); t.hidden=!t.hidden; tog.classList.toggle("on",!t.hidden)});
  /* คำอธิบายใต้ช่องเลือก เปลี่ยนทันทีที่เลือก จะได้ไม่ต้องเลื่อนไปอ่านที่อื่น */
  const help=$("#scopehelp");
  function drawHelp(){
    if(!help)return;
    const p=PERMS.find(x=>x[0]===$("#sp").value)||PERMS[0];
    const sc=scopeInfo($("#ss").value);
    help.innerHTML='<div class="sh1"><b>'+esc(p[1])+'</b> · '+esc(p[2])+'</div>'+
      '<div class="sh1"><b>'+esc(sc[1])+'</b> · '+esc(sc[2])+'</div>'+
      (sc[3]?'<div class="shwarn">This scope shows revenue, deal values and commission. '+
        'Think twice before giving it to anyone outside the company — pick '+
        '<b>Floor plan only</b> if a contractor or client just needs to see the layout.</div>'
       :'<div class="shok">No money figures are visible with this scope</div>');
  }
  if(help){ drawHelp(); ["#sp","#ss"].forEach(k=>{const el=$(k); if(el)el.addEventListener("change",drawHelp)}) }
  const add=$("#sadd");
  if(add)add.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const em=$("#sem").value.trim().toLowerCase();
    if(!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(em)){$("#sem").focus();return}
    if(rows.some(r=>r.email===em)){alert("This email has already been invited");return}
    const external=!em.endsWith("@"+HOME);
    rows.push({email:em, permission:external?"view":$("#sp").value,
      scope:$("#ss").value, external, accepted:false, at:ymd(new Date())});
    pShare();
  });
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-sf]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const r=rows[+el.dataset.si2];
    r[el.dataset.sf==="permission"?"permission":"scope"]=el.value; pShare();
  }));
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-srm]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){rows.splice(+b.dataset.srm,1);pShare();return}
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Confirm revoke?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="Revoke";b.style.color=""}},4000);
  }));
  const cl=$("#clone");
  if(cl)cl.addEventListener("click",()=>alert(
    "in production a new event is created from event.cloned_from, copying the plan, booth types, budget categories and checklist\n"+
    "this demo does not include the create-event flow"));
}

/* ---------- ผู้ใช้ ---------- */
function pUsers(){
  const M=[["floorplan","Floor plan"],["price","Packages & pricing"],["deal","Deals & clients"],["target","Target & run rate"],["budget","Feasibility"],
   ["stage","Stage"],["exhibitor","Exhibitor care"],["timeline","Project timeline"],
   ["share","Share event"],["user","Users & permissions"]];
  const RS=["sales","operations","exec","admin"];
  const LV={write:["Can edit","--ok"],approve:["Can approve","--ok"],
    read:["Can view","--ink2"],none:["—","--ink3"]};
  const wr=can("user")==="write";
  let hh='<h2 class="sec">Sales team</h2>'+
   (wr?'<div class="bar2">'+
     '<input id="rname" placeholder="Sales rep" style="flex:0 0 190px;padding:9px 12px;'+
       'border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink)">'+
     '<select id="rkind">'+Object.keys(KINDN).map(k=>'<option value="'+k+'">'+KINDN[k]+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'+
     '<input id="rrate" placeholder="Commission %" inputmode="numeric" value="10" style="width:110px;padding:9px 12px;'+
       'border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:8px;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink)">'+
     '<button class="act go" id="raddb" style="padding:9px 16px">Add rep</button></div>':'')+
   '<div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th class="num">Commission</th>'+
   '<th class="num">Deals held</th><th>Status</th>'+(wr?'<th></th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+
   REPS.map((r,i)=>'<tr><td><span style="display:flex;gap:9px;align-items:center">'+avatar(r.name,28)+
     '<b>'+esc(r.name)+'</b></span></td>'+
     '<td>'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-ri="'+i+'" data-rf="kind">'+
       Object.keys(KINDN).map(k=>'<option value="'+k+'"'+(r.kind===k?" selected":"")+'>'+KINDN[k]+'</option>').join("")+
       '</select>':esc(KINDN[r.kind]))+'</td>'+
     '<td class="num">'+(wr?'<span class="inl" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-ri="'+i+'" data-rf="rate">'+
       r.rate+'</span>':r.rate)+'%</td>'+
     '<td class="num">'+DEALS.filter(d=>d.sales===r.name).length+'</td>'+
     '<td>'+(r.active?'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv("--s-paid")+'">Active</span>'
       :'<span class="pill" style="background:'+cv("--ink3")+'">Disable</span>')+'</td>'+
     (wr?'<td><button class="rowdel" data-rtog="'+i+'" style="font-size:12.5px">'+
       (r.active?"Disable":"Enable")+'</button></td>':'')+'</tr>').join("")+
   '</tbody></table></div>'+
   '<div class="note" style="margin-top:12px"><p>Disable rather than delete, so old deals still show who closed them</p></div>';

  $("#page").innerHTML=hh+'<h2 class="sec">Permission matrix</h2><div class="tw"><table><thead><tr><th>Module</th>'+
   RS.map(r=>'<th>'+ROLE[r].n+'</th>').join("")+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+
   M.map(m=>'<tr><td><b>'+m[1]+'</b></td>'+RS.map(r=>{const l=LV[ROLE[r].p[m[0]]]||LV.none;
     return '<td style="color:'+cv(l[1])+';font-weight:'+(l[0]==="Can edit"?600:400)+'">'+l[0]+
      (m[0]==="deal"&&ROLE[r].scope==="own"?'<br><small style="color:var(--ink3)">Own only</small>':"")+'</td>'}).join("")+
    '</tr>').join("")+'</tbody></table></div>';
  const ab=$("#raddb");
  if(ab)ab.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const n=$("#rname").value.trim(); if(!n){$("#rname").focus();return}
    if(REPS.some(r=>r.name===n)){alert("That name is already used");return}
    REPS.push({name:n,kind:$("#rkind").value,rate:+$("#rrate").value||0,active:true});
    pUsers();
  });
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-rf]").forEach(el=>{
    const w=()=>{const r=REPS[+el.dataset.ri];
      if(el.dataset.rf==="rate")r.rate=+(el.textContent.replace(/[^0-9]/g,""))||0;
      else r.kind=el.value; pUsers();};
    if(el.tagName==="SELECT")el.addEventListener("change",w);
    else el.addEventListener("blur",w);
  });
  $("#page").querySelectorAll("[data-rtog]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const r=REPS[+b.dataset.rtog]; r.active=!r.active; pUsers();}));
}

/* ───────── ไทม์ไลน์โครงการ ───────── */
/* ───────── คัดลอกวาง แบบเดียวกับ Google Sheets ─────────
   เลือกแถวด้วยช่องติ๊กหน้าชื่องาน แล้ว Ctrl+C ได้ TSV ที่วางลงชีตได้เลย
   Ctrl+V รับ TSV จากชีตกลับเข้ามาเป็นแถวใหม่ ไม่ต้องพิมพ์ทีละช่อง */
let tlSel=new Set(), tlAnchor=null, tlCtx=null, tlView=null;
const _mac=/Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform||navigator.userAgent);
const KZC=_mac?"\u2318C":"Ctrl+C", KZV=_mac?"\u2318V":"Ctrl+V";
/* ย้ายไปอีกช่วงแล้วล้างการเลือกทิ้ง ไม่งั้นคัดลอกข้ามช่วงกันมั่ว */
function tlEnter(v){ if(tlView!==v){ tlView=v; tlSel.clear(); tlAnchor=null; tlPick=null } }

const stKey=l=>{ const k=Object.keys(TLST).find(x=>TLST[x].l===String(l||"").trim());
  return k&&k!=="plan"?k:"" };
function parseDate(v){
  const t=String(v||"").trim(); if(!t)return null;
  let m=t.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/);
  if(m)return fixBE(+m[1],+m[2],+m[3]);
  m=t.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{2,4})$/);
  if(m){ let y=+m[3]; if(y<100)y+=2000; return fixBE(y,+m[2],+m[1]) }
  return null;
}
function fixBE(y,mo,d){ if(y>2400)y-=543;            // ปีพุทธในชีตไทย
  const x=new Date(y,mo-1,d); return isNaN(x)?null:x }
function parseTime(v){
  const m=String(v||"").trim().match(/^(\d{1,2})[:.](\d{2})$/);
  if(!m)return null; const h=+m[1],mi=+m[2];
  return (h>=0&&h<=24&&mi>=0&&mi<60)?mh(h+mi/60):null;
}
function weekOf(v){ const d=parseDate(v); if(!d)return null;
  return Math.round((monday(d)-tlShowWeek())/604800000) }
const tsvLine=a=>a.map(x=>String(x==null?"":x).replace(/[\t\r\n]/g," ")).join("\t");
function tsvParse(txt){
  return String(txt).replace(/\r/g,"").split("\n").filter(l=>l.trim())
    .map(l=>l.split("\t"));
}

/* แถบเครื่องมือที่โผล่มาเมื่อเลือกแถว */
function selBar(n,wr){
  /* เหมือน Google Sheets เลือกแล้วใช้ปุ่มลัด หรือคลิกขวาเอาเมนู
     เหลือชิปเล็ก ๆ บอกว่าเลือกอยู่กี่แถว ไม่มีแถบปุ่มเต็มหน้าจอ */
  if(!n)return "";
  return '<div class="selchip"><b>'+n+(n===1?" row":" rows")+' selected</b>'+
    '<span class="selk">right-click for actions</span>'+
    '<button id="sclr" title="Esc">Clear</button></div>';
}
/* เมนูคลิกขวา แบบเดียวกับสเปรดชีต */
/* ---------- ฝ่ายที่รับผิดชอบ ----------
   เดิมเป็นข้อความใต้ชื่องาน พิมพ์เองทั้งหมด เลยสะกดไม่ตรงกันและอ่านยาก
   ย้ายมาเป็นคอลัมน์ของตัวเอง เลือกจากรายการ ติ๊กได้หลายฝ่ายพร้อมกัน */
const TEAMS=["Event","Sale","Marketing","Content & Ads","Graphic","Production","Admin","Finance","All"];
function teamsOf(r){ return String(r.by||"").split(",").map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean) }
function teamList(){
  const o=TEAMS.slice();
  tlRows().forEach(r=>teamsOf(r).forEach(t=>{ if(!o.includes(t))o.push(t) }));
  return o;
}
/* โชว์สองป้ายพอ เกินนั้นบอกเป็นจำนวน ไม่งั้นงานที่มีสี่ฝ่ายดันแถวสูงกว่าคนอื่นเท่าตัว */
function teamChips(r){
  const t=teamsOf(r);
  if(!t.length)return '<span class="tmnone">Nobody yet</span>';
  const show=t.slice(0,2).map(x=>'<span class="tmc">'+esc(x)+'</span>').join("");
  return show+(t.length>2?'<span class="tmmore">+'+(t.length-2)+'</span>':"");
}
/* กล่องติ๊กลอยอยู่บน body ไม่งั้นโดนกรอบเลื่อนแนวนอนของตารางตัดหัวตัดหาง */
function teamMenu(btn,r,redraw){
  document.querySelectorAll(".ctxm").forEach(e=>e.remove());
  const cur=teamsOf(r), list=teamList();
  const m=document.createElement("div");
  m.className="ctxm tmm";
  m.innerHTML='<div class="tmh">Who owns this task</div>'+
    list.map(t=>'<button data-tm="'+esc(t)+'" class="tmi'+(cur.includes(t)?" on":"")+'">'+
      '<i class="tmk">'+(cur.includes(t)?"✓":"")+'</i>'+esc(t)+'</button>').join("")+
    '<hr><button data-tmnew="1" class="tmi">+ Another team…</button>'+
    (cur.length?'<button data-tmclr="1" class="tmi dg">Clear</button>':'');
  document.body.appendChild(m);
  const b=btn.getBoundingClientRect(), w=m.offsetWidth, hgt=m.offsetHeight;
  m.style.left=Math.min(b.left,innerWidth-w-8)+"px";
  m.style.top=(b.bottom+6+hgt>innerHeight?Math.max(8,b.top-hgt-6):b.bottom+6)+"px";
  const close=()=>{ m.remove(); document.removeEventListener("mousedown",out) };
  const out=e=>{ if(!m.contains(e.target))close() };
  setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener("mousedown",out),0);
  const put=list2=>{ r.by=list2.join(", ") };
  m.querySelectorAll("[data-tm]").forEach(x=>x.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const t=x.dataset.tm, now=teamsOf(r);
    put(now.includes(t)?now.filter(y=>y!==t):now.concat(t));
    close(); redraw();
  }));
  const nb=m.querySelector("[data-tmnew]");
  if(nb)nb.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const t=(prompt("Name of the team")||"").trim();
    close(); if(!t)return;
    const now=teamsOf(r); if(!now.includes(t))put(now.concat(t));
    redraw();
  });
  const cb=m.querySelector("[data-tmclr]");
  if(cb)cb.addEventListener("click",()=>{ r.by=""; close(); redraw() });
}
function rowMenu(x,y,P,redraw,wr){
  document.querySelectorAll(".ctxm").forEach(e=>e.remove());
  const n=tlSel.size;
  const item=(id,label,key,cls)=>'<button data-cm="'+id+'"'+(cls?' class="'+cls+'"':'')+'>'+
    label+'<i>'+key+'</i></button>';
  const m=document.createElement("div");
  m.className="ctxm";
  m.innerHTML=item("copy","Copy",KZC)+
    (wr?item("paste","Paste",KZV):"")+
    (wr?item("dup","Duplicate",""):"")+
    (wr?item("del","Delete",'Del',"dg"):"")+
    '<hr>'+item("clear","Clear selection","Esc");
  document.body.appendChild(m);
  const w=m.offsetWidth, hgt=m.offsetHeight;
  m.style.left=Math.min(x,innerWidth-w-8)+"px";
  m.style.top=Math.min(y,innerHeight-hgt-8)+"px";
  const close=()=>{ m.remove(); document.removeEventListener("mousedown",close) };
  setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener("mousedown",close),0);
  m.querySelectorAll("[data-cm]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",async()=>{
    const k=b.dataset.cm; close();
    if(k==="copy")doCopy(true);
    else if(k==="paste"){
      try{ const t=await navigator.clipboard.readText(); if(t)applyPaste(t);
           else toastTL("Clipboard is empty — press Ctrl+V instead") }
      catch(e){ toastTL("The browser will not let a menu read the clipboard — press Ctrl+V instead") }
    }
    else if(k==="dup"){ const ALL=tlRows();
      const add=[...tlSel].map(r=>Object.assign({},r,{name:r.name+" (copy)"}));
      add.forEach(r=>ALL.push(r)); tlSel.clear(); add.forEach(r=>tlSel.add(r)); redraw() }
    else if(k==="del"){ if(!confirm("Delete "+n+" selected rows?"))return;
      const ALL=tlRows(); [...tlSel].forEach(r=>{const q=ALL.indexOf(r); if(q>=0)ALL.splice(q,1)});
      tlSel.clear(); redraw() }
    else { tlSel.clear(); redraw() }
  }));
}
/* ช่องเลขแถวแบบสเปรดชีต กดที่เลขเพื่อเลือกทั้งแถว
   Shift เลือกเป็นช่วง Cmd หรือ Ctrl เลือกทีละแถวแบบสลับ */
const selNo=(i)=>'<td class="gtno" data-rn="'+i+'" draggable="true" '+
  'title="Drag to reorder"><span>'+(i+1)+'</span></td>';
const grpNo=(g)=>'<td class="gtno gtnog" data-gsel="'+esc(g)+'" title="Select whole category"></td>';
/* ลากสลับลำดับแถว จับที่ช่องเลขแถว
   ลากทีเดียวได้ทั้งชุดที่เลือกไว้ ถ้าแถวที่ลากอยู่ในชุดนั้น
   ลากข้ามหมวดได้ ระบบย้ายหมวดให้ตามที่ไปวาง */
let dragSet=null;
function wireDrag(P,list,redraw){
  const ALL=tlRows();
  const clear=()=>P.querySelectorAll(".dropab,.dropbe")
    .forEach(e=>e.classList.remove("dropab","dropbe"));

  P.querySelectorAll("td.gtno[draggable]").forEach(td=>{
    td.addEventListener("dragstart",e=>{
      const r=list[+td.dataset.rn]; if(!r)return;
      dragSet = tlSel.has(r) && tlSel.size>1 ? list.filter(x=>tlSel.has(x)) : [r];
      e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed="move";
      e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain","row");   // Firefox ต้องมีข้อมูลถึงจะเริ่มลาก
      td.closest("tr").classList.add("dragging");
    });
    td.addEventListener("dragend",()=>{
      dragSet=null; clear();
      P.querySelectorAll(".dragging").forEach(e=>e.classList.remove("dragging"));
    });
  });

  P.querySelectorAll("tr.gtr[data-drow], tr.gtg[data-dgrp]").forEach(tr=>{
    tr.addEventListener("dragover",e=>{
      if(!dragSet)return;
      e.preventDefault(); e.dataTransfer.dropEffect="move";
      const b=tr.getBoundingClientRect();
      const below = e.clientY > b.top + b.height/2;
      clear(); tr.classList.add(below?"dropbe":"dropab");
    });
    tr.addEventListener("drop",e=>{
      if(!dragSet)return;
      e.preventDefault();
      const below = e.clientY > tr.getBoundingClientRect().top + tr.getBoundingClientRect().height/2;
      const moving=dragSet.slice(); dragSet=null; clear();

      let grp, anchor;
      if(tr.dataset.dgrp!=null){
        /* วางบนหัวหมวด แปลว่าเอาไปไว้ต้นหมวดนั้น */
        grp=tr.dataset.dgrp;
        anchor=ALL.find(x=>x.grp===grp&&!moving.includes(x)) ?? null;
      }else{
        const target=list[+tr.dataset.drow];
        if(!target||moving.includes(target))return;
        grp=target.grp; anchor=target;
      }
      moving.forEach(r=>{ const k=ALL.indexOf(r); if(k>=0)ALL.splice(k,1) });
      moving.forEach(r=>{ r.grp=grp });
      let at = anchor ? ALL.indexOf(anchor) + (below&&tr.dataset.dgrp==null ? 1 : 0) : ALL.length;
      if(at<0)at=ALL.length;
      ALL.splice(at,0,...moving);
      redraw();
    });
  });
}
function wireSel(P,list,redraw){
  P.querySelectorAll("td.gtno[data-rn]").forEach(td=>td.addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const bi=+td.dataset.rn, r=list[bi];
    if(!r)return;
    if(e.shiftKey&&tlAnchor!=null){
      const a=Math.min(tlAnchor,bi), b=Math.max(tlAnchor,bi);
      for(let k=a;k<=b;k++)tlSel.add(list[k]);
    } else if(e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey){
      if(tlSel.has(r))tlSel.delete(r); else tlSel.add(r); tlAnchor=bi;
    } else {
      /* กดเปล่า ๆ เลือกแถวเดียวเหมือนสเปรดชีต กดซ้ำที่แถวเดิมคือยกเลิก */
      const only=tlSel.size===1&&tlSel.has(r);
      tlSel.clear(); if(!only)tlSel.add(r); tlAnchor=bi;
    }
    redraw();
  }));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-gsel]").forEach(cb=>cb.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=cb.dataset.gsel, rows=list.filter(r=>r.grp===g);
    const all=rows.every(r=>tlSel.has(r));
    rows.forEach(r=>all?tlSel.delete(r):tlSel.add(r)); redraw();
  }));
  const wrx=tlCtx?tlCtx.wr:false;
  P.querySelectorAll("tr.gtr").forEach((tr,k)=>tr.addEventListener("contextmenu",e=>{
    const r=list[k]; if(!r)return;
    e.preventDefault();
    if(!tlSel.has(r)){ tlSel.clear(); tlSel.add(r); tlAnchor=k; redraw();
      setTimeout(()=>rowMenu(e.clientX,e.clientY,P,redraw,wrx),0); return }
    rowMenu(e.clientX,e.clientY,P,redraw,wrx);
  }));
  const b=id=>P.querySelector(id);
  if(b("#sclr"))b("#sclr").addEventListener("click",()=>{tlSel.clear();redraw()});
  if(b("#scopy"))b("#scopy").addEventListener("click",()=>doCopy(true));
  if(b("#spaste"))b("#spaste").addEventListener("click",async()=>{
    try{ const t=await navigator.clipboard.readText();
      if(t)applyPaste(t); else alert("Clipboard is empty — try Ctrl+V instead") }
    catch(err){ alert("the browser will not let a button read the clipboard — press Ctrl+V instead") }});
  if(b("#sdel"))b("#sdel").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(!confirm("Delete "+tlSel.size+" selected rows?"))return;
    const ALL=tlRows(); [...tlSel].forEach(r=>{const k=ALL.indexOf(r); if(k>=0)ALL.splice(k,1)});
    tlSel.clear(); redraw()});
  if(b("#sdup"))b("#sdup").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const ALL=tlRows(), add=[...tlSel].map(r=>Object.assign({},r,{name:r.name+" (copy)"}));
    add.forEach(r=>ALL.push(r)); tlSel.clear(); add.forEach(r=>tlSel.add(r)); redraw()});
}
function doCopy(viaButton){
  if(!tlCtx)return;
  const rows=tlSel.size?tlCtx.list().filter(r=>tlSel.has(r)):tlCtx.list();
  if(!rows.length)return;
  const txt=tlCtx.toTSV(rows);
  if(viaButton){
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(()=>toastTL("Copy "+rows.length+" rows copied — paste straight into Sheets"))
      .catch(()=>{ legacyCopy(txt); toastTL("Copy "+rows.length+" rows") });
  }
  return txt;
}
function legacyCopy(t){                       // สำรองไว้ถ้าเบราว์เซอร์ไม่ให้ใช้ clipboard API
  const ta=document.createElement("textarea"); ta.value=t;
  ta.style.cssText="position:fixed;opacity:0"; document.body.appendChild(ta);
  ta.select(); try{document.execCommand("copy")}catch(e){} ta.remove();
}
function applyPaste(txt){
  if(!tlCtx)return;
  const rows=tsvParse(txt); if(!rows.length)return;
  const n=tlCtx.fromTSV(rows);
  toastTL(n?"pasted "+n+" rows":"Could not read what was pasted — it has to be a tab-separated table");
}
let tlToast=null;
function toastTL(msg){
  if(tlToast)tlToast.remove();
  const d=document.createElement("div"); d.className="tltoast"; d.textContent=msg;
  document.body.appendChild(d); tlToast=d;
  setTimeout(()=>{ if(d===tlToast){d.remove();tlToast=null} },2600);
}
/* ติดตั้งครั้งเดียว หน้าอื่นไม่โดนกระทบเพราะเช็ค tlCtx ก่อนเสมอ */
document.addEventListener("copy",e=>{
  if(!tlCtx||!tlSel.size)return;
  if(String(getSelection())) return;            // ผู้ใช้เลือกข้อความอยู่ ปล่อยให้เบราว์เซอร์ทำงานปกติ
  const t=doCopy(false); if(!t)return;
  e.clipboardData.setData("text/plain",t); e.preventDefault();
  toastTL("Copy "+tlSel.size+" rows copied — paste straight into Sheets");
});
document.addEventListener("paste",e=>{
  if(!tlCtx||!tlCtx.wr)return;
  const a=document.activeElement;
  if(a&&(a.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(a.tagName)))return;
  const t=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).getData("text");
  if(!t)return; e.preventDefault(); applyPaste(t);
});
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
  if(!tlCtx)return;
  const a=document.activeElement;
  if(a&&(a.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(a.tagName)))return;
  if(e.key==="Escape"&&tlSel.size){ tlSel.clear(); tlCtx.redraw() }
  else if((e.key==="Delete"||e.key==="Backspace")&&tlSel.size&&tlCtx.wr){
    e.preventDefault();
    if(!confirm("Delete "+tlSel.size+" selected rows?"))return;
    const ALL=tlRows(); [...tlSel].forEach(r=>{const k=ALL.indexOf(r); if(k>=0)ALL.splice(k,1)});
    tlSel.clear(); tlCtx.redraw();
  }
});
/* สัปดาห์ที่ 0 คือสัปดาห์ที่จัดงาน ค่าลบคือก่อนงาน เก็บเป็นตัวเลขสัปดาห์
   ไม่ใช่วันที่ตายตัว พอเลื่อนวันงานทั้งแผนจึงขยับตามเอง */
const TLST={plan:{l:"Not started",c:"var(--tl-plan)"},doing:{l:"In progress",c:"var(--tl-doing)"},
 done:{l:"Done",c:"var(--tl-done)"},risk:{l:"Blocked",c:"var(--tl-risk)"}};
const THM=["Jan","Feb","Mar","Apr","May","Jun","Jul","Aug","Sep","Oct","Nov","Dec"];
let tlOpen={}, tlDrag=null, tlFilter="";
/* แถบที่กดเลือกไว้ กดครั้งแรกเลือก กดค้างครั้งที่สองถึงจะลากได้
   เก็บเป็นตัวแถวไม่ใช่เลขลำดับ เพราะตารางวาดใหม่แล้วลำดับเลื่อนได้ */
let tlPick=null, tlRedraw=null;
/* คำอธิบายวิธีใช้ ไม่กางค้างกลางจอ เก็บเป็นไอคอนแล้วกดดูเมื่อต้องการ
   เปิดค้างไว้ครั้งแรกครั้งเดียว ปิดแล้วจำว่าปิด */
const HINT={};
function hintBox(k,text){
  const open=HINT[k]===undefined?true:HINT[k];
  return '<div class="hintw'+(open?" on":"")+'">'+
    '<button class="ihelp" data-hint="'+k+'" title="How this works">i</button>'+
    (open?'<span class="hintt">'+esc(text)+'</span>'+
      '<button class="hintx" data-hint="'+k+'" title="Hide">×</button>':'')+
    '</div>';
}
function wireHint(P,redraw){
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-hint]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const k=b.dataset.hint; HINT[k]=HINT[k]===undefined?false:!HINT[k]; redraw() }));
}
/* แผนกับจริงเก็บแยกกัน แผนคือ a,b จริงคือ aa,ab ถ้ายังไม่มีจริงคือยังไม่ได้บันทึก
   ผลต่างนับเป็นสัปดาห์ที่ปลายแถบ บวกคือช้ากว่าแผน */
const hasAct=r=>r.aa!=null&&r.ab!=null;
const pA=r=>Math.min(r.a,r.b), pB=r=>Math.max(r.a,r.b);
const aA=r=>Math.min(r.aa,r.ab), aB=r=>Math.max(r.aa,r.ab);
const slip=r=>hasAct(r)?aB(r)-pB(r):null;
function seedAct(r){ if(!hasAct(r)){ r.aa=r.a; r.ab=r.b } }

function monday(d){ const x=new Date(d); const g=(x.getDay()+6)%7; x.setDate(x.getDate()-g); x.setHours(0,0,0,0); return x }
function tlShowWeek(){ const ev=effEventDate(EV); return ev.date?monday(new Date(ev.date+"T00:00:00")):monday(new Date()) }
function tlWeekStart(k){ const d=new Date(tlShowWeek()); d.setDate(d.getDate()+k*7); return d }
function tlNowWeek(){ return Math.round((monday(new Date())-tlShowWeek())/604800000) }
function tlDate(k){ const d=tlWeekStart(k); d.setDate(d.getDate()+6);
  return d.getDate()+" "+THM[d.getMonth()] }
function tlRows(){ return EV.timeline||(EV.timeline=[]) }
function tlGroups(){ const o=[]; tlRows().forEach(r=>{ if(!o.includes(r.grp))o.push(r.grp) }); return o }

/* เติมงานจากโครงมาตรฐานเฉพาะช่วงที่เปิดอยู่ เทียบชื่อที่ตัดอักขระพิเศษออกแล้ว
   จะได้ไม่เติมซ้ำของที่มีอยู่ */
function tlFill(ph){
  const R=tlRows(), key=x=>String(x||"").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9ก-๙]/g,"");
  const have=new Set(R.filter(r=>(r.ph||"pre")===ph).map(r=>key(r.name)));
  const add=(D.timelineTemplate||[]).filter(t=>(t.ph||"pre")===ph&&!have.has(key(t.name)));
  if(!add.length){alert("This phase already matches the standard set");return 0}
  add.forEach(t=>R.push(Object.assign({},t)));
  /* จัดให้งานหมวดเดียวกันอยู่ติดกัน เรียงตามลำดับที่เจอครั้งแรก */
  const ord=[]; R.forEach(r=>{if(!ord.includes(r.grp))ord.push(r.grp)});
  R.sort((x,y)=>ord.indexOf(x.grp)-ord.indexOf(y.grp));
  alert("Added "+add.length+" items in this phase");
  return add.length;
}
/* ───── แก้ได้ทุกอย่าง: วันจัดงาน ช่วงเวลาที่วาด และคอลัมน์ของตัวเอง ───── */

/* วันจัดงานเป็นตัวตั้งของทั้งไทม์ไลน์ แก้ตรงนี้แล้วทุกแถบขยับตาม */
function tlDateBar(wr){
  const ev=effEventDate(EV), a=ev.date||"", b=EV.end_date||a;
  const nb=EV.buildDays==null?1:EV.buildDays, ns=EV.strikeDays==null?1:EV.strikeDays;
  /* ตั้งครั้งเดียวตอนเริ่มงาน ไม่ต้องกางค้างไว้ทุกครั้งที่เปิดหน้า */
  return '<details class="dwrap"'+(a?'':' open')+'><summary>'+
    (a?'Show dates · '+esc(a)+(b&&b!==a?' – '+esc(b):'')+
       ' · build-up '+nb+'d · teardown '+ns+'d':'Set the show dates')+'</summary>'+
   '<div class="dbar">'+
   '<span class="dbl">Show date</span>'+
   (wr?'<input type="date" id="evd1" value="'+esc(a)+'">'
      :'<b>'+esc(a)+'</b>')+
   '<span class="dbs">to</span>'+
   (wr?'<input type="date" id="evd2" value="'+esc(b)+'">':'<b>'+esc(b)+'</b>')+
   '<span class="dbs">Build-up before</span>'+
   (wr?'<input type="number" id="evbd" min="0" max="7" value="'+nb+'">':'<b>'+nb+'</b>')+
   '<span class="dbs">days · teardown after</span>'+
   (wr?'<input type="number" id="evsd" min="0" max="7" value="'+ns+'">':'<b>'+ns+'</b>')+
   '<span class="dbs">day</span>'+
   (ev.rolled?'<span class="dbw">moved from '+esc(ev.orig)+' forward to next year automatically</span>':'')+
   '</div></details>';
}
function wireDateBar(P,redraw){
  const d1=P.querySelector("#evd1"); if(!d1)return;
  const set=()=>{
    const a=P.querySelector("#evd1").value, b=P.querySelector("#evd2").value;
    if(a){ EV.eventDate=a; EV.event_date=a; EV.end_date=(b&&b>=a)?b:a;
      EV.dates=fmtRange(a,EV.end_date) }
    EV.buildDays=Math.max(0,+P.querySelector("#evbd").value||0);
    EV.strikeDays=Math.max(0,+P.querySelector("#evsd").value||0);
    onDay=null; redraw();
  };
  ["#evd1","#evd2","#evbd","#evsd"].forEach(k=>{
    const el=P.querySelector(k); if(el)el.addEventListener("change",set)});
}
function fmtRange(a,b){
  const x=new Date(a+"T00:00:00"), y=new Date(b+"T00:00:00");
  const yy=y.getFullYear();
  return x.getDate()+(x.getMonth()===y.getMonth()?"":" "+THM[x.getMonth()])+
    (a===b?"":"–"+y.getDate())+" "+THM[y.getMonth()]+" "+yy;
}

/* คอลัมน์ของตัวเอง วางท้ายตารางเหมือนช่อง Date กับ Remark ในชีตเดิม */
function tlCols(){ return EV.tlCols||(EV.tlCols=[]) }
function colHead(wr){
  return tlCols().map((c,ci)=>'<th class="gtx3" rowspan="2">'+
    (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-cl="'+ci+'">'+esc(c.l)+'</span>'+
        '<button class="gtdel gtdel2" data-cd="'+ci+'" title="Delete this column">×</button>':esc(c.l))+
    '</th>').join("");
}
function colCells(r,i,wr){
  return tlCols().map(c=>'<td class="gtx3">'+
    (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-cv="'+i+'" data-ck="'+esc(c.k)+'">'+
        esc((r.x||{})[c.k]||"—")+'</span>':esc((r.x||{})[c.k]||""))+'</td>').join("");
}
function wireCols(P,R,redraw){
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-cl]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(); if(t)tlCols()[+el.dataset.cl].l=t; redraw()}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-cd]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){ const c=tlCols().splice(+b.dataset.cd,1)[0];
      tlRows().forEach(r=>{ if(r.x)delete r.x[c.k] }); redraw(); return }
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="×";b.style.color=""}},4000)}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-cv]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const r=R[+el.dataset.cv]; (r.x=r.x||{})[el.dataset.ck]=
      el.textContent.trim().replace(/^—$/,""); redraw()}));
  const ab=P.querySelector("#colnew");
  if(ab)ab.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const l=prompt("New column name","Budget"); if(!l)return;
    tlCols().push({k:"c"+Date2()+tlCols().length,l:l.trim()}); redraw()});
}
/* เลี่ยง Date.now ในบางสภาพแวดล้อม ใช้ตัวนับของเราเอง */
let _ck=0; function Date2(){ return (++_ck)+"" }
/* เดือนกับสัปดาห์แปลงกลับไปมาได้ จะได้ให้ผู้ใช้กำหนดช่วงเป็นเดือนตรงๆ */
function monthWeek(ms,end){
  const p=String(ms||"").split("-"), y=+p[0], m=+p[1];
  if(!y||!m)return null;
  const d=end?new Date(y,m,0):new Date(y,m-1,1);
  return Math.round((monday(d)-tlShowWeek())/604800000);
}
function weekMonth(k){ const d=tlWeekStart(k);
  return d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0") }
function shiftMonth(ms,n){ const p=String(ms).split("-");
  const d=new Date(+p[0],+p[1]-1+n,1);
  return d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0") }
function tlRange(){ return EV.tlRange||(EV.tlRange={}) }
/* ช่วงที่พอดีกับงานจริง เผื่อหัวท้ายข้างละหนึ่งเดือน
   เปิดมาต้องเห็นแถบทันที ไม่ใช่เห็นช่องว่างแล้วต้องเลื่อนหา */
function fitRange(R){
  if(!R.length)return null;
  return [shiftMonth(weekMonth(Math.min(...R.map(pA))),-1),
          shiftMonth(weekMonth(Math.max(...R.map(pB))),1)];
}
function monthBar(ph,lo,hi,nOut){
  const r=tlRange()[ph];
  const f=r?r[0]:weekMonth(lo), t=r?r[1]:weekMonth(hi);
  const [y1,m1]=f.split("-").map(Number), [y2,m2]=t.split("-").map(Number);
  const n=(y2-y1)*12+(m2-m1)+1;
  const z=(k,l,tip)=>'<button class="mb'+(n===k?" on":"")+'" data-zoom="'+k+'" title="'+esc(tip)+'">'+l+'</button>';
  return '<div class="mbar">'+
   '<div class="mbz">'+
     z(3,"Quarter","Show three months")+z(6,"6 months","Show six months")+
     z(12,"Year","Show a full year")+
     '<button class="mb" id="mfit" title="Zoom so every task fits on screen">Fit to tasks</button>'+
     '<button class="mb" id="mtoday" title="Jump to the column for this week">Today</button>'+
   '</div>'+
   '<button class="mb mbtog" id="mrangetog" aria-expanded="false" '+
     'title="Set the exact months to show">'+f+' – '+t+' · '+n+' months ▾</button>'+
   '<div class="mbset" id="mrangebox" hidden>'+
     '<button class="mb" data-mv="a-">−</button><input type="month" id="mfrom" value="'+f+'">'+
     '<button class="mb" data-mv="a+">+</button><span class="dbs">to</span>'+
     '<button class="mb" data-mv="b-">−</button><input type="month" id="mto" value="'+t+'">'+
     '<button class="mb" data-mv="b+">+</button>'+
   '</div>'+
   (nOut?'<span class="dbw">'+nOut+' tasks outside this range — see ◀ ▶ at the end of the row</span>':'')+
   '</div>';
}
function wireMonthBar(P,ph,lo,hi,redraw){
  const cur=()=>tlRange()[ph]||[weekMonth(lo),weekMonth(hi)];
  const set=(a,b)=>{ if(((+b.split("-")[0])*12+ +b.split("-")[1])<
      ((+a.split("-")[0])*12+ +a.split("-")[1])){alert("The end month cannot be before the start month");return}
    tlRange()[ph]=[a,b]; (EV.tlRangeSet=EV.tlRangeSet||{})[ph]=true; redraw() };
  const f=P.querySelector("#mfrom"), t=P.querySelector("#mto");
  if(f)f.addEventListener("change",()=>set(f.value,t.value));
  if(t)t.addEventListener("change",()=>set(f.value,t.value));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-mv]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const c=cur(), k=b.dataset.mv;
    if(k==="a-")set(shiftMonth(c[0],-1),c[1]);
    else if(k==="a+")set(shiftMonth(c[0],1),c[1]);
    else if(k==="b-")set(c[0],shiftMonth(c[1],-1));
    else set(c[0],shiftMonth(c[1],1));
  }));
  const tog=P.querySelector("#mrangetog"), box=P.querySelector("#mrangebox");
  if(tog)tog.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    box.hidden=!box.hidden; tog.setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(!box.hidden));
    tog.classList.toggle("on",!box.hidden) });
  /* ซูมเป็นจำนวนเดือน โดยยึดกลางช่วงที่เห็นอยู่ไว้ */
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-zoom]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const k=+b.dataset.zoom, c=cur();
    const v=x=>{const p=x.split("-");return +p[0]*12+ +p[1]};
    const mid=(v(c[0])+v(c[1]))/2, half=(k-1)/2;
    const at=n=>{const y=Math.floor((n-1)/12), m=((n-1)%12)+1;
      return y+"-"+String(m).padStart(2,"0")};
    set(at(Math.round(mid-half)),at(Math.round(mid+half)));
  }));
  const fb=P.querySelector("#mfit");
  if(fb)fb.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    delete tlRange()[ph]; if(EV.tlRangeSet)delete EV.tlRangeSet[ph]; redraw() });
  const tb=P.querySelector("#mtoday");
  if(tb)tb.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const el=P.querySelector("th.gtnow")||P.querySelector("td.gtnow");
    if(el){ el.scrollIntoView({block:"nearest",inline:"center"}); toastTL("Jumped to this week") }
    else toastTL("This week is outside the range shown");
  });
}
function pTimeline(ph){
  tlEnter(ph); tlRedraw=()=>pTimeline(ph);
  const wr=can("timeline")==="write", ALL=tlRows(), now=tlNowWeek();
  const R=ALL.filter(r=>(r.ph||"pre")===ph);
  const PH={pre:{t:"Pre-event",d:"Work that has to be done before the show, counted back in weeks"},
            post:{t:"Post-event",d:"Work that has to be closed out after the show, counted forward in weeks"}}[ph];
  if(!ALL.length){
    const T=D.timelineTemplate||[];
    $("#page").innerHTML='<div class="note"><p><b>This event has no timeline yet</b></p>'+
     '<p>Start from the standard set '+T.length+' items, split into pre-event '+
     T.filter(x=>(x.ph||"pre")==="pre").length+' · On-site '+
     T.filter(x=>x.ph==="on").length+' · Post-event '+T.filter(x=>x.ph==="post").length+
     ' then trim or add to fit this event</p></div>'+
     (wr?'<div class="addbar"><button id="tlstd">Use standard set</button> '+
         '<button id="tlblank">Start from scratch</button></div>':'');
    const b=$("#tlstd"); if(b)b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
      EV.timeline=(D.timelineTemplate||[]).map(x=>Object.assign({},x)); pTimeline(ph)});
    const b2=$("#tlblank"); if(b2)b2.addEventListener("click",()=>{
      EV.timeline=[{grp:"New category",name:"New task",by:"Event",a:-4,b:-4,st:"",note:"",ph:ph}];
      pTimeline(ph)});
    return;
  }
  if(!R.length){
    $("#page").innerHTML='<div class="note"><p><b>No tasks in the'+PH.t+'</b></p><p>'+PH.d+'</p></div>'+
     (wr?'<div class="addbar"><button id="tlfill2">Fill from standard set</button></div>':'');
    const b=$("#tlfill2"); if(b)b.addEventListener("click",()=>{ tlFill(ph); pTimeline(ph)});
    return;
  }
  /* ช่วงสัปดาห์ที่ต้องวาด กว้างพอคลุมทุกงาน บวกสัปดาห์วันนี้ */
  const ra=R.map(r=>Math.min(r.a,r.b)), rb=R.map(r=>Math.max(r.a,r.b));
  /* tLo tHi คือขอบเขตของงานจริง ใช้ยืดช่วงให้ไม่มีงานหลุด
     lo hi คือช่วงที่วาดเมื่อยังไม่มีช่วงมาตรฐาน รวมสัปดาห์ปัจจุบันเข้าไปด้วย */
  const tLo=ph==="post"?Math.min(0,...ra):Math.min(-2,...ra),
        tHi=ph==="post"?Math.max(1,...rb):Math.max(0,...rb);
  const lo=Math.min(tLo,ph==="post"?0:(now<=0?now:0)),
        hi=Math.max(tHi,ph==="post"?(now>=0?now:0):0);
  EV.tlRangeSet=EV.tlRangeSet||{};
  if(!tlRange()[ph]){ const ft=fitRange(R); if(ft)tlRange()[ph]=ft }
  const rg=tlRange()[ph];
  let LO=lo, HI=hi;
  if(rg){ const a=monthWeek(rg[0],false), b=monthWeek(rg[1],true);
    if(a!=null&&b!=null&&b>=a){ LO=a; HI=b } }
  /* ช่วงมาตรฐานตั้งไว้ก่อน แต่ถ้ามีงานเลยออกไปก็ยืดให้เอง งานจะได้ไม่หายไปเงียบ ๆ
     เฉพาะตอนที่ผู้ใช้ยังไม่ได้ตั้งช่วงเอง */
  if(!EV.tlRangeSet[ph]&&rg){ LO=Math.min(LO,tLo); HI=Math.max(HI,tHi) }
  const cols=[]; for(let k=LO;k<=HI;k++)cols.push(k);
  const outN=R.filter(r=>pB(r)<LO||pA(r)>HI).length;
  /* หัวเดือน รวมสัปดาห์ที่อยู่เดือนเดียวกัน */
  const mons=[], band={};
  cols.forEach(k=>{ const d=tlWeekStart(k), key=d.getFullYear()+"-"+d.getMonth();
    const last=mons[mons.length-1];
    let first=false;
    if(last&&last.key===key)last.n++; else { mons.push({key,n:1,
      l:THM[d.getMonth()]+" "+d.getFullYear()}); first=true }
    band[k]=(mons.length%2?"":" gtalt")+(first?" gtms":"") });

  const ev=effEventDate(EV);

  let h=tlDateBar(wr)+'<p class="edhint" style="margin:2px 0 12px">'+PH.d+'</p>';
  h+=selBar([...tlSel].filter(r=>R.includes(r)).length,wr);
  h+='<div class="tlbar">'+
    '<input id="tlq" class="cell" placeholder="Search task or team" value="'+esc(tlFilter)+'" style="max-width:260px">'+
    '<span class="tlleg"><i class="lgplan"></i>Plan'+Object.keys(TLST).map(k=>
      '<i style="background:'+TLST[k].c+'"></i>'+TLST[k].l).join("")+'</span>'+
    dlBtn("dltl","Download CSV")+
    (wr?' <button class="act" id="tlfill" style="padding:9px 15px">Fill from standard set</button>'+
        ' <button class="act" id="colnew" style="padding:9px 15px">+ Add column</button>':'')+
   '</div>';
  h+=monthBar(ph,lo,hi,outN);
  if(wr)h+=hintBox("gantt","The top outlined bar is the plan, the solid bar under it is what actually "+
    "happened. Click a bar once to select it, then drag its ends to resize or its middle to move it. "+
    "Once a task becomes in progress or done, the actual bar starts as a copy of the plan — "+
    "drag it to match what really happened.");

  h+='<div class="tw"><table class="gt"><thead>'+
   '<tr><th class="gtno" rowspan="2"></th>'+
   '<th class="gtn" rowspan="2">Task · note</th>'+
   '<th class="gts" rowspan="2">Status</th>'+
   '<th class="gtby" rowspan="2">Owner</th>'+
   '<th class="gtd gtd2" rowspan="2">Due by<br><small>Plan / actual</small></th>'+
   mons.map(m=>'<th colspan="'+m.n+'" class="gtm">'+m.l+'</th>').join("")+
   colHead(wr)+'</tr><tr>'+
   cols.map(k=>{const d=tlWeekStart(k), w=Math.floor((d.getDate()-1)/7)+1;
     return '<th class="gtw'+band[k]+(k===now?" gtnow":"")+(k===0?" gtshow":"")+'">W'+w+'</th>'}).join("")+
   '</tr></thead><tbody>';

  const q=tlFilter.trim().toLowerCase();
  let rowNo=0;                       // เลขแถวไล่ต่อเนื่องทั้งตาราง เหมือนสเปรดชีต
  const G=[]; R.forEach(r=>{if(!G.includes(r.grp))G.push(r.grp)});
  G.forEach(g=>{
    const idx=R.map((r,i)=>[r,i]).filter(([r])=>r.grp===g)
      .filter(([r])=>!q||(r.name+" "+r.by+" "+(r.note||"")).toLowerCase().includes(q));
    if(q&&!idx.length)return;
    const gd=idx.filter(([r])=>r.st==="done").length, op=tlOpen[g]!==false;
    const ga=Math.min(...idx.map(([r])=>pA(r))),
          gb=Math.max(...idx.map(([r])=>pB(r)));
    const pct=Math.round(gd/idx.length*100);
    h+='<tr class="gtg" data-dgrp="'+esc(g)+'">'+grpNo(g)+'<td class="gtn gtgh" colspan="4">'+
      '<button class="gtx" data-gt="'+esc(g)+'">'+(op?"▾":"▸")+'</button>'+
      (wr?'<span class="gtgn" contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-gn="'+esc(g)+'">'+esc(g)+'</span>'
         :'<span class="gtgn">'+esc(g)+'</span>')+
      '<span class="gtpb"><i style="width:'+pct+'%"></i></span>'+
      '<small>'+gd+'/'+idx.length+'</small>'+
      (wr&&op?'<button class="gtx2" data-ga="'+esc(g)+'">+ Add task</button>':'')+'</td>'+
      cols.map(k=>'<td class="gtc'+band[k]+(k===now?" gtnow":"")+'">'+
        (k===ga&&k===gb?'<i class="gsum gsone"></i>'
         :k===ga?'<i class="gsum gsa"></i>':k===gb?'<i class="gsum gsb"></i>'
         :(k>ga&&k<gb?'<i class="gsum"></i>':''))+'</td>').join("")+
      tlCols().map(()=>'<td class="gtx3"></td>').join("")+'</tr>';
    if(!op)return;
    idx.forEach(([r,i])=>{
      const a=pA(r), b=pB(r), ha=hasAct(r), xa=ha?aA(r):0, xb=ha?aB(r):-1;
      const st=TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan, isLate=r.st!=="done"&&b<now, sl=slip(r);
      h+='<tr class="gtr'+(tlSel.has(r)?" sel":"")+'" data-drow="'+i+'">'+selNo(rowNo++)+'<td class="gtn">'+
        (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-tn="'+i+'">'+esc(r.name)+'</span>':esc(r.name))+
        (wr?' <button class="gtdel" data-tx="'+i+'" title="Delete this task">×</button>':'')+
        '<br><small class="gtnote">'+
        (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-tnote="'+i+'">'+esc(r.note||"—")+'</span>'
           :esc(r.note||"—"))+'</small></td>'+
      '<td class="gts">'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-tst="'+i+'">'+
        Object.keys(TLST).map(k=>'<option value="'+k+'"'+((r.st||"plan")===k?" selected":"")+'>'+
          TLST[k].l+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
        :'<span style="color:'+st.c+';font-weight:700">'+st.l+'</span>')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="gtby">'+(wr
        ?'<button class="tmb" data-tby="'+i+'" title="Choose which teams own this task">'+
          teamChips(r)+'</button>'
        :'<span class="tmb ro">'+teamChips(r)+'</span>')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="gtd gtd2">'+
        '<span class="dpl">'+tlDate(b)+'</span>'+
        (ha?'<span class="dac'+(sl>0?" bad":sl<0?" good":"")+'">'+tlDate(xb)+
              (sl?(sl>0?" late ":" early ")+Math.abs(sl)+" weeks":" On plan")+'</span>'
           :'<span class="dac none">'+(isLate?"Overdue "+(now-b)+" weeks":"Not recorded")+'</span>')+
        '</td>'+
      cols.map(k=>{
        const pkP=tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w==="plan";
        const pkA=tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w==="act";
        const tip=(pkP||pkA)
          ? "Drag the ends to resize, the middle to move the whole bar"
          : (ha?"Click the top bar for the plan, the lower bar for what actually happened"
               :"Click the bar to select it, then drag to change the weeks");
        return '<td class="gtc'+band[k]+(k===now?" gtnow":"")+'" data-r="'+i+'" data-k="'+k+
        '" title="'+tip+'">'+
        (k>=a&&k<=b?'<i class="gplan'+(k===a?" ba":"")+(k===b?" bb":"")+(pkP?" pk":"")+'"></i>':'')+
        (k>=xa&&k<=xb?'<i class="gact'+(k===xa?" ba":"")+(k===xb?" bb":"")+(pkA?" pk":"")+
          '" style="background:'+(isLate?"var(--tl-risk)":st.c)+'"></i>':'')+
        (k===LO&&b<LO?'<i class="goff" title="is before the range shown">◀</i>':'')+
        (k===HI&&a>HI?'<i class="goff r" title="is after the range shown">▶</i>':'')+
        '</td>'}).join("")+colCells(r,i,wr)+'</tr>';
    });
  });
  h+='</tbody></table></div>';
  if(wr)h+='<div class="addbar"><button id="tlgadd">+ Add category</button></div>';
  $("#page").innerHTML=h;
  const P=$("#page");
  const iso=k=>ymd(tlWeekStart(k));
  tlCtx={ wr, redraw:()=>pTimeline(ph), list:()=>R,
    toTSV(rows){
      const head=["Category","Task","Team","Status","Plan start","Plan finish","Actual start","Actual finish","Note"]
        .concat(tlCols().map(c=>c.l));
      return [tsvLine(head)].concat(rows.map(r=>tsvLine(
        [r.grp,r.name,r.by||"",(TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan).l,
         iso(pA(r)),iso(pB(r)),hasAct(r)?iso(aA(r)):"",hasAct(r)?iso(aB(r)):"",r.note||""]
        .concat(tlCols().map(c=>(r.x||{})[c.k]||""))))).join("\n");
    },
    fromTSV(rows){
      /* ข้ามบรรทัดหัวตาราง ถ้าคัดลอกมาทั้งตาราง */
      if(rows[0]&&/^(หมวด|งาน|ลำดับ|รายการ)$/.test((rows[0][0]||"").trim()))rows=rows.slice(1);
      const ALL=tlRows(), gs=[...new Set(R.map(r=>r.grp))];
      let lastG=(tlSel.size?[...tlSel][0].grp:null)||gs[0]||"New category", n=0;
      rows.forEach(c=>{
        const one=c.length<2;
        const grp=one?lastG:((c[0]||"").trim()||lastG);
        const name=(one?c[0]:c[1]||"").trim();
        if(!name)return;
        lastG=grp;
        const w1=weekOf(c[4]), w2=weekOf(c[5]), a1=weekOf(c[6]), a2=weekOf(c[7]);
        const r={grp,name,by:(c[2]||"").trim(),st:stKey(c[3]),
          a:w1!=null?w1:(ph==="post"?1:-4), b:w2!=null?w2:(w1!=null?w1:(ph==="post"?1:-4)),
          note:(c[8]||"").trim(),ph:ph,x:{}};
        if(a1!=null){ r.aa=a1; r.ab=a2!=null?a2:a1 }
        tlCols().forEach((cc,ci)=>{ const v=(c[9+ci]||"").trim(); if(v)r.x[cc.k]=v });
        ALL.push(r); n++;
      });
      if(n){ const ord=[]; ALL.forEach(r=>{if(!ord.includes(r.grp))ord.push(r.grp)});
        ALL.sort((x,y)=>ord.indexOf(x.grp)-ord.indexOf(y.grp)); pTimeline(ph) }
      return n;
    }};
  wireSel(P,R,()=>pTimeline(ph));
  wireHint(P,()=>pTimeline(ph));
  if(wr)wireDrag(P,R,()=>pTimeline(ph));
  /* เปิดมาให้เห็นช่วงที่มีงานจริง ไม่ค้างอยู่กับสัปดาห์ว่างทางซ้าย */
  const box=P.querySelector(".tw");
  if(box){ const first=Math.min(...R.map(r=>Math.min(r.a,r.b)));
    const cell=box.querySelector("th.gtw"); const w=cell?cell.offsetWidth:26;
    box.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,(first-LO-2)*w); }

  P.querySelector("#tlq").addEventListener("input",e=>{
    tlFilter=e.target.value; const p=e.target.selectionStart; pTimeline(ph);
    const n=$("#tlq"); n.focus(); n.setSelectionRange(p,p);});
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-gt]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=b.dataset.gt; tlOpen[g]=tlOpen[g]===false; pTimeline(ph)}));
  wireDateBar(P,()=>pTimeline(ph));
  if(!wr){ wireTlCsv(P,ph); return }

  /* กดแถบครั้งแรกเพื่อเลือก กดค้างบนแถบที่เลือกไว้แล้วถึงจะลาก
     จับปลายซ้ายขวาเพื่อยืดหด จับกลางเพื่อเลื่อนทั้งแถบโดยความยาวเท่าเดิม
     ไม่ต้องมีสวิตช์บอกว่ากำลังแก้แผนหรือของจริงอีกแล้ว ดูจากแถบที่กด */
  P.querySelectorAll("td.gtc[data-r]").forEach(td=>{
    td.addEventListener("mousedown",e=>{
      e.preventDefault();
      const i=+td.dataset.r, k=+td.dataset.k, r=R[i], ha=hasAct(r);
      const y=e.clientY-td.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      const w=(ha&&y>=20)?"act":"plan";                 // แถบแผนอยู่บน แถบจริงอยู่ล่าง
      if(!(tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w===w)){
        tlPick={row:r,w:w}; pTimeline(ph); return;      // ครั้งแรก แค่เลือก
      }
      const a=w==="act"?aA(r):pA(r), b=w==="act"?aB(r):pB(r);
      const mode = k<a||k>b ? (Math.abs(k-a)<=Math.abs(k-b)?"a":"b")
                 : k===a&&k===b ? "b"
                 : k===a ? "a" : k===b ? "b" : "move";
      tlDrag={row:r,w:w,mode:mode,a:a,b:b,k0:k,lo:a,hi:b,r:i};
      paintDrag(P);
    });
    td.addEventListener("mouseenter",()=>{
      if(!tlDrag)return;
      const k=+td.dataset.k, d=tlDrag;
      if(d.mode==="move"){ const sh=k-d.k0; d.lo=d.a+sh; d.hi=d.b+sh }
      else if(d.mode==="a"){ d.lo=Math.min(k,d.b); d.hi=Math.max(k,d.b) }
      else { d.lo=Math.min(d.a,k); d.hi=Math.max(d.a,k) }
      paintDrag(P);
    });
  });
  document.addEventListener("mouseup",tlUp);
  function tlUp(){ document.removeEventListener("mouseup",tlUp);
    if(!tlDrag)return; const d=tlDrag; tlDrag=null;
    const r=d.row, a=Math.min(d.lo,d.hi), b=Math.max(d.lo,d.hi);
    if(a===d.a&&b===d.b){ pTimeline(ph); return }        // กดแล้วไม่ได้ลาก ไม่ต้องแก้อะไร
    if(d.w==="act"){ r.aa=a; r.ab=b } else { r.a=a; r.b=b }
    pTimeline(ph); }

  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tn]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(); if(t)R[+el.dataset.tn].name=t; pTimeline(ph)}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tby]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>
    teamMenu(b,R[+b.dataset.tby],()=>pTimeline(ph))));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tnote]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    R[+el.dataset.tnote].note=el.textContent.trim().replace(/^—$/,""); pTimeline(ph)}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tst]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const r=R[+el.dataset.tst]; r.st=el.value;
    /* พอเริ่มลงมือแล้ว ตั้งแถบจริงตามแผนไว้ก่อน จะได้มีอะไรให้ลากปรับ */
    if(["doing","done","risk"].includes(r.st))seedAct(r);
    pTimeline(ph)}));
  wireMonthBar(P,ph,lo,hi,()=>pTimeline(ph));
  wireCols(P,R,()=>pTimeline(ph));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-gn]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const o=el.dataset.gn, n=el.textContent.trim();
    if(n&&n!==o){R.forEach(r=>{if(r.grp===o)r.grp=n}); tlOpen[n]=tlOpen[o]} pTimeline(ph)}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-ga]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=b.dataset.ga, last=R.filter(r=>r.grp===g).pop();
    const at=ALL.lastIndexOf(last)+1||ALL.length;
    ALL.splice(at,0,{grp:g,name:"New task",by:last?last.by:"Event",
      a:last?last.b:(ph==="post"?1:-4),b:last?last.b:(ph==="post"?1:-4),
      st:"",note:"",ph:ph}); pTimeline(ph)}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-tx]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){const t=R[+b.dataset.tx]; const k=ALL.indexOf(t);
      if(k>=0)ALL.splice(k,1); pTimeline(ph); return}
    b.dataset.armed="1"; const o=b.textContent; b.textContent="Delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent=o;b.style.color=""}},4000)}));
  P.querySelector("#tlgadd").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    ALL.push({grp:"New category "+(G.length+1),name:"New task",by:"Event",
      a:ph==="post"?1:-4,b:ph==="post"?1:-4,st:"",note:"",ph:ph});
    pTimeline(ph)});
  const fb=P.querySelector("#tlfill");
  if(fb)fb.addEventListener("click",()=>{ tlFill(ph); pTimeline(ph)});
  wireTlCsv(P,ph);
}
/* ───────── ระหว่างงาน ─────────
   ช่วงนี้ใช้หน่วยเป็นวันและชั่วโมง ไม่ใช่สัปดาห์ จึงเป็นตารางรันคิวรายวัน
   วันจริงคำนวณจากวันจัดงาน บวกวันเซ็ตอัปหน้าหนึ่งวัน และวันรื้อถอนท้ายหนึ่งวัน */
/* ช่วงชั่วโมงที่วาด ปรับได้ต่องาน ค่าเริ่มต้น 07:00 ถึง 23:00 */
const onH0=()=>EV.onH0==null?7:EV.onH0, onH1=()=>EV.onH1==null?23:EV.onH1;
let onDay=null;
function tlDays(){
  const ev=effEventDate(EV); if(!ev.date)return [];
  const a=new Date(ev.date+"T00:00:00");
  const e=EV.end_date?new Date(EV.end_date+"T00:00:00"):new Date(a);
  if(e<a)e.setTime(a.getTime());
  const nb=EV.buildDays==null?1:EV.buildDays, ns=EV.strikeDays==null?1:EV.strikeDays;
  const out=[];
  for(let i=nb;i>=1;i--){ const x=new Date(a); x.setDate(x.getDate()-i);
    out.push({d:x,kind:"build",l:nb>1?"Build-up "+(nb-i+1):"Build-up"}) }
  let n=1;
  for(let x=new Date(a); x<=e; x.setDate(x.getDate()+1))
    out.push({d:new Date(x),kind:"show",l:"Show day "+(n++)});
  for(let i=1;i<=ns;i++){ const x=new Date(e); x.setDate(x.getDate()+i);
    out.push({d:x,kind:"strike",l:ns>1?"Teardown "+i:"Teardown"}) }
  return out;
}
const hm=t=>{const [a,b]=String(t||"09:00").split(":");return (+a)+(+b||0)/60};
const mh=v=>{const h=Math.floor(v),m=Math.round((v-h)*60);
  return String(h).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(m).padStart(2,"0")};
function onRows(){ return tlRows().filter(r=>r.ph==="on") }
const hasActT=r=>!!(r.at1&&r.at2);
function seedActT(r){ if(!hasActT(r)){ r.at1=r.t1; r.at2=r.t2 } }
const slipMin=r=>hasActT(r)?Math.round((hm(r.at2)-hm(r.t2))*60):null;

function pTimelineOn(){
  tlEnter("on"); tlRedraw=pTimelineOn;
  const wr=can("timeline")==="write", ALL=tlRows(), days=tlDays();
  const R=onRows();
  if(!R.length){
    $("#page").innerHTML='<div class="note"><p><b>No on-site run sheet yet</b></p>'+
     '<p>This phase is not counted in weeks like the other two — it is a daily run sheet broken down by hour '+
     'from build-up day, through each show day, to teardown</p></div>'+
     (wr?'<div class="addbar"><button id="onfill">Use standard set</button></div>':'');
    const b=$("#onfill"); if(b)b.addEventListener("click",()=>{ tlFill("on"); pTimelineOn()});
    return;
  }
  if(!days.length){ $("#page").innerHTML='<div class="note"><p>No show date has been set</p></div>'; return }
  /* วัน -1 ในโครงมาตรฐานหมายถึงวันสุดท้าย แปลงเป็นเลขวันจริงตอนใช้งาน */
  R.forEach(r=>{ if(r.d<0||r.d>=days.length)r.d=days.length-1 });
  if(onDay==null||onDay>=days.length)onDay=Math.max(0,days.findIndex(x=>x.kind==="show"));
  const day=days[onDay], list=R.filter(r=>r.d===onDay);
  const H0=onH0(), H1=Math.max(onH1(),H0+2);
  const HR=[]; for(let x=H0;x<=H1;x++)HR.push(x);
  const span=H1-H0;
  const pc=v=>((Math.min(Math.max(v,H0),H1)-H0)/span*100);

  let h=tlDateBar(wr);
  h+='<div class="daytabs">'+days.map((d,i)=>
    '<button class="dtb'+(i===onDay?" on":"")+' k-'+d.kind+'" data-od="'+i+'">'+
    esc(d.l)+'<small>'+ymdTH(d.d)+'</small>'+
    '<b>'+R.filter(r=>r.d===i).length+'</b></button>').join("")+'</div>';
  h+='<div class="tlbar">'+
    '<span class="tlleg"><i class="lgplan"></i>Plan'+Object.keys(TLST).map(k=>
      '<i style="background:'+TLST[k].c+'"></i>'+TLST[k].l).join("")+'</span>'+
    dlBtn("dlon","Download CSV")+
    (wr?' <button class="act" id="onfill2" style="padding:9px 15px">Fill from standard set</button>'+
        ' <button class="act" id="oncopy" style="padding:9px 15px">Copy run sheet to another day</button>'+
        ' <button class="act" id="colnew" style="padding:9px 15px">+ Add column</button>':'')+
   '</div>'+
   (wr?'<div class="dbar dbar2"><span class="dbl">Hours shown</span>'+
     '<input type="number" id="onh0" min="0" max="20" value="'+H0+'"><span class="dbs">to</span>'+
     '<input type="number" id="onh1" min="2" max="24" value="'+H1+'"><span class="dbs"></span></div>':'');
  h+=selBar([...tlSel].filter(r=>list.includes(r)).length,wr);
  if(wr)h+=hintBox("run","The thin bar on top is the planned time, the thick bar below is what actually "+
    "happened. Pick which one your dragging edits, then drag on the time bar — it snaps to half hours.");

  h+='<div class="tw"><table class="gt onx"><thead><tr>'+
   '<th class="gtno"></th>'+
   '<th class="gtn">Run-sheet item · note</th><th class="gts">Status</th>'+
   '<th class="gtby">Owner</th>'+
   '<th class="gtd gtd2">Time<br><small>Plan / actual</small></th>'+
   HR.map(x=>'<th class="onh">'+String(x).padStart(2,"0")+'</th>').join("")+
   colHead(wr)+'</tr></thead><tbody>';
  const grps=[]; list.forEach(r=>{if(!grps.includes(r.grp))grps.push(r.grp)});
  let rowNo=0;
  if(!grps.length)h+='<tr><td class="gtn" colspan="'+(4+HR.length+tlCols().length)+'" style="padding:22px 14px;'+
    'color:var(--ink3)">No run-sheet items today — press + Add run-sheet item below</td></tr>';
  grps.forEach(g=>{
    const idx=R.map((r,i)=>[r,i]).filter(([r])=>r.d===onDay&&r.grp===g);
    const gd=idx.filter(([r])=>r.st==="done").length;
    const pct=Math.round(gd/idx.length*100);
    h+='<tr class="gtg" data-dgrp="'+esc(g)+'">'+grpNo(g)+'<td class="gtn gtgh" colspan="4">'+
      '<span class="gtgn">'+esc(g)+'</span>'+
      '<span class="gtpb"><i style="width:'+pct+'%"></i></span><small>'+gd+'/'+idx.length+'</small>'+
      (wr?'<button class="gtx2" data-oa="'+esc(g)+'">+ Add run-sheet item</button>':'')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="ontrack" colspan="'+HR.length+'"><div class="onlane">'+
        (function(){const s=Math.min(...idx.map(([r])=>hm(r.t1))),
                     e=Math.max(...idx.map(([r])=>Math.max(hm(r.t2),hm(r.t1)+.5)));
          return '<i class="onsum" style="left:'+pc(s)+'%;width:'+
            Math.max(pc(e)-pc(s),1.2)+'%"></i>'})()+
      '</div></td></tr>';
    idx.sort((a,b)=>hm(a[0].t1)-hm(b[0].t1)).forEach(([r,i])=>{
      const t1=hm(r.t1), t2=Math.max(hm(r.t2),t1+.5), st=TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan;
      const ha=hasActT(r), y1=ha?hm(r.at1):0, y2=ha?Math.max(hm(r.at2),y1+.5):0;
      const sm=slipMin(r);
      h+='<tr class="gtr'+(tlSel.has(r)?" sel":"")+'" data-drow="'+i+'">'+selNo(rowNo++)+'<td class="gtn">'+
        (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-on="'+i+'">'+esc(r.name)+'</span>':esc(r.name))+
        (wr?' <button class="gtdel" data-ox="'+i+'" title="Delete this run-sheet item">×</button>':'')+
        '<br><small class="gtnote">'+
        (wr?'<span contenteditable="plaintext-only" data-onote="'+i+'">'+esc(r.note||"—")+'</span>'
           :esc(r.note||"—"))+'</small></td>'+
      '<td class="gts">'+(wr?'<select class="cell" data-ost="'+i+'">'+
        Object.keys(TLST).map(k=>'<option value="'+k+'"'+((r.st||"plan")===k?" selected":"")+'>'+
          TLST[k].l+'</option>').join("")+'</select>'
        :'<span style="color:'+st.c+';font-weight:700">'+st.l+'</span>')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="gtby">'+(wr
        ?'<button class="tmb" data-oby="'+i+'" title="Choose which teams own this item">'+
          teamChips(r)+'</button>'
        :'<span class="tmb ro">'+teamChips(r)+'</span>')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="gtd gtd2"><span class="dpl">'+mh(t1)+'–'+mh(t2)+'</span>'+
        (ha?'<span class="dac'+(sm>0?" bad":sm<0?" good":"")+'">'+mh(y1)+'–'+mh(y2)+
              (sm?(sm>0?" late ":" early ")+Math.abs(sm)+" ":" On plan")+'</span>'
           :'<span class="dac none">Not recorded</span>')+'</td>'+
      '<td class="ontrack" colspan="'+HR.length+'"'+(wr?' data-ol="'+i+'"':'')+'>'+
        '<div class="onlane">'+
