          '<i class="onplan'+(tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w==="plan"?" pk":"")+
            '" style="left:'+pc(t1)+'%;width:'+Math.max(pc(t2)-pc(t1),1.2)+
            '%" title="'+(tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w==="plan"
              ?"Drag an end to resize, the middle to move it"
              :"Planned "+mh(t1)+" to "+mh(t2)+" — click to select")+'"></i>'+
          (ha?'<i class="onbar'+(pc(y2)-pc(y1)<9?" narrow":"")+
            (tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w==="act"?" pk":"")+
            '" style="left:'+pc(y1)+'%;width:'+Math.max(pc(y2)-pc(y1),1.2)+'%;background:'+st.c+
            '" title="'+(tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w==="act"
              ?"Drag an end to resize, the middle to move it"
              :"Actual "+mh(y1)+" to "+mh(y2)+" — click to select")+'">'+
            '<span>'+mh(y1)+'–'+mh(y2)+'</span></i>':'')+
        '</div></td>'+colCells(r,i,wr)+'</tr>';
    });
  });
  h+='</tbody></table></div>';
  if(wr)h+='<div class="addbar"><button id="ongadd">+ Add category for this day</button></div>';
  $("#page").innerHTML=h; const P=$("#page");
  tlCtx={ wr, redraw:pTimelineOn, list:()=>list,
    toTSV(rows){
      const head=["Category","Run-sheet item","Team","Status","Plan start","Plan finish","Actual start","Actual finish","Note"]
        .concat(tlCols().map(c=>c.l));
      return [tsvLine(head)].concat(rows.map(r=>tsvLine(
        [r.grp,r.name,r.by||"",(TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan).l,r.t1,r.t2,r.at1||"",r.at2||"",r.note||""]
        .concat(tlCols().map(c=>(r.x||{})[c.k]||""))))).join("\n");
    },
    fromTSV(rows){
      if(rows[0]&&/^(หมวด|คิวงาน|งาน|ลำดับ|รายการ)$/.test((rows[0][0]||"").trim()))rows=rows.slice(1);
      const ALL=tlRows(), gs=[...new Set(list.map(r=>r.grp))];
      let lastG=(tlSel.size?[...tlSel][0].grp:null)||gs[0]||"New category", n=0;
      rows.forEach(c=>{
        const one=c.length<2;
        const grp=one?lastG:((c[0]||"").trim()||lastG);
        const name=(one?c[0]:c[1]||"").trim();
        if(!name)return;
        lastG=grp;
        const p1=parseTime(c[4]), p2=parseTime(c[5]), q1=parseTime(c[6]), q2=parseTime(c[7]);
        const r={grp,name,by:(c[2]||"").trim(),st:stKey(c[3]),d:onDay,
          t1:p1||"09:00", t2:p2||mh(hm(p1||"09:00")+1),
          note:(c[8]||"").trim(),ph:"on",a:0,b:0,x:{}};
        if(q1){ r.at1=q1; r.at2=q2||mh(hm(q1)+1) }
        tlCols().forEach((cc,ci)=>{ const v=(c[9+ci]||"").trim(); if(v)r.x[cc.k]=v });
        ALL.push(r); n++;
      });
      if(n)pTimelineOn();
      return n;
    }};
  wireSel(P,list,pTimelineOn);
  wireHint(P,pTimelineOn);
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-od]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    onDay=+b.dataset.od; pTimelineOn()}));
  P.querySelector("#dlon").addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const out=[["day","Date","Category","Run-sheet item","Team","Status","Plan start","Plan finish",
      "Actual start","Actual finish","Behind plan (minutes)","Note"].concat(tlCols().map(c=>c.l))];
    days.forEach((dd,di)=>R.filter(r=>r.d===di)
      .sort((a,b)=>hm(a.t1)-hm(b.t1))
      .forEach(r=>out.push([dd.l,ymd(dd.d),r.grp,r.name,r.by||"",
        (TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan).l,r.t1,r.t2,r.at1||"",r.at2||"",
        slipMin(r)==null?"":slipMin(r),r.note||""]
          .concat(tlCols().map(c=>(r.x||{})[c.k]||"")))));
    saveCSV("timeline-on-"+EV.id+".csv",out,e.currentTarget)});
  wireDateBar(P,pTimelineOn);
  if(!wr)return;

  /* เหมือนหน้าก่อนงาน กดแถบครั้งแรกเพื่อเลือก แล้วกดค้างลากยืดหดหรือเลื่อน
     แถบแผนอยู่บน แถบเวลาจริงอยู่ล่าง ดูจากที่กดว่าจะแก้อันไหน จับทีละครึ่งชั่วโมง */
  let drag=null;
  const hourAt=(td,ev)=>{ const b=td.getBoundingClientRect();
    return Math.round((H0+(ev.clientX-b.left)/b.width*span)*2)/2 };
  P.querySelectorAll("td.ontrack[data-ol]").forEach(td=>{
    td.addEventListener("mousedown",e=>{ e.preventDefault();
      const i=+td.dataset.ol, r=R[i], ha=hasActT(r);
      const y=e.clientY-td.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      const w=(ha&&y>=20)?"act":"plan";
      if(!(tlPick&&tlPick.row===r&&tlPick.w===w)){
        if(w==="act"&&!ha)seedActT(r);
        tlPick={row:r,w:w}; pTimelineOn(); return;
      }
      const a=w==="act"?hm(r.at1):hm(r.t1), b=w==="act"?hm(r.at2):hm(r.t2);
      const x=hourAt(td,e), edge=Math.min(1,(b-a)/4);
      const mode = x<=a+edge ? "a" : x>=b-edge ? "b" : "move";
      drag={row:r,w:w,td:td,mode:mode,a:a,b:b,x0:x,lo:a,hi:b}; paintOn() });
    td.addEventListener("mousemove",e=>{ if(!drag||drag.td!==td)return;
      const x=hourAt(td,e), d=drag;
      if(d.mode==="move"){ const sh=x-d.x0; d.lo=d.a+sh; d.hi=d.b+sh }
      else if(d.mode==="a"){ d.lo=Math.min(x,d.b-.5); d.hi=d.b }
      else { d.lo=d.a; d.hi=Math.max(x,d.a+.5) }
      paintOn() });
  });
  function paintOn(){ if(!drag)return;
    const a=Math.min(drag.lo,drag.hi), b=Math.max(drag.lo,drag.hi);
    const bar=drag.td.querySelector(drag.w==="act"?".onbar":".onplan");
    if(!bar)return;
    bar.style.left=pc(a)+"%"; bar.style.width=Math.max(pc(b)-pc(a),1.2)+"%";
    if(drag.w==="act"){ bar.classList.toggle("narrow",pc(b)-pc(a)<9);
      bar.querySelector("span").textContent=mh(a)+"–"+mh(Math.max(b,a+.5)) } }
  document.addEventListener("mouseup",up);
  function up(){ document.removeEventListener("mouseup",up); if(!drag)return;
    const d=drag; drag=null; const r=d.row;
    const a=Math.min(d.lo,d.hi), b=Math.max(d.lo,d.hi);
    if(a===d.a&&b===d.b){ pTimelineOn(); return }
    if(d.w==="act"){ r.at1=mh(a); r.at2=mh(Math.max(b,a+.5)) }
    else { r.t1=mh(a); r.t2=mh(Math.max(b,a+.5)) }
    pTimelineOn() }

  P.querySelectorAll("[data-on]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    const t=el.textContent.trim(); if(t)R[+el.dataset.on].name=t; pTimelineOn()}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-oby]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>
    teamMenu(b,R[+b.dataset.oby],pTimelineOn)));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-onote]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("blur",()=>{
    R[+el.dataset.onote].note=el.textContent.trim().replace(/^—$/,""); pTimelineOn()}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-ost]").forEach(el=>el.addEventListener("change",()=>{
    const r=R[+el.dataset.ost]; r.st=el.value;
    if(["doing","done","risk"].includes(r.st))seedActT(r);
    pTimelineOn()}));
  ["#onh0","#onh1"].forEach(k=>{const el=P.querySelector(k); if(el)
    el.addEventListener("change",()=>{ EV.onH0=Math.max(0,+P.querySelector("#onh0").value||0);
      EV.onH1=Math.min(24,+P.querySelector("#onh1").value||24); pTimelineOn()})});
  wireCols(P,R,pTimelineOn);
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-ox]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    if(b.dataset.armed){const k=ALL.indexOf(R[+b.dataset.ox]);
      if(k>=0)ALL.splice(k,1); pTimelineOn(); return}
    b.dataset.armed="1"; b.textContent="Delete?"; b.style.color=cv("--risk");
    setTimeout(()=>{if(b.dataset.armed){delete b.dataset.armed;b.textContent="×";b.style.color=""}},4000)}));
  P.querySelectorAll("[data-oa]").forEach(b=>b.addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const g=b.dataset.oa, last=R.filter(r=>r.d===onDay&&r.grp===g).pop();
    ALL.push({grp:g,name:"New item",by:last?last.by:"Event",d:onDay,
      t1:last?last.t2:"09:00",t2:last?mh(hm(last.t2)+1):"10:00",
      st:"",note:"",ph:"on",a:0,b:0}); pTimelineOn()}));
  P.querySelector("#ongadd").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    ALL.push({grp:"New category",name:"New item",by:"Event",d:onDay,t1:"09:00",t2:"10:00",
      st:"",note:"",ph:"on",a:0,b:0}); pTimelineOn()});
  P.querySelector("#onfill2").addEventListener("click",()=>{ tlFill("on"); pTimelineOn()});
  P.querySelector("#oncopy").addEventListener("click",()=>{
    const src=R.filter(r=>r.d===onDay);
    if(!src.length){alert("Nothing to copy from today");return}
    const to=prompt("Copy the run sheet for "+day.l+" ("+src.length+" items) to which day\n"+
      days.map((d,i)=>(i+1)+". "+d.l).join("\n")+"\n\nType the day number");
    const i=(+to)-1;
    if(!(i>=0&&i<days.length)){ if(to!=null)alert("That day number is not valid"); return }
    if(i===onDay){alert("is the same day");return}
    const key=r=>r.grp+"|"+r.name+"|"+r.t1;
    const have=new Set(R.filter(r=>r.d===i).map(key));
    const add=src.filter(r=>!have.has(key(r)));
    add.forEach(r=>ALL.push(Object.assign({},r,{d:i,st:"",note:r.note})));
    alert("Copied to "+days[i].l+" "+add.length+" Item"+
      (add.length<src.length?" (skipped duplicates "+(src.length-add.length)+")":""));
    onDay=i; pTimelineOn()});
}
function ymdTH(d){ return d.getDate()+" "+THM[d.getMonth()] }
function paintDrag(P){
  if(!tlDrag)return; const a=Math.min(tlDrag.lo,tlDrag.hi), b=Math.max(tlDrag.lo,tlDrag.hi);
  P.querySelectorAll('td.gtc[data-r="'+tlDrag.r+'"]').forEach(td=>{
    const k=+td.dataset.k; td.classList.toggle("gsel",k>=a&&k<=b)});
}
function wireTlCsv(P,ph){
  P.querySelector("#dltl").addEventListener("click",e=>{
    const out=[["Category","Task","Team","Status","Plan start","Plan finish","Actual start","Actual finish",
      "Behind plan (weeks)","Note"].concat(tlCols().map(c=>c.l))];
    tlRows().filter(r=>(r.ph||"pre")===ph).forEach(r=>{
      const s=slip(r);
      out.push([r.grp,r.name,r.by||"",(TLST[r.st]||TLST.plan).l,
        tlDate(pA(r)),tlDate(pB(r)),
        hasAct(r)?tlDate(aA(r)):"",hasAct(r)?tlDate(aB(r)):"",
        s==null?"":s,r.note||""].concat(tlCols().map(c=>(r.x||{})[c.k]||"")))});
    saveCSV("timeline-"+ph+"-"+EV.id+".csv",out,e.currentTarget)});
}

matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme:dark)").addEventListener("change",()=>{if($("#app").classList.contains("on"))draw()});

</script>
